# 4.2.4 Model

- [Controllable Video Generation With Text-Based Instructions](https://doi.org/10.1109/tmm.2023.3262972): Uses text instructions to control video generation and editing, fitting the model-side controllability line rather than an external agent harness.
- [EMAGE: Towards Unified Holistic Co-Speech Gesture Generation via Masked Audio Gesture Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.00374): Trains a masked audio-gesture transformer on BEAT2, combines audio rhythm and content features with four VQ-VAEs, and accepts masked gesture hints to synthesize synchronized face, body, hands, and global co-speech motion.
- [From Covert Hiding To Visual Editing: Robust Generative Video Steganography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.00652): Hides secret messages inside semantic features during generative face-video editing, using an end-to-end RoGVS network for robustness to social-network distortions and higher payload capacity.
- [VideoStudio: Generating Consistent-Content and Multi-scene Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.01256): Uses an LLM to expand a prompt into a multi-scene script, tracks shared entities across scenes, and applies diffusion generation with entity consistency for long multi-scene videos.
- [Moonshot: Towards Controllable Video Generation and Editing with Multimodal Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.01827): Adds multimodal video blocks with decoupled image and text cross-attention, plus optional image ControlNet integration, so text, image, and geometry conditions can drive generation and editing without extra ControlNet training.
- [VASE: Object-Centric Appearance and Shape Manipulation of Real Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.02473): Edits real videos at the object level by adapting an image-conditioned diffusion model with temporal layers and shape-control training, enabling appearance changes and explicit structural edits.
- [Latte: Latent Diffusion Transformer for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.03048): Uses a latent diffusion transformer for video generation, contributing a model-side generation backbone.
- [MagicVideo-V2: Multi-Stage High-Aesthetic Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.04468): Combines a text-to-image model, motion generator, reference-image embedding, and frame interpolation into a multi-stage pipeline for high-resolution aesthetic text-to-video generation.
- [Morphable Diffusion: 3D-Consistent Diffusion for Single-image Avatar Creation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.04728): Injects an articulated 3D morphable human model into multi-view diffusion so a single image can yield 3D-consistent, animatable avatars with controllable pose and expression.
- [Object-Centric Diffusion for Efficient Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.05735): Speeds text-guided video editing by object-centric sampling and token merging, assigning more denoising compute to edited foreground regions while reducing background work.
- [RAVEN: Rethinking Adversarial Video Generation with Efficient Tri-plane Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.06035): Uses a hybrid explicit-implicit tri-plane GAN with a single clip latent and optical-flow module to generate temporally coherent 256 px videos over 5 seconds with lower FLOPs.
- [Video Super-Resolution Transformer with Masked Inter&Intra-Frame Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.06312): Reduces video super-resolution cost with masked intra-frame and inter-frame attention plus adaptive block masks that reuse previously enhanced features without sacrificing PSNR.
- [360DVD: Controllable Panorama Video Generation with 360-Degree Video Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.06578): Adapts pretrained text-to-video diffusion to panorama clips with a lightweight 360-Adapter, 360 enhancement techniques, motion conditions, and the WEB360 panoramic video dataset.
- [VideoCrafter2: Overcoming Data Limitations for High-Quality Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.09047): Improves video diffusion quality under data limits through the VideoCrafter2 training recipe.
- [Vlogger: Make Your Dream A Vlog](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.09414): Decomposes minute-level vlog creation into LLM-directed script, actor, ShowMaker, and voicer stages, with ShowMaker trained for both text-to-video generation and prediction.
- [CustomVideo: Customizing Text-to-Video Generation with Multiple Subjects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.09962): Handles multiple subject references by composing subjects in a shared image, disentangling them with latent attention control, and using object masks during attention learning.
- [WorldDreamer: Towards General World Models for Video Generation via Predicting Masked Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.09985): Learns a general world model by discretizing visual inputs and predicting masked tokens under multimodal prompts, supporting text-to-video, image-to-video, and video editing across natural and driving scenes.
- [Motion-Zero: Zero-Shot Moving Object Control Framework for Diffusion-Based Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.10150): Controls object trajectories without training by adding a position-aware initial noise prior, U-Net attention-map spatial constraints, and shifted temporal attention during denoising.
- [Inflation with Diffusion: Efficient Temporal Adaptation for Text-to-Video Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.10404): Inflates a text-to-image super-resolution diffusion model into video, adds a temporal adapter, and studies tuning trade-offs for temporally coherent text-to-video super-resolution.
- [MotionMix: Weakly-Supervised Diffusion for Controllable Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.11115): Splits diffusion denoising into noisy-condition rough generation followed by unannotated-motion refinement, using weak supervision for text-to-motion, action-to-motion, and music-to-dance.
- [Lumiere: A Space-Time Diffusion Model for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.12945): Introduces a space-time diffusion model for video generation.
- [BootsTAP: Bootstrapped Training for Tracking-Any-Point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.00847): Bootstraps a tracking-any-point model with unlabeled real videos in a student-teacher setup, improving TAP-Vid DAVIS and Kinetics results over simulation-only training.
- [Boximator: Generating Rich and Controllable Motions for Video Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.01566): Adds hard and soft box constraints to existing video diffusion models, training only a control module with self-tracking so users can specify object positions, shapes, and motion paths.
- [InteractiveVideo: User-Centric Controllable Video Generation with Synergistic Multimodal Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.03040): Fuses text, image, painting, and drag-and-drop instructions through a synergistic multimodal instruction mechanism for iterative fine-grained video control.
- [Direct-a-Video: Customized Video Generation with User-Directed Camera Movement and Object Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.03162): Improves controllable video generation with explicit camera, object-motion, or 3D-consistency control.
- [ConsistI2V: Enhancing Visual Consistency for Image-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.04324): Improves image-to-video consistency with first-frame spatiotemporal attention and low-frequency first-frame noise initialization, and extends the idea to long autoregressive generation and camera control.
- [Keyframer: Empowering Animation Design using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.06071): Turns natural-language animation prompts into editable animation code with inline previews, and studies decomposed prompting plus direct code edits in a 13-person design study.
- [HeadStudio: Text to Animatable Head Avatars with 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.06149): Binds 3D Gaussians to an animatable head prior and jointly optimizes initialization, distillation, and regularization so text prompts produce realistic drivable head avatars.
- [Magic-Me: Identity-Specific Video Customized Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.09368): Preserves a specified identity in customized videos with 3D Gaussian noise initialization, an extended textual-inversion identity module, and identity-disentangled video diffusion training.
- [Poisoned Forgery Face: Towards Backdoor Attacks on Face Forgery Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.11473): Studies backdoor attacks against face-forgery detection, exposing vulnerabilities in generated-media detectors.
- [CLIPping the Deception: Adapting Vision-Language Models for Universal Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.12927): Adapts CLIP for universal deepfake detection with prompt tuning while retaining the text branch, training on ProGAN and evaluating cross-generator generalization.
- [Video ReCap: Recursive Captioning of Hour-Long Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.13250): Builds a recursive video-language model with curriculum learning and Ego4D-HCap to caption clips, segments, and hour-long videos across multiple temporal hierarchy levels.
- [Bring Your Own Character: A Holistic Solution for Automatic Facial Animation Generation of Customized Characters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.13724): Retargets facial expressions from input face images to customized virtual characters by estimating blendshape coefficients, then packages the animator in a Unity-compatible toolkit.
- [Customize-A-Video: One-Shot Motion Customization of Text-to-Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.14780): Learns one-shot motion customization by applying LoRA to temporal attention layers and using appearance absorbers to separate reference appearance from reusable motion.
- [Snap Video: Scaled Spatiotemporal Transformers for Text-to-Video Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.14797): Scales a video-first EDM with a transformer backbone that trains and samples faster than U-Nets, targeting billion-parameter text-to-video synthesis with better motion fidelity.
- [EMO: Emote Portrait Alive - Generating Expressive Portrait Videos with Audio2Video Diffusion Model under Weak Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.17485): Generates expressive speaking or singing portraits directly from audio with an audio-to-video diffusion pipeline that avoids 3D landmarks while preserving identity and smooth frame transitions.
- [Seeing and Hearing: Open-domain Visual-Audio Generation with Diffusion Latent Aligners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.17723): Generates aligned visual-audio content with diffusion latent aligners.
- [AtomoVideo: High Fidelity Image-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.01800): Uses multi-granularity image injection, adapter training, curated data, and iterative prediction to improve image fidelity, motion intensity, and long-sequence image-to-video generation.
- [UniCtrl: Improving the Spatiotemporal Consistency of Text-to-Video Diffusion Models via Training-Free Unified Attention Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.02332): Applies training-free unified attention control with cross-frame self-attention, motion injection, and spatiotemporal synchronization to improve consistency and motion diversity in text-to-video models.
- [Tuning-Free Noise Rectification for High Fidelity Image-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.02827): Injects noise into the input-image latent and rectifies denoising in a plug-and-play image-to-video pipeline to preserve details and reduce prediction bias.
- [MMoFusion: Multi-modal Co-Speech Motion Generation with Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.02905): Uses a diffusion framework with progressive multimodal fusion, emotion- and identity-conditioned style matrices, and shared and style-specific encoders for diverse co-speech body motion.
- [CAT: Enhancing Multimodal Large Language Model to Answer Questions in Dynamic Audio-Visual Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.04640): Enhances audio-visual question answering with a clue aggregator, AVinstruct mixed instruction data, and ambiguity-aware preference optimization for dynamic audio-video scenes.
- [SplattingAvatar: Realistic Real-Time Human Avatars With Mesh-Embedded Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.05087): Embeds 3D Gaussians on a triangle mesh so explicit mesh motion drives low-frequency deformation while Gaussian splats render high-frequency appearance in real time.
- [VideoElevator: Elevating Video Generation Quality with Versatile Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.05438): Improves text-to-video sampling without training by alternating temporal motion refinement from a text-to-video model with spatial quality elevation from an inflated text-to-image diffusion model.
- [Audio-Synchronized Visual Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.05659): Defines audio-synchronized visual animation, curates AVSync15, and trains AVSyncD diffusion to animate images or videos according to temporally aligned audio cues.
- [Harmonious Group Choreography with Trajectory-Controllable Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.06189): Uses trajectory-controllable diffusion with a dance-trajectory navigator, distance-consistency loss, footwork adaptor, and forward-kinematic loss to generate collision-free group choreography.
- [FastVideoEdit: Leveraging Consistency Models for Efficient Text-to-Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.06269): Uses consistency-model self-consistency for zero-shot text-guided video editing, avoiding DDIM inversion, extra conditions, and per-video fine-tuning while preserving source content.
- [FlowVQTalker: High-Quality Emotional Talking Face Generation through Normalizing Flow and Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.06375): Combines a flow-based coefficient generator with vector-quantized texture modeling so audio can map one-to-many into emotional expressions, blinks, poses, and detailed talking-face textures.
- [V3D: Video Diffusion Models are Effective 3D Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.06738): Fine-tunes a video diffusion model for 360-degree orbital multi-view generation, adds geometric consistency priors, and reconstructs meshes or 3D Gaussians from one image.
- [DragAnything: Motion Control for Anything using Entity Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.07420): Represents any clicked entity with an open-domain embedding and lets users draw trajectories to control one or multiple object motions, including background regions.
- [Motion Mamba: Efficient and Long Sequence Motion Generation with Hierarchical and Bidirectional Selective SSM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.07487): Builds motion generation around hierarchical temporal Mamba blocks and bidirectional spatial Mamba modules inside a symmetric U-Net for efficient long-sequence human motion.
- [SSM Meets Video Diffusion Models: Efficient Video Generation with Structured State Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.07711): Replaces quadratic temporal attention in video diffusion with bidirectional structured state-space layers, reducing memory on long sequences while matching or improving FVD.
- [Text-to-Audio Generation Synchronized with Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.07938): Pairs T2AV-Bench metrics with a video-aligned text-to-audio diffusion model that fuses temporal visual features through Audio-Visual ControlNet and contrastive alignment.
- [AesopAgent: Agent-driven Evolutionary System on Story-to-Video Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.07952): Orchestrates story-to-video production with RAG-based workflow evolution, prompt and utility optimization, and utilities for coherent images, audio, effects, and animation.
- [Follow-Your-Click: Open-domain Regional Image Animation via Short Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.08268): Animates a user-clicked image region from a short motion prompt using first-frame masking, a motion-augmented short-prompt module, and flow-based speed control.
- [Video Editing via Factorized Diffusion Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.09334): Trains Emu Video Edit without paired video-edit data by distilling an image-editing adapter for per-frame edits and a video adapter for temporal consistency.
- [AICL: Action In-Context Learning for Video Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.11535): Uses reference videos as in-context action examples so a video diffusion model can understand uncommon actions and transfer them across three base generators.
- [EffiVED: Efficient Video Editing via Text-instruction Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.11568): Builds instruction-guided video editing pairs from image-editing datasets and open-world videos, then trains an efficient diffusion editor that avoids per-video tuning.
- [ReGenNet: Towards Human Action-Reaction Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.11882): Frames action-reaction synthesis as online reactor motion prediction, annotates actor-reactor order in NTU120, InterHuman, and Chi3D, and trains a Transformer diffusion model with distance-based interaction loss.
- [SV3D: Novel Multi-view Synthesis and 3D Generation from a Single Image using Latent Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.12008): Adapts image-to-video latent diffusion for explicit camera-controlled orbital multi-view synthesis and uses its outputs in improved 3D optimization for reconstruction.
- [VideoMV: Consistent Multi-View Generation Based on Large Video Generative Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.12010): Fine-tunes a video generator for dense multi-view images and uses 3D-aware denoising sampling with a feed-forward reconstruction module to improve consistency.
- [VFusion3D: Learning Scalable 3D Generative Models from Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.12034): Unlocks a video diffusion model's multi-view prior, synthesizes nearly 3 million multi-view examples, and trains a feed-forward image-to-3D generator that runs in seconds.
- [CoCoCo: Improving Text-Guided Video Inpainting for Better Consistency, Controllability and Compatibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.12035): Improves text-guided video inpainting with a motion-capture module, instance-aware region selection, and a compatibility path for injecting personalized models.
- [GaussianFlow: Splatting Gaussian Dynamics for 4D Content Creation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.12365): Derives differentiable Gaussian flow by splatting 3D Gaussian dynamics into image space, enabling optical-flow supervision for 4D generation and novel-view synthesis.
- [Mora: Enabling Generalist Video Generation via A Multi-Agent Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.13248): Combines open-source video modules with multi-agent fine-tuning, data-free synthetic training data, and human-in-the-loop filtering to approximate Sora-style text-to-video, image-to-video, and editing functions.
- [Be-Your-Outpainter: Mastering Video Outpainting through Input-Specific Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.13745): Uses input-specific pseudo-outpainting adaptation followed by pattern-aware diffusion outpainting so generated regions inherit the source video's scene patterns.
- [TimeRewind: Rewinding Time with Image-and-Events Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.13800): Conditions image-to-video diffusion on event-camera motion through an event motion adaptor to synthesize plausible pre-capture frames from a single image.
- [CoMo: Controllable Motion Generation through Language Guided Pose Code Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.13900): Discretizes human motion into interpretable body-part pose codes, generates them autoregressively from text, and lets an LLM edit motions by modifying those codes.
- [Efficient Video Diffusion Models via Content-Frame Motion-Latent Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.14148): Represents each video as a content frame plus low-dimensional motion latent, using a fine-tuned image diffusion model for content and a lightweight motion-latent diffusion model for efficient generation.
- [CAGE: Unsupervised Visual Composition and Animation for Controllable Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.14368): Trains on unannotated videos with randomly selected self-supervised local-feature controls, enabling object composition and animation at specified spatiotemporal locations.
- [AnyV2V: A Tuning-Free Framework For Any Video-to-Video Editing Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.14468): Performs tuning-free video editing by editing the first frame with any image editor and propagating the result through image-to-video generation with temporal feature injection.
- [Videoshop: Localized Semantic Video Editing with Noise-Extrapolated Diffusion Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.14617): Lets users edit the first frame with external image tools, then propagates edits through noise-extrapolated latent inversion with semantic, spatial, and temporal consistency.
- [X-Portrait: Expressive Portrait Animation with Hierarchical Motion Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.15931): Animates a single portrait from driving RGB video with a conditional diffusion renderer, ControlNet-style pose and expression controls, and patch-based local control.
- [Edit3K: Universal Representation Learning for Video Editing Components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.16048): Builds a 3,094-component, 618,800-video editing dataset and learns raw-material-invariant representations for editing-component retrieval, recognition, and recommendation.
- [Make-Your-Anchor: A Diffusion-based 2D Avatar Generation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.16510): Fine-tunes a structure-guided diffusion model on one minute of a person, renders 3D mesh conditions, and extends frame-wise diffusion to long anchor-style videos with overlapping temporal denoising.
- [TRIP: Temporal Residual Learning with Image Noise Prior for Image-to-Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.17005): Uses an image-derived noise prior and temporal residual learning to preserve the static image while modeling coherent inter-frame dynamics in image-to-video diffusion.
- [AniPortrait: Audio-Driven Synthesis of Photorealistic Portrait Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.17694): Extracts audio-driven 3D intermediate representations, projects them into 2D landmarks, then uses diffusion plus a motion module for photorealistic talking portrait animation.
- [TC4D: Trajectory-Conditioned Text-to-4D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.17920): Factors text-to-4D motion into spline-parameterized global bounding-box trajectories and local deformations, supervised by pretrained text-to-video priors.
- [ConvoFusion: Multi-Modal Conversational Diffusion for Co-Speech Gesture Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.17936): Uses multimodal conversational diffusion for co-speech gesture synthesis.
- [Beyond Talking – Generating Holistic 3D Human Dyadic Motion for Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.19467): Trains separate VQ-VAEs for speaker and listener holistic motion and a chain-like autoregressive transformer to generate coordinated dyadic communication movements.
- [Motion Inversion for Video Customization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.20193): Learns temporally coherent motion embeddings from a reference video, injects query-key and value embeddings into temporal transformers, and reuses them for video customization.
- [ST-LLM: Large Language Models Are Effective Temporal Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.00308): Feeds spatial-temporal video tokens directly into an LLM, then uses dynamic masking and tailored training to improve video dialogue and understanding.
- [Towards Variable and Coordinated Holistic Co-Speech Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.00368): Generates variable and coordinated holistic body motion aligned with speech.
- [Video Interpolation with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.01203): Uses cascaded diffusion to jointly generate interpolation frames from start and end frames, then applies guided super-resolution conditioned on original high-resolution endpoints.
- [Direct Preference Optimization of Video Large Multimodal Models from Language Model Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.01258): Uses detailed video captions as language-only evidence for reward scoring and applies direct preference optimization to reduce hallucinations in video question answering.
- [Large Motion Model for Unified Multi-Modal Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.01284): Unifies text-to-motion, music-to-dance, and other motion tasks through MotionVerse data harmonization and a motion-centric multimodal generalist model.
- [MotionChain: Conversational Motion Controllers via Multimodal Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.01700): Tokenizes text, image, and motion prompts and feeds them to a vision-motion-aware language model for multi-turn conversational control of long human motion sequences.
- [Co-Speech Gesture Video Generation via Motion-Decoupled Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.01862): Extracts TPS-based latent motion features that preserve appearance, then uses transformer diffusion to generate temporally aligned audio-driven co-speech gesture videos.
- [MiniGPT4-Video: Advancing Multimodal LLMs for Video Understanding with Interleaved Visual-Textual Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.03413): Advances video multimodal LLMs with interleaved visual-textual tokens.
- [SC4D: Sparse-Controlled Video-to-4D Generation and Motion Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.03736): Decouples sparse motion control from appearance in video-to-4D generation, using Adaptive Gaussian initialization and Gaussian Alignment loss to preserve shape and motion fidelity.
- [AnimateZoo: Zero-shot Video Generation of Cross-Species Animation via Subject Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.04946): Aligns cross-species subjects for zero-shot animal animation with a Laplacian detail booster, prompt-tuned identity extractor, and shape alignment.
- [MagicTime: Time-Lapse Video Generation Models as Metamorphic Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.05014): Transforms pretrained text-to-video models into metamorphic time-lapse generators with MagicAdapter, dynamic frame extraction, and time-lapse videos that encode physical changes.
- [Investigating the Effectiveness of Cross-Attention to Unlock Zero-Shot Editing of Text-to-Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.05519): Studies text-to-video cross-attention for zero-shot editing and shows it can control object shape, position, and movement without one-shot tuning.
- [LoopAnimate: Loopable Salient Object Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.09172): Generates seamless looping object animations by decoupling multi-level image appearance and text semantics and injecting pixel- and feature-level conditions into diffusion.
- [AniClipart: Clipart Animation with Text-to-Video Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.12347): Animates clipart with text-to-video priors.
- [TalkingGaussian: Structure-Persistent 3D Talking Head Synthesis via Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.15264): Represents facial motion as smooth deformations of persistent 3D Gaussian primitives, adding face-mouth deformation and Gaussian initialization for structure-preserving talking heads.
- [ID-Animator: Zero-Shot Identity-Preserving Human Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.15275): Adds a face adapter with learnable facial latent queries and an identity-oriented dataset pipeline so a single reference face can drive zero-shot personalized human video generation.
- [MotionMaster: Training-free Camera Motion Transfer For Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.15789): Disentangles camera and object motion in source videos and transfers camera trajectories to new generations without training temporal camera modules.
- [Beyond Deepfake Images: Detecting AI-Generated Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.15955): Shows image-synthetic detectors fail on generated videos, then learns video-specific traces for synthetic-video detection and generator attribution under H.264 recompression and few-shot transfer.
- [Semantically consistent Video-to-Audio Generation using Multimodal Language Large Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.16305): Uses a multimodal language model to infer key-frame video semantics and generate natural-language audio schemes that prompt text-to-audio models for sound effects and background music.
- [TI2V-Zero: Zero-Shot Image Conditioning for Text-to-Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.16306): Adds image conditioning to pretrained text-to-video diffusion without training or extra modules through a repeat-and-slide denoising strategy.
- [Tunnel Try-on: Excavating Spatial-temporal Tunnels for High-quality Virtual Try-on in Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.17571): Builds focus tunnels around clothing regions, smooths crops with a Kalman filter, injects tunnel position embeddings, and uses an environment encoder for high-detail video try-on.
- [Ock: Unsupervised Dynamic Video Prediction With Object-Centric Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.18423): Predicts dynamic videos with object slots that separate time-static appearance from object-centric kinematics such as position, velocity, and acceleration.
- [FlexiFilm: Long Video Generation with Flexible Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.18620): Adds a temporal conditioner for multi-modal controls and a resampling scheme that reduces overexposure, enabling consistent 30-second-plus long-video diffusion.
- [MeGA: Hybrid Mesh-Gaussian Head Avatar for High-Fidelity Rendering and Head Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.19026): Combines an enhanced FLAME mesh, UV displacement, deferred neural rendering, disentangled neural textures, and Gaussians to model head components for high-fidelity avatars.
- [EMOPortraits: Emotion-Enhanced Multimodal One-Shot Head Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.19110): Revises MegaPortraits with architecture and training changes that better encode intense asymmetric expressions and improve emotion transfer in one-shot head avatars.
- [MotionLCM: Real-time Controllable Motion Generation via Latent Consistency Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.19759): Distills motion latent diffusion into one-step or few-step latent consistency generation and adds latent-space ControlNet supervision for controllable real-time motion.
- [StoryDiffusion: Consistent Self-Attention for Long-Range Image and Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.01434): Adds consistent self-attention to pretrained image diffusion and trains a semantic motion predictor to convert consistent image sequences into long-range videos.
- [Efficient Text-Driven Motion Generation via Latent Consistency Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.02791): Uses motion latent consistency training, a quantized motion autoencoder, and trajectory precomputation to enable few-step or single-step text-driven human motion generation.
- [Matten: Video Generation with Mamba-Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.03025): Mixes spatial-temporal attention for local video content with bidirectional Mamba for global content modeling inside a latent diffusion video generator.
- [AniTalker: Animate Vivid and Diverse Talking Faces through Identity-Decoupled Facial Motion Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.03121): Learns an identity-decoupled universal facial motion representation with self-supervised frame reconstruction and metric-learning identity encoding for talking-face animation.
- [Vidu: a Highly Consistent, Dynamic and Skilled Text-to-Video Generator with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.04233): Uses a U-ViT diffusion backbone to generate coherent 1080p videos up to 16 seconds and tests canny-to-video, prediction, and subject-driven controls.
- [Edit-Your-Motion: Space-Time Diffusion Decoupling Learning for Video Motion Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.04496): Combines DDIM inversion, a lightweight motion attention adapter, recurrent causal attention, and two-stage spatiotemporal learning for one-shot human video motion editing.
- [TALC: Time-Aligned Captions for Multi-Scene Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.04682): Aligns scene captions with early and later visual features in pretrained T2V models, then finetunes on multi-scene video-text pairs for consistent multi-scene synthesis.
- [OneTo3D: One Image to Re-editable Dynamic 3D Model and Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.06547): Converts one image into an editable Gaussian-splatting dynamic 3D representation and uses text instructions to control semantic motion and long 3D video generation.
- [LogoMotion: Visually Grounded Code Generation for Content-Aware Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.07065): Generates logo animation code through visually grounded code synthesis, program repair, and code-connected editing widgets for motion, grouping, and timing.
- [ViViD: Video Virtual Try-on using Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.11794): Uses a garment encoder for fine clothing semantics, attention feature fusion, and a lightweight pose encoder to maintain garment detail and spatiotemporal consistency in video try-on.
- [DisenStudio: Customized Multi-Subject Text-to-Video Generation with Disentangled Spatial Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.12796): Adds spatial-disentangled cross-attention to pretrained text-to-video diffusion so multiple reference subjects keep identity while actions bind to the correct subject.
- [SIGGesture: Generalized Co-Speech Gesture Synthesis via Semantic Injection with Large-Scale Pre-Training Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.13336): Pretrains a diffusion gesture foundation model on pseudo-labeled large-scale data, uses an LLM to propose semantic gestures, and injects those semantics during reverse diffusion.
- [MotionCraft: Physics-based Zero-Shot Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.13557): Warps the noise latent of an image diffusion model with optical flow from physics simulation, giving zero-shot videos prescribed physical motion while preserving generative completion.
- [Video Diffusion Models are Training-free Motion Interpreter and Controller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.14864): Uses PCA to locate motion-aware video-diffusion features, removes content-correlated channels into MOFT, and applies them for training-free, architecture-agnostic motion control.
- [PuzzleAvatar: Assembling 3D Avatars from Personal Albums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.14869): Reconstructs personal 3D avatars from sparse photo albums by assembling facial, body, and clothing components into a drivable avatar representation.
- [Scaling Diffusion Mamba with Bidirectional SSMs for Efficient Image and Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.15881): Replaces attention-heavy diffusion blocks with bidirectional state-space Mamba layers to scale image and video generation more efficiently.
- [Disentangling Foreground and Background Motion for Enhanced Realism in Human Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.16393): Separately models foreground pose motion and background sparse tracking-point motion, then extends clips with global features to reduce long-video drift.
- [I2VEdit: First-Frame-Guided Video Editing via Image-to-Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.16537): Uses the first frame as a structural and appearance anchor so image-to-video diffusion can propagate edits while preserving temporal consistency.
- [Diffusion4D: Fast Spatial-temporal Consistent 4D Generation via Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.16645): Leverages video diffusion priors to synthesize spatially and temporally consistent 4D content without slow per-frame optimization.
- [360-degree Human Video Generation with 4D Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.17405): Uses a 4D diffusion transformer to generate full-circle human videos with consistent appearance across viewpoint and time.
- [RefDrop: Controllable Consistency in Image or Video Generation via Reference Feature Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.17661): Drops and reuses reference features during denoising to trade off identity or style consistency against controllable variation in image and video generation.
- [ToonCrafter: Generative Cartoon Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.17933): Adapts pretrained image-to-video diffusion to sparse cartoon sketches, using dual-reference conditioning and sketch interpolation so line-art keyframes become temporally coherent cartoon inbetweens.
- [VividPose: Advancing Stable Video Diffusion for Realistic Human Image Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.18156): Adapts Stable Video Diffusion for human image animation with pose conditioning that improves motion realism and identity preservation.
- [VITON-DiT: Learning In-the-Wild Video Try-On from Human Dance Videos via Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.18326): Trains a diffusion transformer for video virtual try-on from in-the-wild dance videos, transferring garments across moving people while retaining temporal coherence.
- [T2V-Turbo: Breaking the Quality Bottleneck of Video Consistency Model with Mixed Reward Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.18750): Distills text-to-video diffusion into a fast consistency model and uses mixed human and automatic reward feedback to recover generation quality.
- [EasyAnimate: High-Performance Video Generation Framework with Hybrid Windows Attention and Reward Backpropagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.18991): Builds a high-resolution video generation framework around hybrid-window attention and reward backpropagation for efficient quality tuning.
- [MotionDreamer: Zero-Shot 3D Mesh Animation from Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.20155): Extracts motion priors from video diffusion models to animate 3D meshes from text or reference videos without task-specific animation training.
- [MOFA-Video: Controllable Image Animation via Generative Motion Field Adaptions in Frozen Image-to-Video Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.20222): Adds motion-field adapters to a frozen image-to-video diffusion model so sparse trajectories can control image animation without full model finetuning.
- [CV-VAE: A Compatible Video VAE for Latent Generative Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.20279): Designs a video VAE tokenizer compatible with latent video diffusion models, improving reconstruction quality and temporal compression for generation backbones.
- [4Diffusion: Multi-view Video Diffusion Model for 4D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.20674): Builds a 4D pipeline around multi-view video diffusion, combining spatial-temporal multi-view modeling with multiple diffusion priors to reduce flicker in monocular-video-to-4D generation.
- [Stratified Avatar Generation from Sparse Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.20786): Builds avatars from sparse captures by stratifying geometry and appearance, enabling controllable human rendering with limited observations.
- [Frieren: Efficient Video-to-Audio Generation Network with Rectified Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.00320): Uses rectified flow matching over spectrogram latents and a non-autoregressive vector-field estimator, improving video-to-audio quality and visual-audio synchrony with ODE sampling.
- [ZeroSmooth: Training-free Diffuser Adaptation for High Frame Rate Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.00908): Adapts pretrained diffusers at inference time to synthesize higher-frame-rate videos without additional training.
- [UniAnimate: taming unified video diffusion models for consistent human image animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.01188): Tames a unified video diffusion model for human image animation with controls that improve identity, pose, and temporal consistency.
- [MoLA: Motion Generation and Editing with Latent Diffusion Enhanced by Adversarial Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.01867): Enhances latent diffusion motion generation and editing with adversarial training to produce sharper and more plausible human motions.
- [Follow-Your-Emoji: Fine-Controllable and Expressive Freestyle Portrait Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.01900): Drives portrait animation from emoji-style expression controls, enabling fine-grained and expressive facial motion beyond speech-only conditioning.
- [I4VGen: Image as Free Stepping Stone for Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.02230): Uses intermediate image generation as a bridge for text-to-video synthesis, anchoring appearance before producing temporally coherent motion.
- [CamCo: Camera-Controllable 3D-Consistent Image-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.02509): Improves controllable video generation with explicit camera, object-motion, or 3D-consistency control.
- [V-Express: Conditional Dropout for Progressive Training of Portrait Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.02511): Uses conditional dropout and progressive training to make portrait video generation robust across audio, pose, and reference-image conditions.
- [ViDiT-Q: Efficient and Accurate Quantization of Diffusion Transformers for Image and Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.02540): Quantizes diffusion transformers for image and video generation while preserving denoising accuracy and reducing inference cost.
- [Enhancing Temporal Consistency in Video Editing by Reconstructing Videos with 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.02541): Reconstructs edited videos as 3D Gaussian splats so edits remain geometrically and temporally consistent across frames.
- [Towards Multiple Character Image Animation Through Enhancing Implicit Decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.03035): Improves multi-character image animation by decoupling characters implicitly, reducing motion and identity leakage between subjects.
- [Searching Priors Makes Text-to-Video Synthesis Better](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.03215): Searches and injects useful priors during text-to-video synthesis to improve prompt fidelity, motion, and visual quality.
- [VideoTetris: Towards Compositional Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.04277): Decomposes prompts into compositional spatial and temporal layouts so text-to-video diffusion can generate multiple interacting concepts more reliably.
- [SF-V: Single Forward Video Generation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.04324): Generates videos in a single forward pass, targeting low-latency video synthesis without iterative denoising.
- [ShareGPT4Video: Improving Video Understanding and Generation with Better Captions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.04325): Builds dense high-quality video captions that serve as synthetic supervision for both video understanding and text-to-video generation.
- [Physics3D: Learning Physical Properties of 3D Gaussians via Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.04338): Infers physical properties for 3D Gaussians from video diffusion priors so generated 3D scenes can animate with more plausible dynamics.
- [Lifelong Learning of Video Diffusion Models From a Single Video Stream](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.04814): Updates a video diffusion model from a single incoming video stream, studying continual adaptation without a large retraining corpus.
- [Ada-VE: Training-Free Consistent Video Editing Using Adaptive Motion Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.04873): Uses an adaptive motion prior at inference time to improve temporal consistency in text-guided video editing without tuning the model.
- [Training-Free Video Editing via Optical Flow-Enhanced Score Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.04888): Combines optical-flow constraints with score distillation to edit videos consistently without paired edit data or model finetuning.
- [CoNo: Consistency Noise Injection for Tuning-free Long Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.05082): Injects consistency-preserving noise across denoising windows to extend diffusion generation to longer videos without retraining.
- [MotionClone: Training-Free Motion Cloning for Controllable Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.05338): Clones motion from reference videos through training-free attention and trajectory guidance for controllable video generation.
- [Ctrl-V: Higher Fidelity Video Generation with Bounding-Box Controlled Object Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.05630): Conditions video generation on object bounding boxes so users can specify object locations and motion while preserving visual fidelity.
- [Vript: A Video Is Worth Thousands of Words](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.06040): Provides detailed video descriptions that improve text-video alignment data for training and evaluating generative video models.
- [FRAG: Frequency Adapting Group for Diffusion Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.06044): Uses frequency-adaptive grouping during diffusion editing to preserve low-frequency structure while allowing high-frequency visual changes.
- [GAIA: Rethinking Action Quality Assessment for AI-Generated Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.06087): Introduces an action-quality assessment model for AI-generated videos, focusing on whether generated motion matches intended actions.
- [Aid: Adapting Image2video Diffusion Models for Instruction-Guided Video Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.06465): Adapts image-to-video diffusion models to follow natural-language instructions for video prediction and future-frame generation.
- [Motion Consistency Model: Accelerating Video Diffusion with Disentangled Motion-Appearance Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.06890): Distills video diffusion into a faster consistency model by separating motion dynamics from appearance preservation.
- [4Real: Towards Photorealistic 4D Scene Generation via Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.07472): Uses video diffusion priors to generate photorealistic 4D scenes with consistent appearance, geometry, and temporal evolution.
- [VideoLLaMA 2: Advancing Spatial-Temporal Modeling and Audio Understanding in Video-LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.07476): Advances video-language modeling through spatial-temporal and audio-aware modeling for video LLMs.
- [Hierarchical Patch Diffusion Models for High-Resolution Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.07792): Generates high-resolution videos with hierarchical patch diffusion, decomposing large frames into manageable patch-level denoising stages.
- [Vivid-ZOO: Multi-View Video Generation with Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.08659): Extends diffusion generation to multi-view videos, keeping object appearance consistent across synchronized viewpoints.
- [Hallo: Hierarchical Audio-Driven Visual Synthesis for Portrait Image Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.08801): Synthesizes portrait animation from audio with a hierarchical visual generation pipeline that controls face motion and temporal smoothness.
- [Training-free Camera Control for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.10126): Adds camera-control mechanisms to video generation without retraining the base generator.
- [Long Story Short: Story-level Video Understanding from 20K Short Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.10221): Builds SF20K from 20,143 public short films and instruction-tunes video VLMs for long-term story-level QA, reducing leakage compared with commercial-movie datasets.
- [ViD-GPT: Introducing GPT-style Autoregressive Generation in Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.10981): Adds causal temporal attention, frame-as-prompt conditioning, and key-value caching to video diffusion so autoregressive long-video generation can reuse all previously generated frames.
- [Holistic-Motion2D: Scalable Whole-body Human Motion Generation in 2D Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.11253): Builds a million-sequence 2D whole-body motion corpus with pose annotations and text, then trains a part-aware and confidence-aware baseline for scalable text-to-motion generation.
- [VIA: Unified Spatiotemporal Video Adaptation Framework for Global and Local Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.12831): Adapts pretrained image editing at test time for local mask control and adds global video-context propagation so text edits stay consistent over minute-long videos.
- [ExVideo: Extending Video Diffusion Models via Parameter-Efficient Post-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.14130): Post-tunes existing short-clip video diffusion models with parameter-efficient temporal-extension strategies to generate longer videos at lower training cost.
- [Image Conductor: Precision Control for Interactive Video Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.15339): Separates camera and object motion with camera and object LoRA weights, adds camera-free guidance, and trains on trajectory-curated motion data for precise single-image video control.
- [Identifying and Solving Conditional Image Leakage in Image-to-Video Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.15735): Diagnoses image-to-video diffusion as over-relying on the conditioning image at large timesteps, then reduces low-motion outputs with analytic initialization and time-dependent conditional-image noise.
- [Video-Infinity: Distributed Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.16260): Splits long-video diffusion inference across multiple GPUs with cross-device timing and context exchange, extending short-sequence generators without retraining.
- [FreeTraj: Tuning-Free Trajectory Control in Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.16863): Controls generated object trajectories without training by modifying initial noise sampling and attention computation, with extensions for camera-motion control.
- [Text-Animator: Controllable Visual Text Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.17777): Adds text-embedding injection and visual-text control modules so text-to-video models can preserve rendered letters while keeping motion coherent.
- [MimicMotion: High-Quality Human Motion Video Generation with Confidence-aware Pose Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.19680): Uses confidence-aware pose guidance, regional loss amplification, and long-video stitching to generate arbitrary-length human videos that follow motion references.
- [SVG: 3D Stereoscopic Video Generation via Denoising Frame Matrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.00367): Converts monocular video generations into stereoscopic videos by depth-warping views and using a frame-matrix inpainting scheme with disocclusion-boundary reinjection.
- [Hierarchical Memory for Long Video QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.00603): Uses Flash-VStream STAR Memory to compress long visual-token streams and finetunes with MovieChat-1K video and audio for LOVEU long-video QA.
- [DiffIR2VR-Zero: Zero-Shot Video Restoration with Diffusion-based Image Restoration Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.01519): Reuses pretrained image-restoration diffusion models for video restoration through hierarchical latent warping and hybrid optical-flow or feature token merging, avoiding new training.
- [Boosting Consistency in Story Visualization with Rich-Contextual Conditional Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.02482): Improves story visualization with a frame-prior transformer diffusion stage and rich contextual conditions from known clips and captions to maintain semantic and temporal consistency.
- [LivePortrait: Efficient Portrait Animation with Stitching and Retargeting Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.03168): Scales an implicit-keypoint portrait-animation framework with mixed image-video training, stitching, and retargeting controls for efficient video-driven head motion transfer.
- [VCoME: Verbal Video Composition with Multimodal Editing Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.04697): Defines verbal-video composition with editing effects, curates an effect-composition dataset, and uses a multimodal model to select text, visual, and audio effects.
- [LaSe-E2V: Towards Language-guided Semantic-Aware Event-to-Video Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.05547): Adds language-guided text-conditional diffusion, event-guided spatiotemporal attention, event-aware mask loss, and noise initialization to event-to-video reconstruction.
- [Compositional Video Generation as Flow Equalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.06182): Introduces Vico, which builds a spatiotemporal attention graph over prompt tokens and equalizes token influence so multi-concept video prompts are not dominated by a single word.
- [VIMI: Grounding Video Generation through Multi-modal Instruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.06304): Constructs multimodal prompt-video data with retrieved in-context examples, then pretrains and finetunes one model for grounded video generation under text, image, and mixed instructions.
- [Video-to-Audio Generation with Hidden Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.07464): Models hidden alignment for generating audio from video.
- [VEnhancer: Generative Space-Time Enhancement for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.07667): Trains a video ControlNet on space-time augmentations so generated low-resolution, low-frame-rate clips can be simultaneously upsampled in space and time while reducing flicker.
- [Still-Moving: Customized Video Generation without Customized Video Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.08674): Transfers customized text-to-image weights into inflated text-to-video models with an adaptation scheme that preserves subject or style personalization without customized videos.
- [Live2Diff: Live Stream Translation via Uni-directional Attention in Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.08701): Replaces bidirectional temporal attention with unidirectional attention so a video diffusion model can translate live streams using only current and past frames.
- [Video Diffusion Alignment via Reward Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.08737): Aligns pretrained video diffusion models by backpropagating dense pixel-space gradients from preference-trained visual reward models, reducing the need for supervised target videos.
- [Learning Online Scale Transformation for Talking Head Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.09965): Learns a scale-transformation module from source and driving keypoints so one-shot face reenactment can adjust mismatched face sizes during generation.
- [Noise Calibration: Plug-and-play Content-Preserving Video Enhancement using Pre-trained Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.10285): Optimizes denoising noise under a structure-preserving loss so pretrained video diffusion models can enhance videos without retraining an expert model.
- [Masked Generative Video-to-Audio Transformers with Enhanced Synchronicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.10387): Couples a full-band audio codec with a sequence-to-sequence masked generative transformer so video-to-audio outputs preserve semantic match, audio quality, and action-sound timing.
- [Animate3D: Animating Any 3D Model with Multi-view Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.11398): Trains a multi-view video diffusion prior on rendered 3D animations and combines it with reconstruction and 4D score distillation to animate static 3D assets.
- [MERLIN: Multimodal Embedding Refinement via LLM-based Iterative Navigation for Text-Video Retrieval-Rerank Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.12508): Runs a training-free LLM QA feedback loop that iteratively refines query embeddings for text-video retrieval and reranking on MSR-VTT, MSVD, and ActivityNet.
- [Towards Understanding Unsafe Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.12581): Analyzes unsafe outputs from open video generation models, clusters 2,112 generated clips into categories such as violent, terrifying, pornographic, political, and distorted content.
- [4Dynamic: Text-to-4D Generation with Hybrid Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.12684): Combines a text-to-video prior with staged static and dynamic 4D generation so score distillation receives direct motion supervision for realistic text-to-4D scenes.
- [VD3D: Taming Large Video Diffusion Transformers for 3D Camera Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.12781): Adds ControlNet-style Plucker-coordinate camera conditioning to transformer-based video diffusion models for fine-grained 3D camera motion control.
- [RealViformer: Investigating Attention for Real-World Video Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.13987): Shows channel attention is less artifact-sensitive than spatial covariance attention in real-world VSR, then builds a channel-attention super-resolution model with covariance rescaling.
- [Unlearning Concepts from Text-to-Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.14209): Studies concept unlearning for text-to-video diffusion, giving a model-side control mechanism for generated video content.
- [M2D2M: Multi-Motion Generation from Text with Discrete Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.14502): Uses dynamic transition probabilities and two-phase denoising in a discrete diffusion model to generate coherent text-conditioned sequences containing multiple human actions.
- [Anchored Diffusion for Video Face Reenactment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.15153): Extends diffusion transformers with temporal guidance and non-uniform temporal-spacing training to synthesize longer face-reenactment videos with smoother segment transitions.
- [Cinemo: Consistent and Controllable Image Animation with Motion Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.15642): Predicts motion residuals and applies structural-similarity guidance so image animation preserves source details while following text-guided motion.
- [MovieDreamer: Hierarchical Generation for Coherent Long Visual Sequence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.16655): Factorizes long-form video creation into autoregressive visual-token planning for narrative coherence followed by diffusion rendering for high-fidelity frames.
- [HumanVid: Demystifying Training Data for Camera-controllable Human Image Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.17438): Releases a curated real and synthetic human-animation dataset and studies camera-controllable generation rather than only 2D body motion control.
- [LinguaLinker: Audio-Driven Portraits Animation with Implicit Facial Control Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.18595): Separates multilingual audio features into implicit gates for mouth, eye, and head motion inside a diffusion portrait-animation pipeline.
- [Faster Image2Video Generation: A Closer Look at CLIP Image Embedding’s Impact on Spatio-Temporal Cross-Attentions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.19205): Finds CLIP image embeddings mainly affect aesthetics in Stable Video Diffusion, then caches a lightweight replacement for cross-attention to speed image-to-video inference.
- [FreeLong: Training-Free Long Video Generation with SpectralBlend Temporal Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.19918): Extends short-video diffusion to longer clips without training by blending spectral components in temporal attention to avoid long-range quality degradation.
- [MotionCraft: Crafting Whole-Body Motion with Plug-and-Play Multimodal Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.21136): Trains a diffusion transformer with coarse-to-fine stages and unified motion formats so text, speech, or music controls can be plugged into whole-body motion generation.
- [Tora: Trajectory-oriented Diffusion Transformer for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.21705): Encodes user trajectories into hierarchical spacetime motion patches and fuses them into a diffusion transformer alongside text and visual conditions.
- [Reenact Anything: Semantic Video Motion Transfer Using Motion-Textual Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.00458): Represents reference-video motion as learned text or image embedding tokens, letting image-to-video models transfer motion while preserving target appearance and layout.
- [MDT-A2G: Exploring Masked Diffusion Transformers for Co-Speech Gesture Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.03312): Applies a masked diffusion transformer directly to gesture sequences, using mask modeling to strengthen temporal relations in speech-driven co-speech gesture generation.
- [Puppet-Master: Scaling Interactive Video Generation as a Motion Prior for Part-Level Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.04631): Fine-tunes an image-to-video generator on Objaverse-Animation-HQ with drag-point conditioning and all-to-first attention to model part-level object dynamics.
- [mPLUG-Owl3: Towards Long Image-Sequence Understanding in Multi-Modal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.04840): Adds hyper-attention blocks that place vision and language in a shared language-guided semantic space for long image sequences, interleaved image-text inputs, and lengthy videos.
- [High-Fidelity and Lip-Synced Talking Face Synthesis via Landmark-Based Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.05416): Uses facial landmarks as intermediate structure inside a diffusion talking-face model, reducing audio-to-visual ambiguity while preserving lip sync and appearance detail.
- [HeadGAP: Few-Shot 3D Head Avatar via Generalizable Gaussian Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.06019): Learns Gaussian-splatting head priors from large-scale multiview dynamic data, then personalizes animatable 3D head avatars from few-shot in-the-wild inputs.
- [ControlNeXt: Powerful and Efficient Control for Image and Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.06070): Replaces heavy ControlNet-style branches with a lightweight control architecture that works for image and video diffusion and remains compatible with LoRA style weights.
- [CogVideoX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.06072): An open text-to-video diffusion model with an expert transformer, scaling transformer-based video denoising and training for high-quality generation.
- [FancyVideo: Towards Dynamic and Consistent Video Generation via Cross-frame Textual Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.08189): Adds a cross-frame textual guidance module with temporal information injection and affinity refinement so text control becomes frame-specific for dynamic, consistent videos.
- [LongVILA: Scaling Long-Context Visual Language Models for Long Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.10188): Extends VILA to thousands of video frames through long-context training, long-video supervised tuning, and multimodal sequence parallelism for 2M-token contexts.
- [TrackGo: A Flexible and Efficient Method for Controllable Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.11475): Controls object parts, trajectories, and background motion with free-form masks and arrows injected by a lightweight TrackAdapter in temporal self-attention layers.
- [Real-Time Video Generation with Pyramid Attention Broadcast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.12588): Speeds DiT-based video generation without training by broadcasting redundant attention outputs across denoising steps in a pyramid schedule, including distributed sequence parallelism.
- [xGen-VideoSyn-1: High-fidelity Text-to-Video Synthesis with Compressed Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.12590): Combines spatial-temporal VidVAE compression, a divide-and-merge long-sequence strategy, and a DiT with spatial and temporal attention trained from 13M video-text pairs.
- [EasyControl: Transfer ControlNet to Video Diffusion for Controllable Generation and Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.13005): Transfers ControlNet-style condition features into video diffusion through condition adapters, enabling single-condition-map control and interpolation without rebuilding each control branch.
- [CustomCrafter: Customized Video Generation with Preserving Motion and Concept Composition Abilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.13239): Preserves motion generation and concept composition during subject customization with a plug-and-play parameter update module, avoiding extra guiding videos or recovery finetuning.
- [TVG: A Training-Free Transition Video Generation Method With Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.13413): Generates transition clips without training by fitting latent trajectories with Gaussian Process Regression, interpolation controls, and frequency-aware bidirectional fusion.
- [Training-free Long Video Generation with Chain of Diffusion Model Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.13423): Chains off-the-shelf diffusion experts for structure control and spatiotemporal refinement, using coordinated denoising and long-video constraints for up to 600 frames.
- [MegActor-Σ: Unlocking Flexible Mixed-Modal Control in Portrait Animation with Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.14975): Uses a mixed-modal conditional diffusion transformer and modality-decoupling control training to balance audio and visual signals for portrait animation.
- [Generative Inbetweening: Adapting Image-to-Video Models for Keyframe Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.15239): Adapts an image-to-video diffusion model for keyframe interpolation by finetuning a backward-time counterpart and combining forward and backward estimates during sampling.
- [GenRec: Unifying Video Generation and Recognition with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.15241): Finetunes Stable Video Diffusion with random-frame conditioning so one diffusion framework supports robust video recognition and class-conditioned generation.
- [Kangaroo: A Powerful Video-Language Model Supporting Long-context Video Input](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.15542): Curates high-quality video-language data and uses curriculum training over resolution and frame count to build an 8B long-context video LMM.
- [ReconX: Reconstruct Any Scene From Sparse Views With Video Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.16767): Reframes sparse-view 3D reconstruction as video generation by conditioning a video diffusion model on a global point cloud, then optimizing confidence-aware 3D Gaussian Splatting from the generated views.
- [Compositional 3D-aware Video Generation with LLM Director](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.00558): Uses an LLM director to decompose prompts into 3D concepts, composes expert-generated 3D representations with multimodal LLM trajectory guidance, and refines frames with 2D diffusion SDS.
- [OD-VAE: An Omni-dimensional Video Compressor for Improving Latent Video Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.01199): Compresses video both spatially and temporally with OD-VAE variants, tail initialization, and arbitrary-length inference for more efficient latent video diffusion.
- [AMG: Avatar Motion Guided Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.01502): Conditions video diffusion on controlled 3D-avatar renderings, giving multi-person human video generation explicit control over camera position, body motion, and background style.
- [DiVE: DiT-based Video Generation with Enhanced Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.01595): Builds a DiT video generator with enhanced conditioning controls, using the transformer backbone as the controllable synthesis model rather than a generic diffusion placeholder.
- [CyberHost: Taming Audio-driven Avatar Diffusion Model with Region Codebook Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.01876): Uses Region Codebook Attention and human-prior training signals such as body movement maps, hand clarity, and local enhancement supervision for zero-shot audio-driven full-body animation.
- [ViewCrafter: Taming Video Diffusion Models for High-fidelity Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.02048): Uses video diffusion models for high-fidelity novel-view synthesis from sparse visual inputs.
- [Solving Video Inverse Problems Using Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.02574): Treats video time as an image-diffusion batch dimension and adds batch-consistent sampling so image diffusion models can solve spatiotemporal video inverse problems.
- [Loopy: Taming Audio-Driven Portrait Avatar with Long-Term Motion Dependency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.02634): Adds inter-clip and intra-clip temporal modules plus audio-to-latents mapping so audio-only portrait diffusion learns long-term motion patterns without spatial motion templates.
- [Human-VDM: Learning Single-Image 3D Human Gaussian Splatting from Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.02851): Synthesizes view-consistent human video from one image with a video diffusion module, enhances it with super-resolution and interpolation, then fits 3D Gaussian Splatting.
- [LongLLaVA: Scaling Multi-modal LLMs to 1000 Images Efficiently via a Hybrid Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.02889): Combines Mamba and Transformer blocks with progressive training to process very long image sequences, supporting video understanding and dense visual-context inputs.
- [SVP: Style-Enhanced Vivid Portrait Talking Head Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.03270): Learns a probabilistic intrinsic-style prior from expressions and audio, then injects it through cross-attention into Stable Diffusion for controllable vivid talking-head generation.
- [Phy124: Fast Physics-Driven 4D Content Generation from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.07179): Integrates physical simulation directly into single-image 4D generation, controls dynamics with external forces, and avoids diffusion sampling during the 4D motion phase.
- [Hi3D: Pursuing High-Resolution Image-to-3D Generation with Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.07452): Converts single-image 3D generation into orbital video generation, adding camera-pose conditioning and a 3D-aware video-to-video refiner before Gaussian-splatting reconstruction.
- [DrawingSpinUp: 3D Animation from Single Character Drawings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.08615): Removes and restores view-dependent contour lines and refines single-line thin structures so amateur character drawings can become plausible 3D animations.
- [MFCLIP: Multi-Modal Fine-Grained CLIP for Generalizable Diffusion Face Forgery Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.09724): Extends CLIP with fine-grained text prompts, image-noise visual traces, and sample-pair attention to detect diffusion face forgeries across generators and datasets.
- [OSV: One Step is Enough for High-Quality Image to Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.11367): Combines consistency distillation with GAN training and a latent-space video discriminator so image-to-video diffusion can generate high-quality outputs in one step.
- [GaussianHeads: End-to-End Learning of Drivable Gaussian Head Avatars from Coarse-to-fine Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.11951): Learns drivable Gaussian head avatars end to end by deforming coarse facial geometry, initializing 3D Gaussians on the surface, and refining them for real-time novel-view facial animation.
- [DNI: Dilutional Noise Initialization for Diffusion Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.13037): Adds region-specific noise dilution to the initial latent noise so diffusion video editors can perform non-rigid motion and structure changes instead of only rigid overlays.
- [Temporally Aligned Audio for Video with Autoregression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.13689): Introduces V-AURA, an autoregressive video-to-audio model with high-frame-rate visual features and cross-modal fusion, plus VisualSound for relevance and temporal-alignment evaluation.
- [Dormant: Defending against Pose-driven Human Image Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.14424): Optimizes protective perturbations on a single human image to induce appearance-feature misextraction and frame incoherence in downstream pose-driven animation models.
- [Fake It till You Make It: Curricular Dynamic Forgery Augmentations towards General Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.14444): Improves general deepfake detection by scheduling dynamic forgery augmentations that expose detectors to progressively harder synthetic manipulations.
- [Video-to-Audio Generation with Fine-grained Temporal Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.14709): Conditions latent audio diffusion on frame-level semantics extracted with Grounding SAM, improving temporal alignment between generated audio and visual events.
- [LoVA: Long-form Video-to-Audio Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.15157): Uses a Diffusion Transformer for long-form video-to-audio generation, targeting the concatenation inconsistency that UNet-based V2A systems show on long inputs.
- [MIMAFace: Face Animation via Motion-Identity Modulated Appearance Feature Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.15179): Modulates CLIP appearance features with motion and identity signals and adds inter-clip affinity learning for temporally consistent facial animation from public data.
- [StarVid: Enhancing Semantic Alignment in Video Diffusion Models via Spatial and SynTactic Guided Attention Refocusing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.15259): Uses LLM-planned motion trajectories, a spatial-aware cross-attention loss, and verb-noun syntax contrast to bind subjects and motions in T2V diffusion.
- [Stable Video Portraits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.18083): Person-specific finetunes Stable Diffusion, lifts it to video with temporal 3DMM conditioning, and uses temporal denoising for editable talking-face avatars.
- [PhysGen: Rigid-Body Physics-Grounded Image-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.18964): Couples image understanding, rigid-body image-space simulation, and diffusion rendering so users can control image-to-video motion with forces and torques.
- [FreeMask: Rethinking the Importance of Attention Masks for Zero-Shot Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.20500): Uses Mask Matching Cost to select timestep-appropriate cross-attention masks and improves masked fusion across temporal, cross, and self-attention for zero-shot video editing.
- [ImmersePro: End-to-End Stereo Video Synthesis Via Implicit Disparity Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.00262): Uses implicit disparity learning for end-to-end stereo video synthesis.
- [MM-LDM: Multi-Modal Latent Diffusion Model for Sounding Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.01594): Unifies audio and video as image-like representations, then uses modality-specific perceptual latents and a shared semantic space for joint sounding-video generation.
- [Loong: Generating Minute-level Long Videos with Autoregressive Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.02757): Models text and video tokens as one autoregressive sequence, then uses progressive short-to-long training and inference re-encoding to extend generation to minute-long videos.
- [Redefining Temporal Modeling in Video Diffusion: The Vectorized Timestep Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.03160): Replaces a clip-level scalar timestep with per-frame vectorized timesteps, letting video diffusion assign independent noise schedules for interpolation, image-to-video, and long-video synthesis.
- [VideoGuide: Improving Video Diffusion Models without Training Through a Teacher’s Guide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.04364): Improves pretrained text-to-video diffusion at inference time by interpolating a guiding video diffusion model's early denoised samples into the sampling model, boosting temporal consistency without training.
- [L-C4: Language-Based Video Colorization for Creative and Consistent Color](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.04972): Uses language-guided instance-aware text embeddings, temporally deformable attention, and cross-clip fusion for semantically controlled and temporally consistent video colorization.
- [Beyond FVD: Enhanced Evaluation Metrics for Video Generation Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.05203): Diagnoses FVD's Gaussian, temporal-sensitivity, and sample-size failures, then proposes JEDi with JEPA embeddings and MMD as a more human-aligned video-quality metric.
- [The Dawn of Video Generation: Preliminary Explorations with SORA-like Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.05227): Analyzes SORA-like video generators through architecture, data, training, and evaluation gaps, emphasizing the shift from U-Net to DiT models and the limits of current metrics for long controllable video.
- [ViBiDSampler: Enhancing Video Interpolation Using Bidirectional Diffusion Sampler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.05651): Samples sequentially along forward and backward image-to-video paths, using start and end frames plus CFG++ and DDS guidance for bounded keyframe interpolation.
- [T2V-Turbo-v2: Enhancing Video Generation Model Post-Training through Data, Reward, and Conditional Guidance Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.05677): Distills a consistency text-to-video model with curated data, multiple reward models, and conditional guidance energy design, including motion guidance in the teacher ODE solver.
- [Pyramidal Flow Matching for Efficient Video Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.05954): Reinterprets denoising as linked pyramid stages and trains one DiT end to end, reducing full-resolution cost while supporting autoregressive temporal pyramids.
- [ByTheWay: Boost Your Text-to-Video Generation Model to Higher Quality in a Training-free Way](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.06241): Uses training-free temporal self-guidance and Fourier-based motion enhancement to reduce temporal-attention disparities and increase motion amplitude without adding parameters or sampling cost.
- [MimicTalk: Mimicking a personalized and expressive 3D talking face in minutes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.06734): Adapts a person-agnostic 3D talking-face model with static-dynamic hybrid tuning and in-context stylized audio-to-motion, reducing personalized adaptation to minutes.
- [DreamMesh4D: Video-to-4D Generation with Sparse-Controlled Gaussian-Mesh Hybrid Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.06756): Binds Gaussian splats to mesh faces and drives them with sparse deformation graphs plus geometric skinning, improving 4D object consistency from monocular video.
- [TextToon: Real-Time Text Toonify Head Avatar from Single Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.07160): Learns a conditional-embedding triplane in a Gaussian deformation field and adds adaptive pixel translation for real-time text-stylized drivable head avatars.
- [AvatarGO: Zero-shot 4D Human-Object Interaction Generation and Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.07164): Uses LLM-guided contact retargeting and correspondence-aware motion optimization to synthesize animatable 4D human-object interaction scenes directly from text.
- [Language-Guided Joint Audio-Visual Editing via One-Shot Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.07463): One-shot adapts paired audio and vision diffusion models, then adds cross-modal semantic enhancement to avoid language-vision neglect in audio-visual editing.
- [Hallo2: Long-Duration and High-Resolution Audio-Driven Portrait Image Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.07718): Extends audio-driven portrait diffusion to long, 4K videos with noisy patch-drop augmentation, latent vector quantization, temporal alignment, and expression text labels.
- [Progressive Autoregressive Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.08151): Assigns progressively increasing per-frame noise levels and denoises in small shifted intervals to autoregressively generate 60-second videos with less quality drift.
- [DINTR: Tracking via Diffusion-based Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10053): Recasts object tracking as diffusion-style interpolation, replacing Gaussian noise mapping with an interpretable conditional interpolation process over video frames.
- [VideoAgent: Self-Improving Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10076): Refines generated robot video plans through self-conditioning consistency and environment feedback, reducing hallucinated physics before downstream control extraction.
- [Animate-X: Universal Character Image Animation with Enhanced Motion Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10306): Adds a Pose Indicator that combines CLIP motion gist with explicit simulated pose inputs, enabling LDM-based animation for humans and anthropomorphic characters.
- [DragEntity:Trajectory Guided Video Generation using Entity and Positional Relationships](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10751): Represents entities and positional relationships so users can drag multiple objects along trajectories while preserving relative spatial structure in generated videos.
