- [Cavia: Camera-controllable Multi-view Video Diffusion with View-Integrated Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10774): Replaces spatial and temporal attention with view-integrated attention so one input image can yield multiple camera-controlled, spatiotemporally consistent videos.
- [MaskControl: Spatio-Temporal Control for Masked Motion Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10780): Adds logits regularization, inference-time logit optimization, and differentiable expectation sampling to masked motion models for any-joint and any-frame control.
- [Boosting Camera Motion Control for Video Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10802): Finds camera-control degradation in DiTs is driven by conditioning choice, then adds Camera Motion Guidance and sparse pose specification for long-video camera control.
- [High-Resolution Frame Interpolation with Patch-based Cascaded Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.11838): Introduces HiFI, a patch-based cascaded pixel diffusion model that performs fixed-resolution patch upsampling for high-resolution frame interpolation.
- [The State of Robot Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.12172): Surveys robot motion generation across explicit model-based and learned implicit methods, making this an adjacent motion-generation reference rather than a core video generator.
- [Shaping a Stabilized Video by Mitigating Unintended Changes for Concept-Augmented Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.12526): Stabilizes concept-augmented video editing by suppressing unintended background and identity changes, making concept insertion less prone to framewise drift.
- [AVID: Adapting Video Diffusion Models to World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.12822): Trains a learned-mask adapter on small action-labeled datasets to turn frozen pretrained video diffusion models into action-conditioned world models.
- [Movie Gen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.13720): A family of media foundation models covering text-to-video, personalized video, precise editing, and audio generation.
- [DAWN: Dynamic Frame Avatar with Non-autoregressive Diffusion Framework for Talking Head Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.13726): Replaces autoregressive talking-head rollout with a non-autoregressive diffusion framework that generates dynamic-length avatar frames all at once, reducing error accumulation and latency.
- [DreamVideo-2: Zero-Shot Subject-Driven Video Customization with Precise Motion Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.13830): Balances subject preservation and box-sequence motion control with a subject-binding objective and motion-control adapter for zero-shot customized video.
- [VidPanos: Generative Panoramic Videos from Casual Panning Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.13832): Turns casual panning clips into generative panoramic videos by estimating camera trajectory and synthesizing temporally consistent wide-field video content.
- [Addressing Blind Guessing: Calibration of Selection Bias in Multiple-Choice Question Answering by Video Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.14248): Diagnoses answer-position selection bias in video-language MCQA and introduces BOLD post-processing calibration, making it an adjacent video-model evaluation and debiasing entry rather than a generator.
- [FrameBridge: Improving Image-to-Video Generation with Bridge Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.15371): Inserts a bridge model between text-to-image and image-to-video diffusion so generated starting frames better preserve motion cues and downstream temporal consistency.
- [Allegro: Open the Black Box of Commercial-Level Video Generation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.15458): Releases an open video-generation model report covering Allegro's data pipeline, architecture, training recipe, and evaluation, with code and weights for commercial-level temporal consistency.
- [CamI2V: Camera-Controlled Image-to-Video Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.15957): Improves controllable video generation with explicit camera, object-motion, or 3D-consistency control.
- [Warped Diffusion: Solving Video Inverse Problems with Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.16152): Treats videos as continuous warping transformations between image frames and adds test-time self-equivariant guidance so image diffusion models can solve video inpainting and 8x super-resolution with less flicker.
- [LongVU: Spatiotemporal Adaptive Compression for Long Video-Language Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.17434): Compresses long videos for VLMs by pruning redundant frames with DINOv2 features, applying text-guided frame feature reduction, and reducing spatial tokens using temporal dependencies.
- [Pay Attention and Move Better: Harnessing Attention for Interactive Motion Generation and Training-free Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.18977): Builds MotionCLR with explicit self-attention and cross-attention modeling, then edits human motions by manipulating attention maps for emphasis, replacement, and example-based generation.
- [Framer: Interactive Frame Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.18978): Lets users guide frame interpolation by specifying keypoint trajectories between start and end images, with an autopilot mode that estimates and refines keypoints automatically.
- [FasterCache: Training-Free Video Diffusion Model Acceleration with High Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.19355): Reuses cached features during denoising to accelerate video diffusion at inference time, targeting high-quality speedups without finetuning the generator.
- [MarDini: Masked Autoregressive Diffusion for Video Generation at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.20280): Combines masked autoregressive temporal planning with lightweight spatial diffusion generation so one model supports interpolation, image-to-video, and video expansion under arbitrary frame masks.
- [ARLON: Boosting Diffusion Transformers with Autoregressive Models for Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.20502): Bridges autoregressive models and diffusion transformers with a latent VQ-VAE, adaptive semantic injection, and coarse visual-token training for long dynamic video generation.
- [On Learning Multi-Modal Forgery Representation for Diffusion Generated Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.23623): Proposes MM-Det, which derives multi-modal forgery representations from an LMM, augments them with in-and-across-frame attention, and evaluates on the DVF diffusion-video forensics dataset.
- [Enhancing Motion in Text-to-Video Generation with Decomposed Encoding and Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.24219): Decomposes text encoding and conditioning into content and motion branches, then adds text-motion and video-motion supervision to improve T2V motion dynamics.
- [URAvatar: Universal Relightable Gaussian Codec Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.24223): Trains a universal 3D-Gaussian relightable head-avatar model on controlled multiview scans, then finetunes it from a phone scan using inverse rendering for real-time animation and relighting.
- [GameGen-X: Interactive Open-world Game Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.00769): Trains a diffusion transformer on more than one million gameplay clips from over 150 games and adds InstructNet control experts for interactive open-world game video continuation.
- [Video prediction using score-based conditional density estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.00842): Frames next-frame prediction as implicit conditional-density estimation, training sequence-to-image networks with a resilience-to-noise objective to sample likely future trajectories.
- [Fast and Memory-Efficient Video Diffusion Using Streamlined Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.01171): Reduces video diffusion memory and compute with Feature Slicer, Operator Grouping, and Step Rehash, enabling AnimateDiff-style generation on a single consumer GPU.
- [MoMu-Diffusion: On Learning Long-Term Motion-Music Synchronization and Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.01805): Aligns motion and music latents with BiCoR-VAE, then uses a multimodal Transformer diffusion model and cross-guidance sampling for long beat-matched motion or music generation.
- [AutoVFX: Physically Realistic Video Editing from Natural Language Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.02394): Combines neural scene modeling, LLM-generated code, and physical simulation to turn a source video plus natural-language instructions into photorealistic dynamic VFX edits.
- [Adaptive Caching for Faster Video Generation With Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.02397): Introduces AdaCache, a training-free video-DiT acceleration method that adapts cache schedules per sample and uses motion regularization to trade compute for quality.
- [DimensionX: Create Any 3D and 4D Scenes from a Single Image with Controllable Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.04928): Uses ST-Director with dimension-aware LoRAs to decouple spatial structure and temporal evolution in video diffusion, reconstructing controllable 3D and 4D scenes from one image.
- [SG-I2V: Self-Guided Trajectory Control in Image-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.04989): Provides zero-shot trajectory control for image-to-video generation using only a pretrained I2V diffusion model, avoiding finetuning and annotated motion datasets.
- [ReCapture: Generative Video Camera Controls for User-Provided Videos using Masked Video Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.05003): Re-angles a user-provided video by creating a noisy anchor with a new camera path, then using masked video fine-tuning to regenerate a clean temporally consistent clip.
- [Don't Look Twice: Faster Video Transformers with Run-Length Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.05222): Speeds video transformers by run-length tokenizing repeated temporal patches before inference, reducing token counts without dataset-specific tuning.
- [Tell What You Hear From What You See - Video to Audio Generation Through Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.05679): Builds VATT with an LLM-based video-to-text converter and transformer audio-token generator so optional text prompts can steer video-to-audio generation.
- [Improved Video VAE for Latent Video Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.06449): Improves latent video diffusion compression with keyframe-based temporal compression and group causal convolution, addressing image-VAE initialization and causal-frame imbalance.
- [I2VControl-Camera: Precise Video Camera Control with Adjustable Motion Strength](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.06525): Controls image-to-video camera motion with point trajectories in camera coordinates and a higher-order trajectory operator for adjustable subject-motion strength.
- [A Multidimensional Measurement of Photorealistic Avatars Quality of Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.09066): Provides an open subjective test framework for photorealistic avatar quality across ten human factors, showing weak correlation between most dimensions and PSNR, SSIM, LPIPS, FID, or FVD.
- [JoyVASA: Portrait and Animal Image Animation with Diffusion-Based Audio-Driven Facial Dynamics and Head Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.09209): Separates static 3D facial representation from dynamic facial motion, then uses an audio-conditioned diffusion transformer to animate human and animal portraits across languages.
- [EchoMimicV2: Towards Striking, Simplified, and Semi-Body Human Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.10061): Simplifies half-body human animation with audio-pose dynamic harmonization, head partial attention for using headshot data, and phase-specific denoising losses.
- [OnlyFlow: Optical Flow Based Motion Conditioning for Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.10501): Conditions text-to-video generation on optical flow extracted from an input video, injecting flow-encoder features into the backbone to transfer motion without task-specific training.
- [AnimateAnything: Consistent and Controllable Animation for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.10836): Converts camera paths, text prompts, and user motion annotations into frame-wise optical-flow priors through multi-scale control fusion, with frequency-domain stabilization for large motion.
- [StableV2V: Stabilizing Shape Consistency in Video-to-Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.11045): Edits the first frame, aligns source motion with the edited prompt, and propagates the aligned content to later frames; the paper also curates DAVIS-Edit for evaluation.
- [Towards motion from video diffusion models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.12831): Probes whether public text-to-video diffusion priors can synthesize human motion by deforming SMPL-X bodies with score distillation sampling.
- [What You See Is What Matters: A Novel Visual and Physics-Based Metric for Evaluating Video Generation Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.13609): Introduces VAMP, a generated-video quality metric with appearance consistency and physical motion plausibility components validated on corrupted and generated videos.
- [Novel View Extrapolation with Video Diffusion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.14208): Uses Stable Video Diffusion priors as ViewExtrapolator to refine artifact-prone radiance-field renders for novel-view extrapolation without SVD finetuning.
- [DissolveStereo: Coarse Depth Injection for Zero-Shot Stereo Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.14295): Generates stereo videos without paired training data using noisy stereo-latent restart, iterative latent harmonization, and dissolved depth maps to improve view and temporal consistency.
- [Unleashing the Potential of Multi-modal Foundation Models and Video Diffusion for 4D Dynamic Physical Scene Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.14423): PhysFlow combines multimodal material inference, 3D Gaussian scene representation, differentiable MPM, video diffusion, and optical-flow guidance for 4D physical scene simulation.
- [Efficient Long Video Tokenization via Coordinate-based Patch Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.14762): CoordTok encodes long clips as factorized triplanes and reconstructs randomly sampled coordinate patches, cutting 128-frame videos to 1,280 tokens instead of thousands.
- [Self-Correcting Text-to-Video Generation with Misalignment Detection and Localized Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.15115): VideoRepair detects fine-grained text-video misalignment with MLLM-generated questions, plans region preservation, and locally regenerates only problematic areas.
- [Optical-Flow Guided Prompt Optimization for Coherent Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.15540): MotionPrompt trains an optical-flow discriminator and optimizes learnable prompt tokens during reverse sampling to guide video diffusion toward natural motion dynamics.
- [ReWind: Understanding Long Videos with Instructed Learnable Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.15556): Adds a read-perceive-write learnable memory and memory-guided frame selection for efficient long-video understanding, making it an adjacent video VLM entry rather than a generator.
- [FATE: Full-head Gaussian Avatar with Textural Editing from Monocular Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.15604): Reconstructs editable 360-degree full-head Gaussian avatars from monocular video using sampling-based densification, neural baking into attribute maps, and universal completion.
- [M3-CVC: Controllable Video Compression with Multimodal Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.15798): Combines LMM-extracted spatiotemporal descriptions, semantic-motion keyframe selection, and text-guided diffusion reconstruction for controllable ultra-low-bitrate video compression.
- [SMGDiff: Soccer Motion Generation using Diffusion Probabilistic Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.16216): Generates controllable soccer player motion with a two-stage trajectory-to-autoregressive-diffusion pipeline and contact guidance for realistic ball-foot interactions.
- [Sonic: Shifting Focus to Global Audio Perception in Portrait Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.16331): Improves audio-driven portrait animation by separating global audio perception into intra-clip and inter-clip cues rather than relying on auxiliary visual stabilization signals.
- [Ca2-VDM: Efficient Autoregressive Video Diffusion Model with Causal Generation and Cache Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.16375): Uses causal feature computation and cache sharing across autoregressive diffusion steps so overlapping conditioning frames are reused instead of recomputed.
- [DreamRunner: Fine-Grained Compositional Story-to-Video Generation with Retrieval-Augmented Motion Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.16657): Structures scripts with an LLM for scene and object layout, retrieves motion priors, and performs test-time adaptation for compositional multi-scene story-to-video generation.
- [Generative Omnimatte: Learning to Decompose Video into Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.16683): Learns video layer decomposition for controllable video generation and editing.
- [Importance-Based Token Merging for Efficient Image and Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.16720): Preserves high-information diffusion tokens during merging, using importance scores such as classifier-free guidance to accelerate AnimateDiff-style video generation with less quality loss.
- [EmotiveTalk: Expressive Talking Head Generation through Audio Information Decoupling and Emotional Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.16726): Decouples audio into lip-motion and expression representations with V-AID, then injects target emotion through an emotional talking-head diffusion backbone for long expressive portraits.
- [InTraGen: Trajectory-controlled Video Generation for Object Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.16804): Adds object-ID interaction encoding, four trajectory-interaction datasets, and a trajectory quality metric so controlled text-to-video models can synthesize multi-object interactions.
- [Pathways on the Image Manifold: Image Editing via Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.16819): Reformulates image editing as an image-to-video transition from the source image to the edited result, using temporal continuity to preserve identity while following complex edit instructions.
- [Free2 Guide: Training-Free Text-to-Video Alignment Using Image LVLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.17041): Uses path-integral control to guide diffusion with black-box image LVLM rewards over stitched video frames, improving text-video alignment without training a video reward model.
- [WF-VAE: Enhancing Video VAE by Wavelet-Driven Energy Flow for Latent Video Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.17459): Builds a wavelet-flow video autoencoder that pushes low-frequency energy into the latent representation and uses causal cache to avoid blockwise long-video discontinuities.
- [Towards Precise Scaling Laws for Video Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.17470): Fits video-DiT scaling laws that explicitly model learning rate and batch size, allowing compute-budgeted model and hyperparameter selection before large-scale training.
- [VideoDirector: Precise Video Editing via Text-to-Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.17592): Fixes text-to-video inversion artifacts with spatial-temporal decoupled guidance, multi-frame null-text optimization, and self-attention control for localized video edits.
- [StableAnimator: High-Quality Identity-Preserving Human Image Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.17697): Animates a reference human image with pose sequences using a face-aware encoder, distribution-aware identity adapter, and HJB-guided denoising for stronger identity preservation.
- [I2VControl: Disentangled and Unified Video Motion Synthesis Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.17765): Unifies camera control, object dragging, and motion brush into point-trajectory representations with spatial partitioning, avoiding conflicts among multiple motion controls.
- [MotionCharacter: Identity-Preserving and Motion Controllable Human Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.18281): Separates action type from motion intensity using text phrases and optical-flow-derived control, enabling fine-grained human motion changes while preserving character identity.
- [HiFiVFS: High Fidelity Video Face Swapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.18293): Builds a Stable Video Diffusion based face-swapping framework with identity-desensitized attribute extraction, adversarial learning, and detailed identity injection for temporal stability.
- [Individual Content and Motion Dynamics Preserved Pruning for Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.18375): Compresses video diffusion into VDMini by pruning shallow content-heavy blocks, retaining deeper motion layers, and distilling frame content plus multi-frame dynamics.
- [CAT4D: Create Anything in 4D with Multi-View Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.18613): Turns a monocular video into camera-specified multi-view videos with a multi-view video diffusion prior, then optimizes deformable 3D Gaussians for 4D reconstruction.
- [AC3D: Analyzing and Improving 3D Camera Control in Video Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.18673): Improves controllable video generation with explicit camera, object-motion, or 3D-consistency control.
- [SPAgent: Adaptive Task Decomposition and Model Selection for General Video Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.18983): Decomposes a user request into generation and editing subtasks, selects specialized video models for each step, and coordinates them with an LLM controller for general-purpose video creation.
- [Timestep Embedding Tells: It’s Time to Cache for Video Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.19108): Uses timestep embeddings to decide when cached video-diffusion features can be reused, reducing redundant denoising computation while preserving generation quality.
- [Gaussians-to-Life: Text-Driven Animation of 3D Gaussian Splatting Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.19233): Animates static 3D Gaussian scenes from text by coupling scene deformation with video-generation priors, turning splatted scenes into temporally coherent motion.
- [Trajectory Attention for Fine-grained Video Motion Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.19324): Adds trajectory attention so user-specified motion paths can guide fine-grained object movement inside video diffusion rather than relying on prompt-only control.
- [Fleximo: Towards Flexible Text-to-Human Motion Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.19459): Generates human-motion videos from flexible text controls, targeting controllable body motion rather than generic text-to-video sampling.
- [Ditto: Motion-Space Diffusion for Controllable Realtime Talking Head Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.19509): Moves talking-head synthesis into a compact motion space so diffusion can produce controllable real-time facial motion before rendering portrait frames.
- [Open-Sora Plan: Open-Source Large Video Generation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.00131): Documents an open-source large video-generation model plan and stack.
- [VISION-XL: High Definition Video Inverse Problem Solver using Latent Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.00156): Uses SDXL latent image diffusion with pseudo-batch consistent sampling and pseudo-batch inversion to solve HD video inverse problems such as deblurring, super-resolution, inpainting, and frame averaging on a single GPU.
- [DreamDance: Animating Human Images by Enriching 3D Geometry Cues from 2D Poses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.00397): Enriches 2D pose inputs with estimated 3D geometry cues, then drives human image animation so generated bodies keep more plausible depth and limb structure.
- [Motion Dreamer: Realizing Physically Coherent Video Generation through Scene-Aware Motion Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.00547): Uses scene-aware motion reasoning to predict physically plausible object trajectories before video synthesis, reducing motion that conflicts with layout or scene constraints.
- [PhyT2V: LLM-Guided Iterative Self-Refinement for Physics-Grounded Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.00596): Wraps text-to-video generation in an LLM-guided self-refinement loop that critiques physical implausibility and revises prompts or conditions toward physics-grounded clips.
- [VideoSAVi: Self-Aligned Video Language Models without Human Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.00624): Self-aligns video LLMs by critiquing initial answers, forming video-derived preference pairs, and applying DPO; it is an adjacent video-understanding alignment entry rather than a video generator.
- [Synergizing Motion and Appearance: Multi-Scale Compensatory Codebooks for Talking Head Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.00719): Jointly learns multi-scale motion and appearance codebooks and uses transformer retrieval to compensate motion flows and appearance features for talking-head generation.
- [Hallo3: Highly Dynamic and Realistic Portrait Image Animation with Video Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.00733): Builds a portrait image animation system on a video diffusion transformer, targeting large head motion and expressive dynamics rather than only small lip-sync changes.
- [Coherent Video Inpainting Using Optical Flow-Guided Efficient Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.00857): Guides diffusion video inpainting with a dual-branch FloED design, restored optical flow, multi-scale flow adapters, latent interpolation, and flow-attention caching for coherent object removal and background restoration.
- [FLOAT: Generative Motion Latent Flow Matching for Audio-Driven Talking Portrait](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.01064): Models talking-portrait motion in a latent flow-matching space, mapping audio to head pose and expression trajectories before rendering temporally smooth portraits.
- [MoTrans: Customized Motion Transfer with Text-driven Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.01343): Transfers customized reference motion into a text-driven video diffusion model, separating reusable motion guidance from target appearance during generation.
- [CPA: Camera-pose-awareness Diffusion Transformer for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.01429): Adds camera-pose control to DiT video generation through Sparse Motion Encoding and Temporal Attention Injection, preserving original DiT weights while supporting camera trajectories and object movement.
- [Enhancing Video-LLM Reasoning via Agent-of-Thoughts Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.01694): Distills Agent-of-Thoughts reasoning traces into video LLM training, improving multi-step video question answering without requiring the agentic teacher at inference time.
- [SEAL: SEmantic Attention Learning for Long Video Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.01798): Represents long videos as compact scene, object, and action entities with diversity-aware attention subset selection; it is off-axis for generation but relevant as a video representation model.
- [World-consistent Video Diffusion with Explicit 3D Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.01821): Adds explicit 3D scene modeling to video diffusion so camera motion and generated content remain consistent in a shared world coordinate frame.
- [Progress-Aware Video Frame Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.02071): Introduces ProgressCaptioner with FrameCap and FrameCapEval to produce frame-level captions that track action progression, an adjacent captioning model useful for keyframe selection and video understanding.
- [Generative Photography: Scene-Consistent Camera Control for Realistic Text-to-Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.02168): Uses 3D-aware scene representation and camera control to render scene-consistent photographic views from text, making it adjacent to video camera-control and novel-view generation.
- [Improving Dynamic Object Interactions in Text-to-Video Generation with AI Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.02617): Aligns text-to-video models for object interactions by using vision-language AI feedback as binary rewards in offline RL-style fine-tuning, improving multi-object dynamics and falling-object realism.
- [Motion Prompting: Controlling Video Generation with Motion Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.02700): Uses motion trajectories as prompts for video generation, translating user-specified paths into object and camera movement constraints.
- [Mimir: Improving Video Diffusion Models for Precise Text Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.03085): Improves prompt comprehension in video diffusion with text-understanding focused training and evaluation, targeting failures on attributes, relations, and compositional details.
- [DIVE: Taming DINO for Subject-Driven Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.03347): Uses DINO features to locate and preserve the target subject during video editing, enabling subject-driven edits without broad background drift.
- [HunyuanVideo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.03603): Reports Tencent's open video foundation model with a video-text latent diffusion architecture, curated data pipeline, and scaling recipe for text-to-video generation.
- [INFP: Audio-Driven Interactive Head Generation in Dyadic Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.04037): Generates interactive talking-head motion from dyadic audio by learning low-dimensional conversational motion latents and denoising them into speaking and listening head behaviors, supported by the DyConv dataset.
- [HANDI: Hand-Centric Text-and-Image Conditioned Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.04189): Focuses text-and-image conditioned diffusion on hand-centric actions by automatically predicting the motion area and adding a Hand Refinement Loss for smoother, clearer hand poses in egocentric scenes.
- [Divot: Diffusion Powers Video Tokenizer for Comprehension and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.04432): Trains a diffusion-powered video tokenizer whose features condition denoising and can be decoded back to video, then couples it with Divot-Vicuna for video comprehension and text-to-video generation.
- [DiCoDe: Diffusion-Compressed Deep Tokens for Autoregressive Video Generation with Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.04446): Uses diffusion-compressed deep video tokens with roughly 1000x token reduction so autoregressive language models can generate seconds-to-minute videos efficiently.
- [MEMO: Memory-Guided Diffusion for Expressive Talking Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.04448): Adds memory-guided diffusion to talking-video generation so identity, expression, and motion cues can be retained across longer portrait clips.
- [Factorized Video Autoencoders for Efficient Generative Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.04452): Compresses videos into a four-plane factorized latent space that grows sublinearly with input size, reducing latent-diffusion memory and compute for video generation, prediction, and interpolation.
- [4Real-Video: Learning Generalizable Photo-Realistic 4D Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.04462): Generates 4D videos over time and viewpoint axes with a two-stream diffusion transformer that alternates temporal and viewpoint updates through synchronization layers.
- [LiFT: Leveraging Human Feedback for Text-to-Video Model Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.04814): Collects human preferences over text-to-video outputs and uses them to align the generator toward better prompt following and perceptual quality.
- [SoPo: Text-to-Motion Generation Using Semi-Online Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.05095): Optimizes text-to-motion models with semi-online preference feedback, updating motion generation toward human-preferred naturalness and text alignment.
- [Mind the Time: Temporally-Controlled Multi-Event Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.05263): Introduces explicit temporal controls for multi-event prompts, letting users specify when events occur instead of relying on prompt order alone.
- [Stag-1: Towards Realistic 4D Driving Simulation with Video Generation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.05280): Builds controllable 4D driving simulation by reconstructing continuous point-cloud scenes from surround-view data and using video generation to synthesize photorealistic views from arbitrary camera poses.
- [MotionStone: Decoupled Motion Intensity Modulation with Diffusion Transformer for Image-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.05848): Decouples motion intensity from image content in a diffusion-transformer image-to-video model, allowing users to strengthen or weaken motion without changing appearance.
- [Track4Gen: Teaching Video Diffusion Models to Track Points Improves Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.06016): Adds point-tracking supervision to Stable Video Diffusion features, merging generation and tracking losses so objects drift less and remain visually coherent across frames.
- [Latent-Reframe: Enabling Camera Control for Video Diffusion Models Without Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.06029): Reframes latent coordinates during inference to control camera motion in pretrained video diffusion models without additional training.
- [UniPaint: Unified Space-Time Video Inpainting via Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.06340): Uses a mixture-of-experts space-time inpainting model to handle object removal, completion, and temporally coherent video region filling in one framework.
- [CoMA: Compositional Human Motion Generation with Multi-modal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.07320): Combines collaborative language and vision agents with a mask-transformer motion generator, body-part codebooks, text-guided editing, and self-correction for compositional short and long human motions.
- [Mobile Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.07583): Designs a compact video diffusion pipeline for mobile devices, reducing model and sampling cost so on-device text-to-video generation becomes feasible.
- [ObjCtrl-2.5D: Training-free Object Control with Camera Poses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.07721): Controls generated objects without training by combining 2.5D object trajectories with camera-pose guidance inside the video diffusion denoising process.
- [STIV: Scalable Text and Image Conditioned Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.07730): Scales a video generator that accepts both text and image conditions, using the image input to anchor appearance while text specifies motion and scene changes.
- [StyleMaster: Stylize Your Video with Artistic Generation and Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.07744): Combines artistic video generation with video-to-video style translation so users can create or restyle videos while preserving temporal consistency.
- [Motion by Queries: Identity-Motion Trade-offs in Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.07750): Analyzes self-attention query features as a shared carrier for motion, layout, and identity, then uses controlled Q injection for training-free motion transfer and multi-shot identity consistency.
- [PortraitTalk: Towards Customizable One-Shot Audio-to-Talking Face Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.07754): Uses a latent diffusion framework with IdentityNet, AnimateNet, audio-reference conditioning, and decoupled text cross-attention to preserve identity while controlling one-shot talking-face motion.
- [3DTrajMaster: Mastering 3D Trajectory for Multi-Entity Motion in Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.07759): Conditions video diffusion on user-specified 6DoF entity trajectories through a gated 3D-motion object injector, domain adaptation, annealed sampling, and the 360-Motion training set.
- [SynCamMaster: Synchronizing Multi-Camera Video Generation from Diverse Viewpoints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.07760): Adds a multi-view synchronization module to a pretrained text-to-video model so 6DoF camera viewpoints share appearance and geometry, trained with UE renders, multi-camera images, and monocular videos.
- [Repurposing Pre-trained Video Diffusion Models for Event-based Video Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.07761): Adapts internet-scale video diffusion priors to event-guided frame interpolation, using sparse high-rate event streams for large-motion guidance and stronger cross-camera generalization.
- [From Slow Bidirectional to Fast Autoregressive Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.07772): Converts a bidirectional video diffusion transformer into a causal autoregressive generator and distills 50 denoising steps to four with ODE initialization, asymmetric teacher-student supervision, and KV caching.
- [Video Motion Transfer with Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.07776): Extracts Attention Motion Flow from DiT cross-frame attention maps and optimizes latents plus positional embeddings at inference time to transfer reference motion without training.
- [Mojito: Motion Trajectory and Intensity Control for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.08948): Combines a training-free cross-attention Directional Motion Control module with an optical-flow-based Motion Intensity Modulator, separating trajectory direction from motion strength in text-to-video diffusion.
- [LVMark: Robust Watermark for Latent Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.09122): Embeds invisible 512-bit watermarks in latent video diffusion by training a decoder on adjacent-frame consistency, 3D wavelet low-frequency signals, RGB features, and importance-weighted latent layers.
- [LatentSync: Audio Conditioned Latent Diffusion Models for Lip Sync](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.09262): Diagnoses visual shortcut learning in audio-conditioned LDM lip sync, then uses StableSyncNet supervision and Temporal Representation Alignment to improve audio-visual correlation and temporal consistency.
- [InstanceCap: Improving Text-to-Video Generation via Instance-aware Structured Caption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.09283): Converts videos into instance-level structured captions with an auxiliary model cluster, curates InstanceVid-22K, and adapts inference prompts to reduce hallucinated objects and motion descriptions.
- [T-SVG: Text-Driven Stereoscopic Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.09323): Builds a zero-shot stereoscopic pipeline that generates a reference video from text, lifts it to 3D point-cloud sequences, renders two parallax views, and repairs artifacts with video inpainting.
- [Video Seal: Open and Efficient Video Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.09492): Trains an open neural video watermark embedder and extractor with codec distortions, image pretraining, hybrid post-training, extractor tuning, and temporal propagation for efficient high-resolution marking.
- [LiftImage3D: Lifting Any Single Image to 3D Gaussians with Video Generation Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.09597): Releases latent video diffusion 3D priors through articulated small-camera trajectories, MASt3R camera calibration, and distortion-aware Gaussian splatting that learns canonical undistorted geometry.
- [Owl-1: Omni World Model for Consistent Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.09600): Models long videos with a persistent latent world state that decodes observations, predicts dynamics, and updates the state, giving video generators longer-horizon conditions than last-frame chaining.
- [OmniDrag: Enabling Motion Control for Omnidirectional Image-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.09623): Fine-tunes an omnidirectional control module with temporal attention and a spherical motion estimator so drag points can control scene-level and object-level motion in 360-degree image-to-video generation.
- [Dynamic Try-On: Taming Video Virtual Try-on with Dynamic Attention Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.09822): Reuses the DiT backbone as the garment encoder, stores garment features with dynamic fusion, and applies limb-aware dynamic attention to stabilize fast human-pose virtual try-on videos.
- [LinGen: Towards High-Resolution Minute-Length Text-to-Video Generation with Linear Computational Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.09856): Replaces quadratic DiT self-attention with the MATE block, combining Mamba2 long-range scanning, review tokens, and Temporal Swin Attention for single-GPU minute-length high-resolution generation.
- [AniSora: Exploring the Frontiers of Animation Video Generation in the Sora Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.10255): Couples a 10M-scale animation data pipeline with a spatiotemporal-mask controllable generator and a 948-video animation benchmark tailored to style, interpolation, and localized image-guided animation.
- [SnapGen-V: Generating a Five-Second Video within Five Seconds on a Mobile Device](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.10494): Searches hardware-efficient temporal layers over a compact image backbone and applies adversarial fine-tuning to a 0.6B four-step video diffusion model that runs on an iPhone 16 Pro Max.
- [Video Diffusion Transformers are In-Context Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.10783): Activates video-DiT in-context generation by concatenating examples across space or time, joint-captioning multi-scene clips, and lightly fine-tuning on curated tasks without changing the base architecture.
- [CFSynthesis: Controllable and Free-view 3D Human Video Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.11067): Uses a texture-SMPL representation plus foreground-background separation to synthesize identity-, motion-, and scene-controllable human videos under free-view 3D poses.
- [DynamicScaler: Seamless and Scalable Video Generation for Panoramic Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.11100): Applies an Offset Shifting Denoiser over a seamless rotating window and Global Motion Guidance, allowing fixed-resolution diffusion models to produce arbitrary-size panoramic videos with constant VRAM.
- [VividFace: A Diffusion-Based Hybrid Framework for High-Fidelity Video Face Swapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.11279): Trains an image-video hybrid diffusion face-swapper with VidFaceVAE, AIDT identity-pose triplets, occlusion augmentation, and 3D conditioning for temporally coherent identity preservation.
- [BiM-VFI: Bidirectional Motion Field-Guided Frame Interpolation for Video with Non-uniform Motions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.11365): Introduces a bidirectional motion-field map, BiM-guided flow estimation, content-aware upsampling, and VFI-centric flow distillation to reduce blur under acceleration and direction changes.
- [SpatialMe: Stereo Video Conversion Using Depth-Warping and Blend-Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.11512): Converts monocular videos to stereo through depth warping, multi-branch blend inpainting, mask-based hierarchical feature updates, disparity expansion, and the 1,000-video StereoV1K dataset.
- [InterDyn: Controllable Interactive Dynamics with Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.11785): Treats a large video generator as an implicit physics simulator, conditioning it on an initial frame and driving-object motion signal to render temporally consistent object interactions.
- [CAP4D: Creating Animatable 4D Portrait Avatars with Morphable Multi-View Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.12093): Uses morphable multi-view diffusion to reconstruct photoreal 4D portrait avatars from one to 100 reference images, then animate and render them in real time.
- [Can video generation replace cinematographers? Research on the cinematic language of generated video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.12223): Builds a 20-category cinematic-language dataset, trains CameraDiff LoRAs for shot framing, angle, and camera motion, and uses CameraCLIP plus CLIPLoRA for multi-shot style composition.
- [Towards a Universal Synthetic Video Detector: From Face or Background Manipulations to Fully AI-Generated Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.12278): Trains UNITE, a SigLIP-feature transformer detector with attention-diversity loss, to cover face edits, background tampering, non-human videos, and fully synthetic T2V and I2V content.
- [FocusChat: Text-guided Long Video Understanding via Spatiotemporal Information Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.12833): Filters long-video tokens with visual-text semantic branches and a Spatial-Temporal Filtering Module, aligning only query-relevant tokens before the LLM and reducing visual-token load to 16.
- [StreetCrafter: Street View Synthesis with Controllable Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.13188): Conditions controllable video diffusion on LiDAR point-cloud renderings, enabling camera-accurate street-view synthesis, pixel-level scene edits, and dynamic-scene rendering on Waymo and PandaSet.
- [Real-time One-Step Diffusion-based Expressive Portrait Videos Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.13479): Distills portrait diffusion into OSA-LCM, a one-step avatar latent consistency model guided by an avatar discriminator and editing fine-tuning for lip-audio consistency and expressive motion.
- [AKiRa: Augmentation Kit on Rays for optical video generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.14158): Trains a camera adapter with a complex ray-based camera model over a video backbone, controlling motion, focal length, distortion, and aperture effects such as zoom, fisheye, and bokeh.
- [VideoDPO: Omni-Preference Alignment for Video Diffusion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.14167): Adapts DPO to video diffusion by building OmniScore preference pairs that balance visual quality and text-video semantic alignment, then reweights pairs during preference training.
- [Autoregressive Video Generation without Vector Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.14169): Reformulates video generation as non-quantized temporal frame-by-frame and spatial set-by-set autoregression, preserving GPT-style causality while using within-frame bidirectional modeling for efficiency.
- [AniDoc: Animation Creation Made Easier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.14173): Uses video diffusion and correspondence matching to color line-art animation from a reference character and can in-between start and end sketches while preserving temporal consistency.
- [TRecViT: A Recurrent Video Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.14294): Factorizes causal video modeling into temporal gated linear recurrent units, spatial self-attention, and channel MLPs, yielding real-time video features with lower parameters, memory, and FLOPs than ViViT.
- [Llama Learns to Direct: DirectorLLM for Human-Centric Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.14484): Trains a Llama-based DirectorLLM to emit pose and instruction signals as human-motion outlines, which condition UNet or DiT renderers for more faithful human-centric video generation.
- [ScaMo: Exploring the Scaling Law in Autoregressive Motion Generation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.14559): Studies scaling laws for text-to-motion generation using a Motion FSQ-VAE tokenizer and text-prefix autoregressive transformer, relating compute to model size, vocabulary, data, and test loss.
- [EnergyMogen: Compositional Human Motion Generation with Energy-Based Diffusion Model in Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.14706): Interprets latent motion diffusion as energy-based composition, adds cross-attention semantic energy, and fuses energy terms to synthesize multi-concept human motions and augment motion data.
- [Prompt-A-Video: Prompt your Video Diffusion Model via Preference-Aligned LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.15156): Evolves reward-guided prompt pools, fine-tunes an LLM, and applies DPO so prompts become video-centric, model-aware, and aligned to downstream video-diffusion preferences.
- [Align Video Diffusion Model with Online Video-Centric Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.15159): Uses video quality assessment models as modality-aligned feedback and online DPO with generated preference pairs plus curriculum updates to optimize video diffusion quality and reduce flicker.
- [AV-Link: Temporally-Aligned Diffusion Features for Cross-Modal Audio-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.15191): Links frozen audio and video diffusion models through a Fusion Block with temporally aligned self-attention, supporting both video-to-audio and audio-to-video generation in one framework.
- [LeviTor: 3D Trajectory Oriented Image-to-Video Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.15214): Extends drag-style image-to-video control into depth by converting object masks into cluster points with relative depth and instance labels before conditioning the video diffusion model.
- [SyncFlow: Toward Temporally Aligned Joint Audio-Video Generation from Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.15220): Uses a dual diffusion-transformer architecture and staged video, audio, then joint training to generate synchronized audio and video from text with zero-shot video-to-audio transfer.
- [MMAudio: Taming Multimodal Joint Training for High-Quality Video-to-Audio Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.15322): Trains a 157M-parameter flow-matching video-to-audio model jointly on video-audio and text-audio data, with a frame-level synchronization module for semantic and temporal alignment.
- [DOLLAR: Few-Step Video Generation Via Distillation and Latent Reward Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.15689): Combines variational score distillation, consistency distillation, and latent reward optimization to reduce video diffusion to one to four steps while preserving 10-second video quality and diversity.
- [Follow-Your-MultiPose: Tuning-Free Multi-Character Text-to-Video Generation via Pose Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.16495): Separates text and pose guidance by extracting pose masks, prompting each character with an LLM, and using spatial-aligned cross-attention plus multi-branch control for multi-character videos.
- [Layer-and Timestep-Adaptive Differentiable Token Compression Ratios for Efficient Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.16822): Learns token-, layer-, and timestep-wise compression ratios for DiT inference, routing unimportant tokens around computation and offering an adjacent efficiency recipe for diffusion-transformer generators.
- [Adapting Image-to-Video Diffusion Models for Large-Motion Frame Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.17042): Adds a conditional encoder to image-to-video diffusion for frame interpolation, using dual-branch feature extraction and cross-frame attention to handle large motion with lower FVD.
- [VidTwin: Video VAE with Decoupled Structure and Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.17726): Encodes videos into separate structure and dynamics latents, using a Q-Former for low-frequency motion trends and spatially averaged dynamics vectors for rapid fine-grained movement.
- [Smooth-Foley: Creating Continuous Sound for Video-to-Audio Generation Under Semantic Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.18157): Guides video-to-audio generation with text labels across time, combining frame and temporal adapters over pretrained text-to-audio models for continuous sounds with better semantic and temporal alignment.
- [DiTCtrl: Exploring Attention Control in Multi-Modal Diffusion Transformer for Tuning-Free Multi-Prompt Longer Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.18597): Treats multi-prompt generation as temporal editing, using mask-guided attention control and attention sharing inside MM-DiT to produce smooth prompt transitions without training, plus MPVBench.
- [ZeroHSI: Zero-Shot 4D Human-Scene Interaction by Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.18600): Distills human-scene interactions from pretrained video generators and reconstructs them with differentiable rendering, avoiding paired 3D scene and MoCap training data for unseen environments.
- [Video is Worth a Thousand Images: Exploring the Latest Trends in Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.18688): Surveys long-video generation strategies, datasets, metrics, GAN and diffusion foundations, and the extra planning, story, and spatiotemporal consistency requirements beyond short-clip synthesis.
- [ModelGrow: Continual Text-to-Video Pre-training with Model Expansion and Language Understanding Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.18966): Studies continual T2V pretraining by expanding model capacity and replacing weaker text encoders with LLM-based encoders to improve detailed prompt understanding under limited compute.
- [MAKIMA: Tuning-free Multi-Attribute Open-domain Video Editing via Mask-Guided Attention Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.19978): Performs tuning-free multi-attribute video editing by reusing inversion attention and features, modulating masked self- and cross-attention, and propagating consistent features from edited keyframes.
- [Open-Sora: Democratizing Efficient Video Production for All](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.20404): Releases an open video generator with STDiT decoupled spatial-temporal attention, a highly compressive 3D autoencoder, and training, inference, data-preparation code and weights for up to 15-second 720p synthesis.
- [Video Decomposition Prior: Editing Videos Layer by Layer](https://proceedings.iclr.cc/paper_files/paper/2024/hash/7a8a348d9c874a33ca2fdc259723592f-Abstract-Conference.html): Decomposes a video into editable layers so appearance or object edits can be applied per layer and recomposed with better temporal consistency.
- [Robust Blind Video Watermarking Based on Ring Tensor and BCH Coding](https://doi.org/10.1109/jiot.2024.3453960): Embeds blind video watermarks with a ring-tensor representation and BCH error-correction coding, targeting robustness and ownership traceability under video distortions.
- [TIA2V: Video generation conditioned on triple modalities of text-image-audio](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.eswa.2024.126278): Conditions video synthesis jointly on text, image, and audio inputs so semantic prompts, visual appearance, and sound cues steer generation together.
- [Resource Allocation for Video Diffusion Task Offloading in Cloud-Edge Networks: A Deep Active Inference Approach](https://doi.org/10.1109/globecom52923.2024.10901843): Models video-diffusion task offloading in cloud-edge networks with deep active inference, making it an infrastructure efficiency reference rather than a new generator architecture.
- [The Application of AI Video Generation Technology in Virtual Reality (VR) and Augmented Reality (AR)](https://doi.org/10.1109/aidlnn65358.2024.00035): Discusses AI video generation for VR and AR content creation, mainly as an application-oriented reference rather than a distinct diffusion or transformer method.
- [Alignment-aware Patch-level Routing for Dynamic Video Frame Interpolation](https://bmva-archive.org.uk/bmvc/2024/papers/Paper_23/paper.pdf): Routes aligned patches dynamically during video frame interpolation, using patch-level correspondence to handle motion without processing every region identically.
- [Animatable Gaussians: Learning Pose-Dependent Gaussian Maps for High-Fidelity Human Avatar Modeling](https://doi.org/10.1109/cvpr52733.2024.01864): Learns pose-dependent Gaussian maps for human avatars so articulated body poses drive Gaussian attributes for high-fidelity animatable rendering.
- [RealMock: Crafting Realistic Animated Portraits via Dual-Driven Landmark Editing](https://doi.org/10.1109/access.2024.3487414): Uses dual-driven landmark editing to combine portrait identity with driving motion cues, producing more realistic animated portrait videos.
- [Multimodal Emotional Talking Face Generation Based on Action Units](https://doi.org/10.1109/tcsvt.2024.3523359): Uses facial action units as controllable emotion variables while fusing multimodal cues for emotional talking-face video generation.
- [When Does Sora Show: The Beginning of TAO to Imaginative Intelligence and Scenarios Engineering](https://doi.org/10.1109/jas.2024.124383): Analyzes Sora-like video generation through TAO and scenarios engineering, using task, action, and object structure as axes for controllable imaginative synthesis.
- [LAMP: Learn A Motion Pattern for Few-Shot Video Generation](https://doi.org/10.1109/cvpr52733.2024.00677): Learns a reusable motion pattern from a few reference videos and injects it into video generation, enabling few-shot motion customization without a large motion-specific corpus.
- [CAMEL: CAusal Motion Enhancement Tailored for Lifting Text-Driven Video Editing](https://doi.org/10.1109/cvpr52733.2024.00867): Adds causal motion enhancement to text-driven video editing so lifted edits follow coherent motion cues across frames rather than drifting frame by frame.
- [ImproveYourVideos: Architectural Improvements for Text-to-Video Generation Pipeline](https://doi.org/10.1109/access.2024.3522510): Improves text-to-video generation pipeline architecture.
- [TexAVi: Generating Stereoscopic VR Video Clips from Text Descriptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.01156): Generates stereoscopic VR clips from text by coupling text-to-video synthesis with left-right view construction for immersive depth-consistent outputs.
- [Vulnerability-Aware Deepfake Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.01184): uses vulnerability-aware spatio-temporal learning for generalizable and interpretable deepfake video detection.
- [AR4D: Autoregressive 4D Generation from Monocular Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.01722): Uses monocular video as a conditioning signal for autoregressive 4D generation, turning observed motion into dynamic 3D content rather than isolated video frames.
- [Joint Optimization for 4D Human-Scene Reconstruction in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.02158): Introduces JOSH, which jointly optimizes scene geometry, camera poses, and human motion under human-scene contact constraints for in-the-wild 4D human-scene reconstruction.
- [Brick-Diffusion: Generating Long Videos with Brick-to-Wall Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.02741): Extends short-video diffusion to arbitrary-length generation with staggered brick-to-wall latent denoising that lets segments exchange frame information across strides.
- [Star](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.02976): Uses text-to-video models for spatial-temporal augmentation in real-world video super-resolution, linking generative video priors with restoration.
- [TransPixeler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.03006): adds transparent-layer generation to text-to-video models for compositing-friendly video synthesis.
- [Through-The-Mask](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.03059): Uses mask-based motion trajectories to control image-to-video generation.
- [Motion-Aware Generative Frame Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.03699): Combines flow guidance with generative video priors to improve temporal consistency and motion stability in frame interpolation.
- [FlexCache: Flexible Approximate Cache System for Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.04012): Speeds up video diffusion inference by compressing cached features, separating object and background caches, and using a tailored replacement policy for higher throughput.
- [Disentangled Clothed Avatar Generation with Layered Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.04631): Introduces LayerAvatar, a feed-forward diffusion model over layered Gaussian UV feature planes that separates body, hair, and clothes while supporting real-time animation.
- [Progressive Growing of Video Tokenizers for Temporally Compact Latent Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.05442): Progressively trains high-temporal-compression video tokenizers from lower-compression encoders, using cross-level feature mixing to obtain compact latents without simply increasing channel capacity.
- [CamCtrl3D: Single-Image Scene Exploration with Precise 3D Camera Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.06006): Generates scene-exploration videos from one image under explicit 3D camera trajectories.
- [Videoauteur: Towards Long Narrative Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.06173): Pairs a large cooking-video dataset with a Long Narrative Video Director so long-form generation can maintain semantic event coherence and visually aligned keyframes.
- [Qffusion: Controllable Portrait Video Editing via Quadrant-Grid Attention Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.06438): Rearranges two reference images and facial conditions into quadrant-grid latents, learns joint appearance-temporal attention, and recursively propagates edits to arbitrary-length portraits.
- [BlobGEN-Vid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.07647): Uses blob video representations for compositional text-to-video generation.
- [FramePainter: Endowing Interactive Image Editing with Video Diffusion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.08225): Reuses video diffusion priors for interactive image editing, learning real-world dynamics and visual consistency from videos rather than adding a separate reference encoder on top of image diffusion.
- [LayerAnimate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.08295): Adds layer-level control for animation generation.
- [Diffusion Adversarial Post-Training for One-Step Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.08316): Applies adversarial post-training to one-step video generation.
- [DynamicFace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.08553): uses composable 3D facial priors for high-quality and consistent video face swapping.
- [FlexiClip: Locality-Preserving Free-Form Character Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.08676): Extends Bezier trajectory animation with temporal Jacobians, probability-flow ODE modeling, and flow-matching loss to preserve clipart geometry during smooth motion.
- [Ouroboros-Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.09019): Improves consistent content generation in tuning-free long video diffusion.
- [X-Dyna](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.10021): Animates human images with expressive dynamics, extending image-to-video human animation models.
- [DiffVSR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.10110): Provides a diffusion-based recipe for robust video super-resolution under complex degradations.
- [EMO2: End-Effector Guided Audio-Driven Avatar Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.10687): Generates audio-driven avatars through a two-stage design that predicts hand poses from speech before a diffusion model synthesizes synchronized facial expression and body motion.
- [GSVC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.12060): uses 2D Gaussian splatting for efficient video representation and compression, extending model-side video representation coverage.
- [Taming Teacher Forcing for Masked Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.12389): Introduces MAGI and Complete Teacher Forcing, conditioning masked frames on complete observations to bridge masked intra-frame modeling with causal long-video generation.
- [Image Motion Blur Removal in the Temporal Dimension with Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.12604): Treats single-image motion blur as temporal averaging and solves deblurring as a diffusion inverse problem using motion dynamics from a pretrained video diffusion transformer.
- [Improving Video Generation with Human Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.13918): Builds a multi-dimensional human preference dataset for modern video generators, trains reward models, and optimizes rectified-flow video generation with feedback to reduce prompt mismatch and unsmooth motion.
- [VideoShield: Regulating Diffusion-based Video Generation Models via Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.14195): Regulates diffusion-based video generation with watermarking, adding a model-side control mechanism for generated videos.
- [Nautilus: Locality-Aware Autoencoder for Scalable Mesh Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.14317): Builds a locality-aware mesh autoencoder with topology-preserving tokenization, compacting locally shared vertices for scalable artist-like mesh generation.
- [RelightVid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.16330): Builds a temporally consistent diffusion model for video relighting.
- [Separate Motion from Appearance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.16714): Separates motion customization from appearance customization in text-to-video diffusion models.
- [DiffusionRenderer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.18590): Uses video diffusion models for neural inverse and forward rendering, connecting video generation with physically grounded rendering control.
- [Diffusion Latent Beam Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.19252): Improves text-to-video alignment at inference time by searching diffusion latents rather than retraining the generator.
- [Consistent Video Colorization via Palette Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.19331): Uses Stable Video Diffusion with a palette-based color guider that supplies a unified color context across clips for vivid, temporally stable video colorization.
- [OmniHuman-1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.01061): A one-stage human-animation model that scales Diffusion Transformer training with mixed motion-related conditions.
- [VidSketch: Hand-drawn Sketch-Driven Video Generation with Diffusion Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.01101): Accepts any number of hand-drawn sketches plus text prompts and uses level-based sketch control with TempSpatial Attention for coherent sketch-driven animation.
- [BVINet: Unlocking Blind Video Inpainting With Zero Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.01181): Jointly predicts corrupted-region masks and fills them in an end-to-end blind video inpainting network, trained with a consistency loss and synthetic plus real corrupted videos.
- [HuViDPO:Enhancing Video Generation through Direct Preference Optimization for Human-Centric Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.01690): Applies DPO to text-to-video with small action-wise human preference datasets, first-frame conditioning, and SparseCausal Attention for more aligned human-centric videos.
- [Dual-IPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.02088): Applies dual-iterative preference optimization to text-to-video generation.
- [MotionLab: Unified Human Motion Generation and Editing via the Motion-Condition-Motion Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.02358): Unifies human motion generation and editing with a Motion-Condition-Motion formulation and rectified-flow mapping from source motion plus conditions to target motion.
- [Controllable Video Generation with Provable Disentanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.02690): Adds CoVoGAN, a plug-in GAN module that disentangles static and dynamic latents with minimal-change and sufficient-change constraints for identifiable video control.
- [FreqPrior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.03496): Improves video diffusion by filtering Gaussian noise in frequency space to preserve motion dynamics and visual details.
- [DC-VSR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.03502): Uses video diffusion priors for spatially and temporally consistent video super-resolution, adding a restoration-oriented diffusion model line.
- [MotionCanvas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.04299): Supports cinematic shot design through controllable image-to-video generation.
- [On-device Sora: Enabling Training-Free Diffusion-based Text-to-Video Generation for Mobile Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.04363): Enables training-free diffusion-based text-to-video generation on mobile devices.
- [Fast Video Generation with Sliding Tile Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.04507): Replaces expensive full 3D attention in video DiTs with hardware-aware sliding tile attention, exploiting local spatiotemporal attention concentration for faster inference.
- [AuraFusion360: Augmented Unseen Region Alignment for Reference-based 360° Unbounded Scene Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.05176): Inpaints 360-degree Gaussian-splatting scenes with depth-aware unseen masks, adaptive guided depth diffusion, and SDEdit detail enhancement, plus the 360-USID benchmark dataset.
- [FlashVideo: Flowing Fidelity to Detail for Efficient High-Resolution Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.05179): Splits high-resolution video generation into prompt-faithful low-resolution synthesis followed by flow-matching super-resolution that restores fine details efficiently.
- [Fg-T2M++: LLMs-Augmented Fine-Grained Text Driven Human Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.05534): Augments fine-grained text-driven human motion generation with large language models.
- [AI-driven Sign Language Generation with Non-manual Markers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.05661): Adds non-manual markers to sign-language video generation, improving control over facial and body cues beyond hand motion alone.
- [VFX Creator: Animated Visual Effect Generation with Controllable Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.05979): Releases Open-VFX with masks and timestamps and trains a Video DiT with spatial-temporal LoRA, mask control, and tokenized start-end timing for controllable effects.
- [CustomVideoX: 3D Reference Attention Driven Dynamic Adaptation for Zero-Shot Customized Video Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.06527): Introduces 3D reference attention for zero-shot personalized video diffusion transformers.
- [History-Guided Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.06764): Conditions video diffusion on historical context so generated videos preserve temporal continuity and dynamics.
- [Lumina-Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.06782): Extends Next-DiT to video with multi-scale spatiotemporal modeling, adapting a diffusion-transformer image backbone into a flexible text-to-video synthesis framework.
- [Lotus: Creating Short Videos From Long Videos With Abstractive and Extractive Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.07096): Generates a short-form script and speech, matches long-video clips to the narration, then lets creators add extractive clips through automated selection and an editing interface.
- [Contextual Gesture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.07239): Generates co-speech gesture video from context-aware gesture representations, extending human-centric video generation beyond faces and lip motion.
- [Enhance-A-Video: Better Generated Video for Free](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.07508): Introduces a training-free approach to enhance the coherence and quality of DiT-based generated videos, named Enhance-A-Video. The core idea is enhancing the cross-frame correlations based on non-diagonal temporal attention distributions.
- [VidCRAFT3: Camera, Object, and Lighting Control for Image-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.07531): Unifies camera motion, object motion, and lighting control for image-to-video generation through point-cloud reconstruction, trajectory-to-flow encoding, and triple-attention fusion.
- [Dynamic Sparsity in Large-Scale Video DiT Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.07590): Applies dynamic sparsity to large-scale Video DiT training, improving efficiency for video generation backbones.
- [Magic 1-For-1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.07701): Generates one-minute video clips within one minute. Core idea: make long-video synthesis practical by aligning generation duration with wall-clock runtime.
- [AnyCharV: Bootstrap Controllable Character Video Generation with Fine-to-Coarse Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.08189): Trains a pose-guided character video base model, then self-boosts with generated videos and coarse-mask replacement to preserve source-character details in target scenes.
- [BEAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.08297): Bridges physically based rendering and Gaussian modeling for relightable volumetric video, adding a physically grounded video-representation route.
- [Light-a-Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.08590): Performs training-free video relighting with progressive light fusion, expanding controllable video-editing coverage.
- [Step-Video-T2V](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.10248): A 30B text-to-video foundation model with deep-compression Video-VAE, bilingual text encoders, 3D full-attention DiT flow matching, and Video-DPO post-training.
- [SkyReels-A1: Expressive Portrait Animation in Video Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.10841): Builds an expressive portrait-animation model in video diffusion transformers.
- [MaskFlow: Discrete Flows For Flexible and Efficient Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.11234): Uses discrete flows for flexible and efficient long-video generation, adding a nonstandard generative modeling route for long temporal synthesis.
- [TextOCVP: Object-Centric Video Prediction with Language Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.11655): Introduces TextOCVP, to address these limitations, introduces TextOCVP, an object-centric model for video prediction guided by textual descriptions. the approach jointly models object dynamics and interactions while incorporating textual guidance, enabling accurate and controllable predictions.
- [MVTokenFlow: High-quality 4D Content Generation using Multiview Token Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.11697): Introduces MVTokenFlow for high-quality 4D content creation from monocular videos.
- [DLFR-VAE: Dynamic Latent Frame Rate VAE for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.11897): Proposes the Dynamic Latent Frame Rate VAE (DLFR-VAE), a training-free paradigm that can make use of adaptive temporal compression in latent space. While existing video generative models apply fixed compression rates via pretrained VAE, we observe that real-world video content exhibits substantial temporal non-uniformity, with high-motion segments contain.
- [MALT Diffusion: Memory-Augmented Latent Transformers for Any-Length Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.12632): Adds memory-augmented latent transformers for any-length video generation.
- [LLMPopcorn: An Empirical Study of LLMs as Assistants for Popular Micro-video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.12945): Exploring these questions, we show that advanced LLMs like DeepSeek-V3 can generate micro-videos with popularity rivaling human content.
- [AV-Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.13133): transforms text into audio-visual human-like interaction sequences.
- [MotionMatcher: Motion Customization of Text-to-Video Diffusion Models via Motion Feature Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.13234): Fine-tunes T2V diffusion by matching high-level spatiotemporal motion features from a pretrained model, avoiding pixel reconstruction leakage from reference videos.
- [Dynamic Concepts Personalization from Single Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.14844): Introduces Set-and-Sequence, a novel framework for personalizing Diffusion Transformers (DiTs)-based generative video models with dynamic concepts. the approach imposes a spatio-temporal weight space within an architecture that does not explicitly separate spatial and temporal features.
- [LAVID](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.14994): Uses an agentic LVLM-based framework to reason over artifacts and detect diffusion-generated videos with interpretable evidence.
- [Hardware-Friendly Static Quantization Method for Video Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.15077): Proposes a novel method for the post-training quantization of OpenSoraopensora, a Video Diffusion Transformer, without relying on dynamic quantization techniques. Our approach employs static quantization, achieving video quality comparable to FP16 and dynamically quantized ViDiT-Q methods, as measured by CLIP, and VQA metrics.
- [AnyTop: Character Animation Diffusion with Any Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.17327): Uses diffusion to animate characters with arbitrary topology, extending animation generation beyond fixed-template rigs.
- [X-Dancer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.17414): Generates expressive human dance videos from music, adding an audio-conditioned human-motion line to video generation.
- [FLAP: Fully-controllable Audio-driven Portrait Video Generation through 3D head conditioned diffusion model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.19455): Conditions portrait diffusion on explicit 3D head pose and expression parameters, allowing independent control of audio-driven speech, head rotation, and eye blinking.
- [Lighting-Controllable Portrait Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.19894): Builds a relightable monocular portrait-animation video diffusion model, adding lighting control to portrait video synthesis.
- [Mobius: Text to Seamless Looping Video Generation via Latent Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.20307): Repurposes pretrained latent video diffusion for seamless text-to-loop generation by connecting start and end noise and shifting frame latents during denoising.
- [InsTaG: Learning Personalized 3D Talking Head from Few-Second Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.20387): Combines a lightweight person-specific 3D Gaussian synthesizer with universal motion priors, enabling fast personalized 3D talking-head adaptation from only a few seconds of video.
- [Training-Free Adaptive Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.21079): Uses adaptive sparse attention for efficient long video generation without retraining, adding an inference-efficiency method for video diffusion models.
- [Raccoon: Multi-stage Diffusion Training with Coarse-to-Fine Curating Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.21314): Couples the CFC-VIDS-1M coarse-to-fine curated dataset with a transformer using decoupled spatial-temporal attention and a progressive four-stage training recipe.
- [FaceShot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.00740): animates arbitrary characters, extending portrait and character video generation.
- [Extrapolating and Decoupling Image-to-Video Generation Models: Motion Modeling is Easier Than You Think](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.00948): Decouples and extrapolates image-to-video diffusion motion modeling for stronger controlled motion.
- [VideoHandles: Editing 3D Object Compositions in Videos Using Video Generative Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.01107): We propose as a method for editing 3D object compositions in videos of static scenes with camera motion.
- [Vid2Avatar-Pro: Authentic Avatar from Videos in the Wild via Universal Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.01610): Uses a universal prior over multi-view clothed human captures and expressive 3D Gaussians to recover photorealistic animatable avatars from monocular in-the-wild videos.
- [KeyFace: Expressive Audio-Driven Facial Animation for Long Sequences via KeyFrame Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.01715): Uses a two-stage diffusion pipeline that first generates low-frame-rate audio-conditioned keyframes and then interpolates them, adding continuous emotion and non-speech vocalization handling.
- [DualDiff+: Dual-Branch Diffusion for High-Fidelity Video Generation with Reward Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.03689): Uses DualDiff, a dual-branch conditional diffusion model with Occupancy Ray-shape Sampling and foreground-aware masks for controllable multi-view driving-scene video generation.
- [Progressively Deformable 2D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.05600): represents video at arbitrary resolutions through progressively deformable 2D Gaussian splatting.
- [GSV3D: Gaussian Splatting-Based Geometric Distillation With Stable Video Diffusion for Single-Image 3D Object Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.06136): Distills SV3D latent outputs through a Gaussian Splatting decoder, enforcing explicit multi-view geometry while retaining 2D diffusion diversity for single-image 3D generation.
- [Text2Story: Advancing Video Storytelling with Text Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.06310): Blends scene and action prompts with bidirectional time-weighted latent mixing, dynamics-informed prompt weighting, and semantic action representations for training-free long-form storytelling.
- [LightMotion: A Light and Tuning-free Method for Simulating Camera Motion in Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.06508): Provides a tuning-free latent-space method for camera-motion control in video generation, simulating panning, zooming, and rotation without extra depth estimation or model fine-tuning.
