- [Motion Anything: Any to Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.06955): Generalizes motion generation from diverse conditions, adding a broad motion-synthesis route for video and animation models.
- [PersonaBooth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.07390): personalizes text-to-motion generation so motion synthesis can preserve subject-specific style.
- [ObjectMover: Generative Object Movement with Video Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.08037): Generates object motion using video priors, supporting controllable object movement in generated videos.
- [RFLAV: Rolling Flow matching for infinite Audio Video generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.08307): Presents R-FLAV, a novel transformer-based architecture that addresses all the key challenges of AV generation. We explore three distinct cross modality interaction modules, with our lightweight temporal fusion module emerging as the most effective and computationally efficient approach for aligning audio and visual modalities.
- [AnyMoLe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.08417): uses video diffusion models for character-agnostic motion in-betweening, reducing dependence on character-specific motion datasets.
- [Versatile Multimodal Controls for Whole-Body Talking Human Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.08714): Builds VersaAnimator with audio-driven 3D motion tokens, text-controlled body actions, a token-to-pose translator, and multimodal video diffusion for whole-body talking humans.
- [SwapAnyone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.09154): Generates consistent and realistic person-swap video synthesis across arbitrary videos.
- [WonderVerse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.09160): Uses video generative priors, controllable scene extension, and inconsistency detection to generate coherent 3D environments.
- [TPDiff: Temporal Pyramid Video Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.09566): Progressively raises frame rate across diffusion stages and trains with stage-wise probability-flow ODE partitioning, cutting training cost and accelerating inference.
- [V2M4: 4D Mesh Animation Reconstruction from a Single Monocular Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.09631): Generates usable 4D mesh animation assets from one monocular video through camera search, mesh reposing, condition optimization, registration, and global texture refinement.
- [Open-Sora 2.0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.09642): Reports a cost-controlled training stack for a commercial-level open video generator, emphasizing data curation, model scaling, and training decisions that fit a roughly $200k budget.
- [VideoMerge: Towards Training-free Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.09926): Extends pretrained video diffusion to longer clips without retraining by merging multiple fixed-size denoising windows instead of asking the model to extrapolate a single noise tensor.
- [Cosh-DiT: Co-Speech Gesture Video Synthesis via Hybrid Audio-Visual Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.09942): Proposes Cosh-DiT, a Co-speech gesture video system with hybrid Diffusion Transformers that perform audio-to-motion and motion-to-video synthesis using discrete and continuous diffusion modeling, respectively.
- [A Self-supervised Motion Representation for Portrait Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.10096): Proposes Semantic Latent Motion (SeMo), a compact and expressive motion representation.
- [DreamInsert: Zero-Shot Image-to-Video Object Insertion from A Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.10342): We propose DreamInsert, which achieves Image-to-Video Object Insertion in a training-free manner for the first time. By incorporating the trajectory of the object into consideration, DreamInsert can predict the unseen object movement, fuse it harmoniously with the background video, and generate the desired video seamlessly.
- [LHM: Large Animatable Human Reconstruction Model from a Single Image in Seconds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.10625): Infers animatable 3D Gaussian human avatars from a single image in seconds with a multimodal transformer that fuses body positional and image features.
- [NIL: No-data Imitation Learning by Leveraging Pre-trained Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.10626): Uses pretrained video diffusion models as demonstration generators for no-data imitation learning across humanoid, quadruped, animal, and unconventional morphologies.
- [V2Edit: Versatile Video Diffusion Editor for Videos and 3D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.10634): Addressing the critical challenge of balancing original content preservation with editing task fulfillment, our approach employs a progressive strategy that decomposes complex editing tasks into a sequence of simpler subtasks.
- [VRMDiff: Text-Guided Video Referring Matting Generation of Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.10678): Treats referring video matting as alpha-matte generation with video diffusion, adds a Latent-Constructive loss, and introduces a 10,000-video captioned matte dataset.
- [Error Analyses of Auto-Regressive Video Diffusion Models: A Unified Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.10704): Analyzes history forgetting and temporal degradation in autoregressive video diffusion with Meta-ARVDM, linking long-horizon errors to conditional mutual information and metric limits.
- [EmoDiffusion: Enhancing Emotional 3D Facial Animation with Latent Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.11028): Disentangles emotional information from speech in a latent diffusion model so 3D facial animation can express emotion while preserving speech synchronization.
- [Cross-Modal Learning for Music-to-Music-Video Description Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.11190): Builds a music-to-music-video description pipeline with Music4All-derived multimodal training data and fine-tuned multimodal models, turning music features into prompts for downstream video generation.
- [LLaVA-MLB: Mitigating and Leveraging Attention Bias for Training-Free Video LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.11205): Compresses and re-expands video tokens using LLM attention, mitigating positional bias with Gridded Attention Pooling and leveraging it through a Visual Summarization Tail.
- [RASA: Replace Anyone, Say Anything - A Training-Free Framework for Audio-Driven and Universal Portrait Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.11571): Edits portrait appearance and lip motion without training by using Unified Animation Control, source inversion latents, visual shape control, audio speech control, and inter-frame control.
- [Frame-wise Conditioning Adaptation for Fine-Tuning Diffusion Models in Text-to-Video Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.12953): Adapts pretrained text-to-video diffusion to text-video prediction by conditioning on initial frames frame by frame rather than relying only on text-to-image style LoRA.
- [SyncDiff: Diffusion-Based Talking Head Synthesis with Bottlenecked Temporal Visual Prior for Improved Synchronization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.13371): Adds a bottlenecked temporal visual prior to diffusion talking-head synthesis, improving lip-speech synchronization while retaining diffusion-level image fidelity.
- [Fast Autoregressive Video Generation with Diagonal Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.14070): Introduces Diagonal Decoding (DiagD), a training-free inference acceleration algorithm for autoregressively pre-trained models that exploits spatial and temporal correlations in videos. the method generates tokens along diagonal paths in the spatial-temporal token grid.
- [PC-Talk: Precise Facial Animation Control for Audio-Driven Talking Face Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.14295): Controls lip-audio alignment and emotion through implicit keypoint deformations, including word-level speaking style, lip scale, emotion intensity, and region-wise emotion mixing.
- [Comp-Attn: Present-and-Align Attention for Compositional Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.14428): Adds present-and-align attention for compositional text-to-video generation. Core idea: separately enforce subject presence and relation alignment so multi-subject prompts are not reduced to visually plausible but semantically incomplete videos.
- [Stable Virtual Camera: Generative View Synthesis with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.14489): Introduces Seva, a generalist diffusion model for novel-view video synthesis that accepts arbitrary input views and target cameras while preserving temporal smoothness.
- [MusicInfuser: Making Video Diffusion Listen and Dance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.14505): Selects adaptable layers in pretrained T2V diffusion with a guidance-inspired influence criterion, aligning dance-video generation to music on small datasets without motion data.
- [Temporal-Consistent Video Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.14863): Uses pretrained diffusion models for temporally consistent video restoration, extending generative video restoration coverage.
- [Temporal Regularization Makes Your Video Generator Stronger](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.15417): Temporal quality is a critical aspect of video generation, as it ensures consistent motion and realistic dynamics across frames. However, achieving high temporal coherence and diversity remains challenging.
- [Sparse and Flexible Keyjoint Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.15557): controls motion synthesis through sparse, flexible keyjoint conditions.
- [Zero-1-to-A](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.15851): Uses video diffusion to turn one image into animatable head avatars, adding a zero-shot portrait-animation model line.
- [VideoRFSplat: Direct Scene-Level Text-to-3D Gaussian Splatting Generation with Flexible Pose and Multi-View Joint Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.15855): Generates scene-level text-to-3D Gaussian splats with flexible poses by jointly modeling multi-view constraints rather than treating each rendered view independently.
- [Scenemi](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.16289): Performs motion in-betweening for human-scene interactions, strengthening controllable motion synthesis in scene-aware video generation.
- [SV4D 2.0: Enhancing Spatio-Temporal Consistency in Multi-View Video Diffusion for High-Quality 4D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.16396): Improves spatio-temporal consistency in multi-view video diffusion for 4D generation, bridging video synthesis and dynamic 3D assets.
- [MagicMotion: Controllable Video Generation with Dense-to-Sparse Trajectory Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.16421): Controls video generation with dense-to-sparse trajectory guidance, letting users specify motion paths while the model fills in coherent video dynamics.
- [Think-Then-React](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.16451): generates unconstrained human reactions by first modeling the observed action and then producing a plausible response motion.
- [A Recipe for Generating 3D Worlds from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.16611): Turns a single image into a navigable 3D world by combining generative scene completion with geometry-aware refinement for world-scale content creation.
- [Enabling Versatile Controls for Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.16983): Adds versatile control mechanisms for video diffusion, expanding controllable video synthesis beyond fixed conditioning modes.
- [Generating, Fast and Slow: Scalable Parallel Video Generation with Video Interface Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.17539): Adds Video Interface Networks to diffusion transformers so local video chunks can denoise in parallel while exchanging global semantic context.
- [InstructVEdit: A Holistic Approach for Instructional Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.17641): Provides a full-cycle instructional video-editing recipe with curated edit pairs, two architecture changes for temporal consistency, and iterative refinement on real-world data.
- [TransAnimate: Taming Layer Diffusion to Generate RGBA Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.17934): Adapts layer diffusion to generate RGBA videos, producing transparent foreground animations that are ready for compositing workflows.
- [OmnimatteZero](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.18033): Uses pretrained video diffusion models for training-free omnimatte extraction, extending video-generation coverage toward foreground-layer control and editing.
- [LongDiff: Training-Free Long Video Generation in One Go](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.18150): Extends short video diffusion models to long video generation without retraining.
- [Resource-Efficient Motion Control for Video Generation via Dynamic Mask Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.18386): Controls video generation with foreground mask motion sequences, first-frame sharing, and autoregressive extension, reducing training data needs while preserving object consistency.
- [Teller: Real-Time Streaming Audio-Driven Portrait Animation with Autoregressive Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.18429): Streams audio-driven portrait animation with autoregressive motion generation so speech-aligned facial motion can be produced in real time.
- [EvAnimate: Event-conditioned Image-to-Video Generation for Human Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.18552): Conditions human image animation on event-camera motion cues, using their high temporal resolution and blur robustness to drive image-to-video diffusion under challenging motion and lighting.
- [AMD-Hummingbird](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.18559): Introduces an efficient text-to-video model, extending the model-side coverage of lightweight or faster video synthesis systems.
- [HunyuanPortrait: Implicit Condition Control for Enhanced Portrait Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.18860): Controls portrait animation through implicit conditioning, improving expression and motion transfer without relying only on explicit pose inputs.
- [Target-Aware Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.18950): We present a target-aware video diffusion model that generates videos from an input image, in which an actor interacts with a specified target while performing a desired action. The target is defined by a segmentation mask, and the action is described through a text prompt.
- [EfficientMT: Efficient Temporal Adaptation for Motion Transfer in Text-To-Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.19369): Trains on synthetic paired motion-transfer samples, repurposes the T2V backbone for temporal extraction, and uses a scaler plus temporal integration to transfer motion without test-time optimization.
- [AccVideo: Accelerating Video Diffusion Model with Synthetic Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.19462): Accelerates video diffusion through synthetic denoising trajectories and few-step guidance.
- [Dance Like a Chicken: Low-Rank Stylization for Human Motion Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.19557): Uses LoRA-MDM to adapt a motion diffusion prior from a few style examples, preserving the motion manifold while enabling style-conditioned text motion generation and blending.
- [AudCast: Audio-Driven Human Video Generation by Cascaded Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.19824): Uses cascaded diffusion transformers for audio-driven human video generation.
- [TokenHSI: Unified Synthesis of Physical Human-Scene Interactions through Task Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.19901): Tokenizes physical human-scene interaction tasks so a unified synthesis model can generate contact-aware human motion in scene context.
- [AvatarArtist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.19906): Builds open-domain 4D avatarization from a portrait image using generative priors for style-diverse animated avatars.
- [EGVD: Event-Guided Video Diffusion Model for Physically Realistic Large-Motion Frame Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.20268): Combines stable video diffusion priors with event-camera signals through a multimodal motion-condition generator, selective finetuning, and real plus simulated event data.
- [Wan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.20314): An open video foundation-model suite with a diffusion-transformer backbone, new VAE, scalable pretraining, and curated data across text-to-video, image-to-video, editing, and personal-video tasks.
- [VPO: Aligning Text-to-Video Generation Models with Prompt Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.20491): Trains a prompt optimizer with safety-aligned SFT data plus text-level and video-level preference feedback, improving harmlessness, intent preservation, and video quality across T2V models.
- [FB-4D: Spatial-Temporal Coherent Dynamic 3D Content Generation with Feature Banks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.20784): Introduces FB-4D, inspired by recent findings that pretrained diffusion features capture rich correspondences, introduces FB-4D, a novel 4D generation framework that integrates a Feature Bank mechanism to enhance both spatial and temporal consistency in generated frames.
- [Free4D: Tuning-Free 4D Scene Generation with Spatial-Temporal Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.20785): Introduces Free4D, present Free4D, a novel tuning-free framework for 4D scene generation from a single image. 3) To lift these generated observations into consistent 4D representation.
- [ChatAnyone: Stylized Real-time Portrait Video Generation with Hierarchical Motion Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.21144): Uses hierarchical audio-driven motion diffusion for stylized facial and body motion, then injects explicit hand controls and face refinement for real-time upper-body portraits.
- [Uni4D: Unifying Visual Foundation Models for 4D Modeling from a Single Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.21761): Unifies visual foundation models for 4D modeling from a single video, combining reconstruction and temporal modeling for dynamic scene assets.
- [StyleMotif: Multi-Modal Motion Stylization using Style-Content Cross Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.21775): Aligns a style encoder with a pretrained multimodal model and fuses style and content latents so text, motion, image, video, or audio references can stylize motion generation.
- [Detecting Localized Deepfake Manipulations Using Action Unit-Guided Video Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.22121): Detects subtle localized deepfake edits by using action-unit-guided video representations targeted at manipulated facial features such as brows, eyes, and mouth motion.
- [Enhance Generation Quality of Flow Matching V2A Model via Multi-Step CoT-Like Guidance and Combined Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.22200): Improves video-to-audio flow matching with Chain-of-Perform guidance and combined preference optimization for better semantic and temporal audio-video alignment.
- [SketchVideo: Sketch-based Video Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.23284): Uses sketches as direct spatial controls for video generation and editing, enabling users to steer both object layout and temporal changes.
- [VLIPP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.23368): Uses VLM planning as a physical prior for image-to-video generation, improving physically plausible dynamics.
- [JointTuner: Appearance-Motion Adaptive Joint Training for Customized Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.23951): Introduces JointTuner, recent advancements in customized video generation have led to significant improvements in the simultaneous adaptation of appearance and motion. Typically, decoupling the appearance and motion training, prior methods often introduce concept interference.
- [HumanDreamer: Generating Controllable Human-Motion Videos via Decoupled Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.24026): Decouples controllable human-motion video generation into separate motion and appearance factors, improving pose control while preserving subject identity.
- [Hierarchical Flow Diffusion for Efficient Frame Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.00380): Models bilateral optical flow with a hierarchical diffusion process and then synthesizes frames with a flow-guided image generator for faster diffusion-based interpolation.
- [Monocular and Generalizable Gaussian Talking Head Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.00665): Trains MGGTalk from monocular data so 3D Gaussian talking-head animation generalizes to unseen identities without per-person retraining or multi-view capture.
- [Articulated Kinematics Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.01204): Distills articulated character motion from video diffusion models into skeleton-based animation control.
- [DreamActor-M1: Holistic, Expressive and Robust Human Image Animation with Hybrid Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.01724): Combines complementary guidance signals for holistic human image animation, targeting expressive motion, robustness, and identity preservation.
- [Generating 360° Video is What You Need For a 3D Scene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.02045): We introduce a practical and scalable solution that uses 360° video as an intermediate scene representation, capturing the full-scene context and ensuring consistent visual content throughout the generation.
- [OmniCam: Unified Multimodal Video Generation via Camera Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.02312): Unifies multimodal video generation with camera-control conditioning, strengthening controllable text-to-video and image-to-video synthesis.
- [OmniTalker: One-shot Real-time Text-Driven Talking Audio-Video Generation With Multimodal Style Mimicking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.02433): Presents OmniTalker, a unified framework that jointly generates synchronized talking audio-video content from input text while emulating the speaking and facial movement styles of the target identity, including speech characteristics, head motion, and facial dynamics.
- [MG-MotionLLM: A Unified Framework for Motion Comprehension and Generation across Multiple Granularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.02478): Unifies motion comprehension and generation at multiple granularities, connecting video-motion understanding with generative motion control.
- [Audio-Visual Controlled Video Diffusion with Masked Selective State Spaces Modeling for Natural Talking Head Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.02542): Uses masked selective state-space modeling to fuse audio and visual controls for natural talking-head video diffusion.
- [VIP Video Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.03041): builds a video inpainting pipeline for real-world human removal.
- [How I Warped Your Noise: a Temporally-Correlated Noise Prior for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.03072): Adds a temporally correlated noise prior for diffusion models, making noise design a controllable route for temporally coherent video generation and restoration.
- [Ditailistener: Controllable High Fidelity Listener Video Generation with Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.04010): Generates controllable high-fidelity listener videos with diffusion.
- [Multi-Identity Human Image Animation with Structural Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.04126): Uses Structural Video Diffusion with identity-specific embeddings and 3D-aware dynamics to animate multiple interacting people while preserving each identity.
- [Video4DGen: Enhancing Video and 4D Generation through Mutual Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.04153): Introduces Video4DGen, a novel framework that excels in generating 4D representations from single or multiple generated videos as well as generating 4D-guided videos. This framework is pivotal for creating high-fidelity virtual contents that maintain both spatial and temporal coherence.
- [Time-adaptive Video Frame Interpolation based on Residual Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.05402): Uses residual diffusion for time-adaptive video frame interpolation, targeting intermediate-frame synthesis at arbitrary temporal positions rather than fixed-rate interpolation only.
- [Towards efficient real-time video motion transfer via generative time series modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.05537): Frames real-time motion transfer as generative time-series modeling, emphasizing low-latency temporal dynamics for transferring motion into generated video.
- [CamC2V: Context-aware Controllable Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.06022): Extends image-to-video diffusion with context-aware camera control, preserving scene content while adding trajectory constraints that ordinary I2V animation lacks.
- [RagMe: Retrieval Augmented Video Generation for Enhanced Motion Realism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.06672): Conditions pretrained text-to-video diffusion on retrieved demonstration videos, grounding object motion realism with minimal finetuning rather than only prompt conditioning.
- [EIDT-V: Exploiting Intersections in Diffusion Trajectories for Model-Agnostic, Zero-Shot, Training-Free Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.06861): Uses intersections in latent diffusion trajectories plus LLM-generated frame prompts and CLIP attention masks to make image diffusion models produce zero-shot text-to-video outputs without architecture changes.
- [SIGMAN: Scaling 3D Human Gaussian Generation with Millions of Assets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.06982): Scales 3D human Gaussian generation with millions of assets, making data scale itself a driver for higher-fidelity digital-human synthesis.
- [GenDoP: Auto-regressive Camera Trajectory Generation as a Director of Photography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.07083): Generates camera trajectories autoregressively for director-of-photography-style video control.
- [STeP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.07549): uses spatiotemporal diffusion priors as a plug-and-play framework for reconstructing scientific videos from sparse measurements.
- [Beyond the Frame](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.07940): generates 360-degree panoramic videos from ordinary perspective videos.
- [TokenMotion: Decoupled Motion Control via Token Disentanglement for Human-centric Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.08181): Disentangles motion-related tokens from appearance tokens in human-centric video generation, enabling motion control without overwriting identity and scene content.
- [Discriminator-Free Direct Preference Optimization for Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.08542): Builds video DPO pairs from real clips as wins and edited corrupted clips as losses, avoiding human or discriminator scoring while teaching diffusion models to reject temporal artifacts.
- [Seaweed-7B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.08685): Describes a 7B video foundation model trained from scratch with a cost-efficient recipe, highlighting architecture, data, and resource choices under 665k H100 GPU-hours.
- [MotionDreamer Motion Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.08959): Uses a localized generative masked Transformer for one-to-many motion synthesis, strengthening controllable motion generation.
- [Zero-Shot Personalized Camera Motion Control for Image-to-Video Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.09472): Controls personalized camera motion for image-to-video synthesis without task-specific training.
- [GaussVideoDreamer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.10001): Combines video diffusion with inconsistency-aware Gaussian splatting for 3D scene generation, linking video priors with spatially coherent scene synthesis.
- [Aligning Anime Video Generation with Human Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.10044): Aligns anime video generation with human feedback and anime-specific rewards.
- [Analysis of Attention in Video Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.10317): Analyzes attention behavior in video diffusion transformers, supporting model-side diagnosis of video generation systems.
- [OmniVDiff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.10825): Builds an omni-controllable video diffusion model for generation and understanding. Core idea: unify multiple control signals so video synthesis can be steered by richer visual and semantic conditions.
- [Taming Consistency Distillation for Accelerated Human Image Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.11143): Proposes DanceLCM with segmented consistency distillation, a lightweight auxiliary head, motion-focused loss, and facial-fidelity feature injection for 2-4 step human animation.
- [UniAnimate-DiT: Human Image Animation with Large-Scale Video Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.11289): Trains a large-scale video diffusion transformer for human image animation, extending UniAnimate-style control with DiT capacity.
- [VideoPanda: Video Panoramic Diffusion with Multi-view Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.11389): Synthesizes 360-degree panoramic videos using multi-view attention in video diffusion.
- [EgoExo-Gen: Ego-centric Video Prediction by Watching Exo-centric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.11732): Predicts egocentric video by learning from exocentric videos, adding a viewpoint-transfer route for first-person video generation.
- [Understanding Attention in Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.12027): analyzes attention mechanisms in video diffusion models, adding a diagnostic lens for temporal generation behavior.
- [Modular-Cam: Modular Dynamic Camera-view Video Generation with LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.12048): Uses an LLM to decompose complex text-to-video prompts into modular scenes and dynamic camera views, improving camera-aware video generation.
- [VGDFR: Diffusion-based Video Generation with Dynamic Latent Frame Rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.12259): Uses dynamic latent frame rates in diffusion-based video generation to better allocate temporal modeling effort.
- [UniPhys: Unified Planner and Controller with Diffusion for Flexible Physics-Based Character Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.12540): Combines diffusion with a unified planner and controller for flexible physics-based character control rather than only visual motion imitation.
- [Supervising 3D Talking Head Avatars with Analysis-by-Audio-Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.13386): Supervises 3D talking-head avatars through an analysis-by-audio-synthesis loop, using audio reconstruction signals to improve speech-driven facial motion.
- [Visual Prompting for One-shot Controllable Video Editing without Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.14335): Propagates a user-edited first frame through a video without DDIM inversion, using visual prompting to keep source content consistent during one-shot controllable editing.
- [Turbo2K: Towards Ultra-Efficient and High-Quality 2K Video Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.14470): Targets efficient 2K video synthesis by redesigning the generation path for high quality under lower compute rather than simply scaling resolution.
- [FlowLoss: Dynamic Flow-Conditioned Loss Strategy for Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.14535): Directly matches optical-flow fields between generated and ground-truth videos, using noise-aware denoising-step weights to improve motion stability and early convergence in diffusion training.
- [DyST-XL: Dynamic Layout Planning and Content Control for Compositional Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.15032): Proposes DyST-XL, a training-free framework that enhances off-the-shelf text-to-video models (e.g., CogVideoX-5B) through frame-aware control. DyST-XL integrates three key innovations: (1) A Dynamic Layout Planner that leverages large language models (LLMs) to parse input prompts into entity-attribute graphs and generates physics-aware keyframe layouts, w.
- [Versatile Compressed Video Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.15380): provides a plug-and-play compressed-video enhancement method, extending video restoration models.
- [DiTPainter: Efficient Video Inpainting with Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.15661): Presents DiTPainter, an end-to-end video inpainting model based on Diffusion Transformer (DiT).
- [Efficient Temporal Consistency in Diffusion-Based Video Editing with Adaptor Modules: A Theoretical Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.16016): Analyzes adapter modules for DDIM-based video editing, proving differentiability, Lipschitz-bounded gradients, descent convergence, and DDIM-inversion stability under a temporal-consistency loss.
- [BadVideo: Stealthy Backdoor Attack Against Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.16907): Introduces a backdoor attack for text-to-video generators that hides harmful targets through spatiotemporal composition and dynamic element transfer in otherwise plausible videos.
- [We'll Fix it in Post](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.17180): Improves text-to-video generation with neuro-symbolic feedback after generation.
- [MV-Crafter: An Intelligent System for Music-Guided Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.17267): Generates music videos through script generation, video generation, and music-video synchronization modules that align visual rhythm and style to the source music.
- [Dynamic Camera Poses and Where to Find Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.17788): Builds and uses dynamic camera-pose resources to improve camera-motion conditioning for generated videos and view-synthesis pipelines.
- [NoiseController: Towards Consistent Multi-View Video Generation via Noise Decomposition and Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.18448): Decomposes initial noise into scene-level and object-level components, coordinates noise across frames, and jointly denoises for consistent multi-view video generation.
- [Audio-Driven Talking Face Video Generation with Joint Uncertainty Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.18810): Adds joint uncertainty learning to audio-driven talking-face video generation, improving visual reliability and quality under varying speech inputs.
- [CineVerse: Consistent Keyframe Synthesis for Cinematic Scene Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.19894): Creates a cinematic scene dataset and trains a two-stage pipeline where an LLM plans setting, characters, and shots before a finetuned T2I model synthesizes consistent keyframes.
- [Advance Fake Video Detection via Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.20669): Extends ViT-based fake-image detection to video by aggregating frame embeddings and temporal evidence for more general AI-generated video detection.
- [HoloTime: Taming Video Diffusion Models for Panoramic 4D Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.21650): Combines video diffusion with panoramic 4D scene reconstruction for immersive scene generation.
- [Eye2Eye: A Simple Approach for Monocular-to-Stereo Video Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.00135): Converts monocular video to stereo video with a simple synthesis approach.
- [Model See Model Do: Speech-Driven Facial Animation with Style Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.01319): Conditions a latent diffusion facial-animation model on a reference style clip, transferring nuanced performance style in addition to lip synchronization and basic emotion.
- [VIDSTAMP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.01406): Adds temporally aware watermarking for ownership and integrity in video diffusion models, extending video-generation safety and provenance controls.
- [DualReal: Adaptive Joint Training for Lossless Identity-Motion Fusion in Video Customization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.02192): Jointly adapts identity and motion for customized text-to-video generation.
- [PAHA: Parts-Aware Audio-Driven Human Animation with Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.03603): Adds parts-aware reweighting and parts-consistency enhancement to diffusion-based audio-driven upper-body animation for fine-grained foreground and audio-motion control.
- [ReactDance: Progressive-Granular Representation for Long-Term Coherent Reactive Dance Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.05589): Uses a hierarchical latent representation inside diffusion to generate reactive dance, handling fine-grained dancer interactions and long-term temporal coherence.
- [Jailbreaking the Text-to-Video Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.06679): Formalizes T2V jailbreaks as discrete prompt optimization, jointly maximizing safety-filter bypass and semantic consistency through iterative prompt-variant search.
- [DAPE: Dual-Stage Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning for Consistent Video Editing with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.07057): Uses parameter-efficient fine-tuning for more consistent diffusion-based video editing.
- [EventDiff: A Unified and Efficient Diffusion Model Framework for Event-based Video Frame Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.08235): Uses an event-frame hybrid autoencoder with spatial-temporal cross attention so diffusion-based interpolation can fuse event streams and frames without explicit flow warping.
- [BiECVC: Gated Diversification of Bidirectional Contexts for Learned Video Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.09193): Improves learned bidirectional video compression with local feature reuse, decoded-motion alignment, linear-attention nonlocal context, and bidirectional context gating.
- [Dyadic Mamba: Long-term Dyadic Human Motion Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.09827): Replaces transformer positional schemes with state-space modeling and concatenated person streams to synthesize arbitrary-length dyadic human motion from text.
- [Aquarius: A Family of Industry-Level Video Generation Models for Marketing Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.10584): Reports an industry-level Aquarius model family tuned for marketing video scenarios, emphasizing production-quality controllability and deployment constraints.
- [QVGen: Pushing the Limit of Quantized Video Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.11497): Pushes quantized video generative models toward lower-bit inference while preserving synthesis quality, making compression a model-design axis.
- [From Shots to Stories: LLM-Assisted Video Editing with Unified Language Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.12237): Converts video shots into L-Storyboard language representations and uses StoryFlow to stabilize LLM reasoning for shot classification, selection, and ordering in video editing.
- [Safe-Sora: Safe Text-to-Video Generation via Graphical Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.12667): Adds graphical watermarking for safer text-to-video generation.
- [Hearing from Silence: Reasoning Audio Descriptions from Silent Videos via Vision-Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.13062): Defines silent-video audio-description reasoning and uses VLMs to infer missing sound descriptions that can condition downstream video-to-audio generation.
- [VSA: Faster Video Diffusion with Trainable Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.13389): Learns trainable sparse attention for video diffusion so the model can accelerate denoising without replacing the generator backbone.
- [FinePhys: Fine-grained Human Action Generation by Explicitly Incorporating Physical Laws for Effective Skeletal Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.13437): Adds physics-based skeletal guidance to diffusion video generation so fine-grained human actions such as gymnastics motions remain more plausible over time.
- [DraftAttention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.14708): Uses low-resolution attention guidance to accelerate video diffusion, adding a task-specific inference-efficiency method for video generation.
- [FastCar: Cache Attentive Replay for Fast Auto-Regressive Video Generation on the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.14709): Uses cache-attentive replay to run autoregressive video generation on edge devices, reusing past computation while limiting quality loss.
- [Intentional Gesture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.15197): Generates speech-aligned gestures that reflect intended communication goals, expanding controllable human video generation.
- [Interspatial Attention for Efficient 4D Human Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.15800): Introduces a new interspatial attention (ISA) mechanism as a scalable building block for modern diffusion transformer (DiT)--based video generation models.
- [Interactive Semantic Human-Body Video Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.16152): uses a generative approach with interactive semantics for human-body video compression.
- [DOVE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.16239): Builds an efficient one-step diffusion model for real-world video super-resolution. Core idea: move video restoration toward faster generative enhancement while preserving temporal consistency.
- [Direct3D-S2: Gigascale 3D Generation Made Easy with Spatial Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.17412): Scales 3D generation with spatial sparse attention, reducing the cost of gigascale scene synthesis while preserving structural detail.
- [T2VUnlearning: A Concept Erasing Method for Text-to-Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.17550): Proposes unlearning-based concept erasing as a solution. First, we adopt negatively-guided velocity prediction fine-tuning and enhance it with prompt augmentation to ensure robustness against prompts refined by large language models (LLMs).
- [Model Already Knows the Best Noise: Bayesian Active Noise Selection via Attention in Video Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.17561): To address this, we propose ANSE (Active Noise Selection for Generation), a model-aware framework that selects high-quality seeds by quantifying attention-based uncertainty.
- [DanceTogether! Identity-Preserving Multi-Person Interactive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.18078): Presents DanceTogether, an identity-preserving multi-person interactive video-generation model with paired skating and human-motion data.
- [VORTA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.18809): Routes sparse attention for efficient video diffusion. Core idea: reduce unnecessary attention computation while keeping the temporal and spatial dependencies needed for video synthesis.
- [Sparse VideoGen2: Accelerate Video Generation with Sparse Attention via Semantic-Aware Permutation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.18875): Uses semantic-aware sparse attention and token permutation to accelerate video-generation diffusion transformers.
- [REGen: Multimodal Retrieval-Embedded Generation for Long-to-Short Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.18880): Introduces REGen, propose a novel retrieval-embedded generation framework that allows a large language model to quote multimodal resources while maintaining a coherent narrative.
- [Geometry-guided Online 3D Video Synthesis with Multi-View Temporal Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.18932): Uses geometry guidance and multi-view temporal constraints for online 3D video synthesis, aiming to keep generated views consistent over camera motion and time.
- [SRDiffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.19151): Accelerates video diffusion inference through sketching-rendering cooperation, adding a video-specific inference-efficiency method.
- [Dynamic-I2V: Exploring Image-to-Video Generation Models via Multimodal LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.19901): Integrates an MLLM condition encoder with a diffusion transformer so image and text inputs capture object-action relations, improving motion controllability and temporal coherence.
- [AniCrafter: Customizing Realistic Human-Centric Animation via Avatar-Background Conditioning in Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.20255): Introduces AniCrafter, a diffusion-based human-centric animation model that can seamlessly integrate and animate a given character into open-domain dynamic backgrounds while following given human motion sequences. Experimental results demonstrate the superior performance of the method.
- [MotionPro: A Precise Motion Controller for Image-to-Video Generation*](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.20287): Adds a precise motion controller for image-to-video generation, making user-specified motion a first-class conditioning signal.
- [Unified Text-Image-to-Video Generation: A Training-Free Approach to Flexible Visual Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.20629): Unifies text and image conditioning at inference time, giving pretrained video generators flexible visual guidance without an additional training stage.
- [Frame-Level Captions for Long Video Generation with Complex Multi Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.20827): Introduces a new approach to solve these problems. First, introduces a novel way to annotate datasets at the frame-level, providing detailed text guidance needed for making complex, multi-scene long videos.
- [RainFusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.21036): Exploits multi-dimensional visual redundancy to accelerate video generation, adding an adaptive efficiency method for generative video models.
- [EF-VI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.21205): enhances end-frame injection for video inbetweening.
- [Frame In-N-Out: Unbounded Controllable Image-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.21491): Introduces Frame In-N-Out, to support this task, introduces a new dataset that is curated semi-automatically, an efficient identity-preserving motion-controllable video Diffusion Transformer architecture, and a comprehensive evaluation protocol targeting this task.
- [Any-to-Bokeh: One-Step Video Bokeh via Multi-Plane Image Guided Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.21593): Uses multi-plane-image-guided diffusion for one-step video bokeh.
- [Learning World Models for Interactive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.21996): Adds action conditioning and autoregressive memory to image-to-video models for interactive video generation.
- [PanoWan: Lifting Diffusion Video Generation Models to 360° with Latitude/Longitude-aware Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.22016): Lifts diffusion video generators to 360-degree synthesis with latitude- and longitude-aware mechanisms tailored to panoramic geometry.
- [Q-VDiT: Towards Accurate Quantization and Distillation of Video-Generation Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.22167): Studies quantization and distillation for accurate low-bit deployment of video-generation diffusion transformers.
- [StateSpaceDiffuser: Bringing Long Context to Diffusion World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.22246): Introduces StateSpaceDiffuser, where a diffusion model is enabled to perform long-context tasks by integrating features from a state-space model, representing the entire interaction history.
- [MOVi: Training-free Text-conditioned Multi-Object Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.22980): Introduces a novel training-free approach for multi-object video generation that leverages the open world knowledge of diffusion models and large language models (LLMs).
- [MMGT: Motion Mask Guided Two-Stage Network for Co-Speech Gesture Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.23120): Uses motion masks in a two-stage network for co-speech gesture video generation, separating gesture-region guidance from final video synthesis.
- [Zero-to-Hero](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.23134): uses zero-shot initialization for reference-based video appearance editing.
- [MiniMax-Remover: Taming Bad Noise Helps Video Object Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.24873): Improves video object removal by controlling harmful noise behavior, adding a robustness-oriented route for video editing models.
- [Foresight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.00329): Reuses layers adaptively to speed text-to-video generation while maintaining quality, adding a task-specific acceleration mechanism.
- [Video Signature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.00652): Embeds implicit watermarks into video diffusion models while preserving visual quality and temporal consistency.
- [SkyReels-Audio: Omni Audio-Conditioned Talking Portraits in Video Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.00830): Presents SkyReels-Audio, a unified framework for synthesizing high-fidelity and temporally coherent talking portrait videos. Built upon pretrained video diffusion transformers, our framework supports infinite-length generation and editing, while enabling diverse and controllable conditioning through multimodal inputs.
- [Temporal In-Context Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.00996): Uses temporal reasoning and in-context fine-tuning to control video diffusion models across varied editing and generation conditions.
- [MoCA-Video: Motion-Aware Concept Alignment for Consistent Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.01004): Edits videos without retraining by localizing objects in frozen diffusion latents, applying momentum correction for semantic shifts, and smoothing artifacts with a gamma residual module.
- [DeepVerse: 4D Autoregressive Video Generation as a World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.01103): Frames 4D autoregressive video generation as a world model, emphasizing temporally persistent scene dynamics rather than short independent clips.
- [FlowMo: Variance-Based Flow Guidance for Coherent Motion in Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.01144): Derives appearance-debiased temporal signals from consecutive-frame latents and guides sampling by reducing patch-wise temporal variance, improving motion coherence without retraining.
- [Playing with Transformer at 30+ FPS via Next-Frame Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.01380): Plays transformer-based generation at 30 FPS with next-frame diffusion, targeting efficient real-time video synthesis.
- [DiffuseSlide: Training-Free High Frame Rate Video Generation Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.01454): Converts low-FPS keyframe videos into high-FPS outputs with a training-free diffusion pipeline that uses noise reinjection and sliding denoising to reduce flicker.
- [OmniV2V: Versatile Video Generation and Editing via Dynamic Content Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.01801): Manipulates dynamic content for versatile video-to-video generation and editing, separating what changes from what should remain temporally consistent.
- [Physics-Guided Motion Loss for Video Generation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.02244): Adds an explicit physics-guided motion loss to video generation training so synthesized motion better follows physically plausible dynamics.
- [SViMo: Synchronized Diffusion for Video and Motion Generation in Hand-object Interaction Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.02444): Recognizing that visual appearance and motion patterns share fundamental physical laws in the real world, we propose a novel framework that combines visual priors and dynamic constraints within a synchronized diffusion process to generate the HOI video and motion simultaneously.
- [LumosFlow: Motion-Guided Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.02497): Adds explicit motion guidance to hierarchical long-video generation, using large-motion key frames, latent optical flow, and refinement to reduce repetition and unnatural transitions.
- [Dual-Expert Consistency Model for Efficient and High-Quality Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.03123): Introduces a dual-expert consistency distillation approach for efficient, temporally consistent video generation.
- [CamCloneMaster: Enabling Reference-based Camera Control for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.03140): Enables reference-based camera control for video generation, transferring camera motion cues from examples instead of relying only on text prompts.
- [IllumiCraft](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.03150): Unifies geometry and illumination diffusion for controllable video generation, linking scene structure with lighting control.
- [FullDiT2: Efficient In-Context Conditioning for Video Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.04213): Based on these insights, we propose FullDiT2, an efficient in-context conditioning framework for general controllability in both video generation and editing tasks, which innovates from two key perspectives.
- [Voyager: Long-Range and World-Consistent Video Diffusion for Explorable 3D Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.04225): Generates world-consistent 3D point-cloud sequences from one image and a user camera path, avoiding separate structure-from-motion or multi-view stereo reconstruction.
- [LayerFlow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.04228): unifies layer-aware video generation so foreground and background structure can be controlled explicitly.
- [Follow-Your-Creation: Empowering 4D Creation through Video Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.04590): Uses video inpainting as the engine for 4D creation, letting edited or completed video regions propagate into dynamic content assets.
- [FPSAttention: Training-Aware FP8 and Sparsity Co-Design for Fast Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.04648): Co-designs FP8 attention and sparsity with training awareness to speed video diffusion while retaining generation quality.
- [FlowDirector: Training-Free Flow Steering for Precise Text-to-Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.05046): Steers text-to-video editing without training by manipulating flow fields, giving precise motion-aware edits at inference time.
- [Astraea: A Token-wise Acceleration Framework for Video Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.05096): Searches token-reduction schedules and uses lightweight token selection with GPU-friendly sparse attention to accelerate video diffusion transformers.
- [Follow-Your-Motion: Video Motion Transfer via Efficient Spatial-Temporal Decoupled Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.05207): To tackle these issues, we propose Follow-Your-Motion, an efficient two-stage video motion transfer framework that finetunes a powerful video diffusion transformer to synthesize complex motion.
- [ContentV: Efficient Training of Video Generation Models with Limited Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.05343): Presents ContentV, an 8B-parameter text-to-video model that achieves state-of-the-art performance (85.14 on VBench) after training on 256 x 64GB Neural Processing Units (NPUs) for merely four weeks.
- [LLIA - Enabling Low-Latency Interactive Avatars: Real-Time Audio-Driven Portrait Video Generation with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.05806): Combines variable-length audio-to-video diffusion, consistency-model training, quantization, pipeline parallelism, state labels, and expression controls for low-latency interactive avatars.
- [Restereo: Diffusion stereo video generation and restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.06023): Uses diffusion modeling for stereo video generation and restoration, extending video generation coverage to stereoscopic consistency.
- [A Systematic Investigation on Deep Learning-Based Omnidirectional Image and Video Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.06710): Studies deep omnidirectional image and video super-resolution methods, making 360-degree restoration and temporal upsampling coverage explicit for generative video enhancement.
- [TV-LiVE: Training-Free, Text-Guided Video Editing via Layer Informed Vitality Exploitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.07205): Adds a training-free text-guided video-editing method based on layer-informed signals.
- [From Generation to Generalization: Emergent Few-Shot Learning in Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.07280): Introduces From Generation to Generalization, to probe the extent of this internal knowledge, introduces a few-shot fine-tuning framework that repurposes VDMs for new tasks using only a handful of examples. the method transforms each task into a visual transition.
- [NOVA3D: Normal Aligned Video Diffusion Model for Single Image to 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.07698): Fine-tunes a video diffusion prior for single-image 3D generation with Geometry-Temporal Alignment attention and de-conflict geometry fusion to improve multi-view consistency.
- [Consistent Video Editing as Flow-Driven Image-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.07713): Decomposes editing into first-frame editing plus flow-driven image-to-video generation, simulating shape-aligned pseudo flows to preserve non-rigid temporal consistency.
- [PolyVivid: Vivid Multi-Subject Video Generation with Cross-Modal Interaction and Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.07848): Proposes PolyVivid, a multi-subject video customization framework that enables flexible and identity-consistent generation.
- [Improving Motion in Image-to-Video Models via Adaptive Low-Pass Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.08456): To address this, we propose adaptive low-pass guidance (ALG), a simple training-free fix to the I2V model sampling procedure to generate more dynamic videos without compromising per-frame image quality.
- [LiftVSR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.08529): Lifts image diffusion priors into video super-resolution with hybrid temporal modeling, extending diffusion-based video restoration coverage.
- [Cross-Frame Representation Alignment for Fine-Tuning Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.09229): Extends REPA to video with CREPA, aligning frame hidden states to neighboring-frame visual features during LoRA finetuning of CogVideoX-5B and Hunyuan Video.
- [Autoregressive Adversarial Post-Training for Real-Time Interactive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.09350): Post-trains a latent video diffusion model into a one-frame-at-a-time generator with adversarial student forcing and a KV-cache-aware architecture for real-time streaming control.
- [Synthetic Human Action Video Data Generation with Pose Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.09411): Generates pose-transfer synthetic human-action videos with controllable 3D Gaussian avatars and RANDOM People identities to scale few-shot action-recognition data.
- [VideoMat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.09665): Extracts physically based rendering materials from video diffusion models. Core idea: reuse video diffusion priors to infer material properties needed for controllable rendering and generation.
- [AnimateAnyMesh: A Feed-Forward 4D Foundation Model for Text-Driven Universal Mesh Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.09982): Introduces a feed-forward 4D foundation model for text-driven mesh animation, producing universal animated meshes without per-instance optimization.
- [PlayerOne: Egocentric World Simulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.09995): Builds an egocentric world simulator, connecting first-person video generation with interactive world-model behavior.
- [Multimodal Cinematic Video Synthesis Using Text-to-Image and Audio Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.10005): Advances in generative artificial intelligence have altered multimedia creation, allowing for automatic cinematic video synthesis from text inputs. This work describes a method for creating 60-second cinematic movies incorporating Stable Diffusion for high-fidelity image synthesis, GPT-2 for narrative structuring, and a hybrid audio pipeline using gTTS an.
- [EmbodiedGen: Towards a Generative 3D World Engine for Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.10600): Builds a generative 3D world engine for embodied intelligence, connecting video-world generation with interactive spatial simulation.
- [iDiT-HOI: Inpainting-based Hand Object Interaction Reenactment via Video Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.12847): Reenacts hand-object interaction through an inpainting-based video diffusion transformer that handles occlusion, object variation, and unseen humans or objects.
- [EraserDiT: Fast Video Inpainting with Diffusion Transformer Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.12853): We propose a Circular Position-Shift strategy to further enhance long-term temporal consistency during the inference stage.
- [VideoPDE: Unified Generative PDE Solving via Video Inpainting Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.13754): Recasts forward and inverse PDE solving as spatiotemporal inpainting, using a pixel-space video diffusion transformer with arbitrary known-data masks for generative simulation.
- [VideoMAR: Autoregressive Video Generatio with Continuous Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.14168): Introduces VideoMAR, a concise and efficient decoder-only autoregressive image-to-video model with continuous tokens, composing temporal frame-by-frame and spatial masked generation. To this end, introduces the temporal short-to-long curriculum learning.
- [Causally Steered Diffusion for Automated Video Counterfactual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.14404): Guides latent video editing with causal-graph text prompts and a VLM-based textual loss so counterfactual edits preserve causal dependencies in generated videos.
- [Toward Rich Video Human-Motion2D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.14428): Introduces Toward Rich Video Human-Motion2D Generation, to address these limitations, we first introduce a new large-scale rich video human motion 2D dataset (Motion2D-Video-150K) comprising 150,000 video sequences. Building upon this dataset.
- [Hunyuan3D 2.1: From Images to High-Fidelity 3D Assets with Production-Ready PBR Material](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.15442): Scales image-to-3D asset generation with production-ready materials, fitting the 3D generation part of the video-generation page.
- [One-Step Diffusion for Video Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.15591): Uses one-step diffusion for detail-rich, temporally consistent video super-resolution.
- [UniRelight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.15673): Relights images and videos by jointly estimating albedo and synthesizing new illumination in one video-diffusion pass, trained on synthetic multi-illumination data and automatically labeled real videos.
- [Advanced Sign Language Video Generation with Compressed and Quantized Multi-Condition Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.15980): Uses compressed and quantized multi-condition tokenization for sign-language video generation, improving identity-preserving pose and hand control.
- [FastInit: Fast Noise Initialization for Temporally Consistent Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.16119): Designs fast noise initialization for video diffusion so early latent noise is temporally coherent, reducing flicker without changing the full generator.
- [Seeing What Matters: Generalizable AI-generated Video Detection with Forensic-Oriented Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.16802): Introduces Seeing What Matters, then, introduces a novel forensic-oriented data augmentation strategy based on the wavelet decomposition and replace specific frequency-related bands to drive the model to exploit more relevant forensic cues.
- [DreamCube: 3D Panorama Generation via Multi-plane Synchronization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.17206): Generates 3D panoramas through multi-plane synchronization, relevant to 3D scene generation.
- [ViDAR: Video Diffusion-Aware 4D Reconstruction From Monocular Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.18792): Uses personalized video diffusion to synthesize pseudo multi-view supervision for monocular 4D Gaussian reconstruction, with diffusion-aware loss and camera-pose optimization.
- [Training-Free Motion Customization for Distilled Video Generators with Adaptive Test-Time Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.19348): Enables training-free motion customization for distilled video generators through adaptive test-time distillation. Core idea: use a slower teacher model only where guidance is needed so fast video generators can follow reference motion more faithfully.
- [AnimaX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.19851): Combines video and pose diffusion to animate inanimate 3D objects, broadening video-generation coverage for 3D-aware animation.
- [DFVEdit: Conditional Delta Flow Vector for Zero-shot Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.20967): To alleviate this problem, we present DFVEdit, an efficient zero-shot video editing method tailored for Video DiTs. DFVEdit eliminates the need for both attention modification and fine-tuning by directly operating on clean latents via flow transformation.
- [Video Virtual Try-on with Conditional Diffusion Transformer Inpainter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.21270): Uses a conditional diffusion-transformer inpainter for video virtual try-on, preserving garment identity and human motion while filling clothing regions across time.
- [Shape-for-Motion: Precise and Consistent Video Editing With 3D Proxy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.22432): Builds a time-consistent 3D proxy mesh for the target object, propagates single-frame edits through time, and feeds geometry and texture renderings to a decoupled video diffusion editor.
- [OmniVCus: Feedforward Subject-driven Video Customization with Multimodal Control Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.23361): Customizes subjects in a feed-forward video generator with multimodal control conditions, reducing the need for per-subject optimization.
- [SynMotion: Semantic-Visual Adaptation for Motion Customized Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.23690): Proposes SynMotion, a new motion-customized video generation model that jointly leverages semantic guidance and visual adaptation.
- [VMoBA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.23858): Uses mixture-of-block attention to make video diffusion transformers more efficient. Core idea: route attention over block-level patterns so long video synthesis can preserve temporal structure with lower compute.
- [MotionGPT3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.24086): Treats human motion as a second modality, extending multimodal generation and representation around motion sequences.
- [Filter-And-Refine: A MLLM Based Cascade System for Industrial-Scale Video Content Moderation](https://doi.org/10.18653/v1/2025.acl-industry.62): Deploys a cascade moderation architecture in which multimodal LLMs first filter candidate videos and then refine decisions for industrial-scale safety review.
- [Populate-A-Scene: Affordance-Aware Human Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.00334): Fine-tunes a text-to-video model to insert people into a single scene image according to action prompts, using cross-attention affordance signals instead of boxes or poses.
- [ARIG: Autoregressive Interactive Head Generation for Real-Time Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.00472): Generates interactive talking heads autoregressively for real-time conversation, focusing on low-latency head motion and response synchronization.
- [AI-Generated Video Detection via Perceptual Straightening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.00583): Detects AI-generated videos through perceptual straightening, using perceptual-space regularities as forensic signals instead of raw artifact templates.
- [Geometry-aware 4D Video Generation for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.01099): Introduces Geometry-aware 4D Video Generation for Robot Manipulation, to address this, introduces a 4D video generation model that enforces multi-view 3D consistency of generated videos by supervising the model with cross-view pointmap alignment during training. Through this geom.
- [FixTalk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.01390): Reduces identity leakage and rendering artifacts in talking-head video generation under extreme cases.
- [LongAnimation: Long Animation Generation with Dynamic Global-Local Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.01945): Introduces LongAnimation, specifically, introduces LongAnimation, a novel framework, which mainly includes a SketchDiT, a Dynamic Global-Local Memory (DGLM), and a Color Consistency Reward.
- [Pixel-Wise Temporal Frequency Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.02398): uses pixel-wise temporal frequency cues for deepfake video detection, extending generated-video authenticity models.
- [Canonswap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.02691): uses canonical-space modulation for high-fidelity and consistent video face swapping.
- [From Long Videos to Engaging Clips: A Human-Inspired Video Editing Framework with Multimodal Narrative Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.02790): Builds HIVE, an automatic editing pipeline that uses MLLMs for character extraction, dialogue analysis, narrative summaries, scene segmentation, highlight selection, and pruning.
- [Runtime-Adaptive Caching for Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.02860): accelerates video diffusion without training by adapting cache use at runtime.
- [RefTok: Reference-Based Tokenization for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.02862): Encodes frame sets conditioned on an unquantized reference frame, preserving object appearance and motion continuity while improving video tokenizer compression and generation metrics.
- [StreamDiT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.03745): Trains flow matching with a moving buffer, mixed buffered-frame partitions, adaLN DiT window attention, and segment-wise distillation to stream 512p text-to-video at 16 FPS.
- [EchoMimicV3: 1.3B Parameters are All You Need for Unified Multi-Modal and Multi-Task Human Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.03905): Unifies multi-task human animation with Soup-of-Tasks masks, Soup-of-Modals cross-attention, phase-aware modality allocation, negative DPO, and phase-aware CFG.
- [Can Video LLMs Refuse to Answer? Alignment for Answerability in Video Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.04976): Aligns Video-LLMs to judge whether a question is answerable from the video and refuse out-of-scope prompts, using a paired dataset pipeline and answerability metrics.
- [Tora2: Motion and Appearance Customized Diffusion Transformer for Multi-Entity Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.05963): Adds a decoupled personalization extractor, gated self-attention over trajectories, text, and visual identity, plus contrastive motion-identity loss for multi-entity customized DiT generation.
- [Democratizing High-Fidelity Co-Speech Gesture Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.06812): Uses 2D full-body skeletons as an audio-to-video bridge, predicting skeleton motion from fine-grained audio and a reference image, and releases the 405-hour CSG-405 dataset.
- [Physics-Grounded Motion Forecasting via Equation Discovery for Trajectory-Guided Image-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.06830): Introduces Physics-Grounded Motion Forecasting via Equation Discovery for Trajectory-Guided Image-to-Video Generation, to address this issue, introduces a novel framework that integrates symbolic regression (SR).
- [Martian World Model: Controllable Video Synthesis with Physically Accurate 3D Reconstructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.07978): Combines controllable video synthesis with physically accurate 3D reconstruction, relevant to world-consistent video generation.
- [M2DAO-Talker: Harmonizing Multi-granular Motion Decoupling and Alternating Optimization for Talking-head Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.08307): Decouples non-rigid oral and facial motion from rigid head motion, using deformation masks, camera parameters, and alternating optimization to reduce talking-head artifacts.
- [Show and Polish](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.10293): Restores face videos with reference-guided identity preservation. Core idea: use a reference identity signal so video restoration improves quality without drifting from the target person over time.
- [MOSPA: Human Motion Generation Driven by Spatial Audio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.11949): Introduces MOSPA, to bridge this gap and enable high-quality modeling of human movements in response to spatial audio, introduces the first comprehensive Spatial Audio-Driven Human Motion (SAM) dataset, which contains diverse and high-quality spatial audio and motion data.
- [HairShifter: Consistent and High-Fidelity Video Hair Transfer via Anchor-Guided Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.12758): Combines image hair transfer with an Anchor Frame plus Animation pipeline and multi-scale gated SPADE decoder to preserve hairstyle fidelity and temporal coherence.
- [Think-Before-Draw: Decomposing Emotion Semantics & Fine-Grained Controllable Expressive Talking Head Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.12761): Uses chain-of-thought emotion parsing to turn text emotion labels into facial-muscle movement descriptions, then applies progressive guidance denoising for controllable expressions.
- [LoViC: Efficient Long Video Generation with Context Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.12952): Compresses context for efficient long-video generation, reducing memory pressure while preserving long-range temporal information.
- [FantasyPortrait: Enhancing Multi-Character Portrait Animation with Expression-Augmented Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.12956): Adds expression-augmented diffusion transformers for multi-character portrait animation, improving character-specific expression transfer and consistency.
- [Leveraging Pre-Trained Visual Models for AI-Generated Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.13224): Adapts pretrained visual models for generic AI-generated video detection beyond face-centric deepfakes, targeting broader synthetic-content forensics.
- [Taming Diffusion Transformer for Efficient Mobile Video Generation in Seconds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.13343): Proposes a series of novel optimizations to significantly accelerate video generation and enable practical deployment on mobile platforms. First, we employ a highly compressed variational autoencoder (VAE) to reduce the dimensionality of the input data without sacrificing visual quality.
- [Encapsulated Composition of Text-to-Image and Text-to-Video Models for High-Quality Video Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.13753): Introduces EVS, a training-free Encapsulated Video Synthesizer that composes T2I and T2V models to enhance both visual fidelity.
- [StableAnimator++: Overcoming Pose Misalignment and Face Distortion for Human Image Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.15064): Improves human image animation with learnable pose alignment and identity preservation.
- [Dream, Lift, Animate: From Single Images to Animatable Gaussian Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.15979): Dreams multi-view images with video diffusion, lifts them to 3D Gaussians, maps them into pose-aware UV space, and animates single-image avatars through body-driven deformation.
- [Enhancing Scene Transition Awareness in Video Generation via Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.18046): Post-trains on the Transition-Aware Video dataset of multi-scene clips so text-to-video models infer when prompts require scene changes while preserving image quality.
- [SemGes: Semantics-Aware Co-Speech Gesture Generation Using Semantic Coherence and Relevance Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.19359): Generates co-speech gestures with semantic coherence and relevance learning, improving avatar motion beyond rhythm-only gesture synthesis.
- [Back to the Features: DINO as a Foundation for Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.19468): Uses DINO features as a foundation for video world models, relevant to generative dynamics and simulator-style video models.
- [TransFlow: Motion Knowledge Transfer from Video Diffusion Models to Video Salient Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.19789): Transfers motion knowledge from video diffusion features into video salient-object detection, using generative temporal representations for downstream segmentation.
- [Compositional Video Synthesis by Temporal Object-Centric Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.20855): Uses temporally consistent object-centric slots for compositional video generation and editing. Core idea: model object identity and dynamics explicitly so insertion, deletion, and replacement remain coherent across frames.
- [Low-Cost Test-Time Adaptation for Robust Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.21858): Applies low-cost self-supervised test-time adaptation to video editing, improving temporal consistency and prompt robustness during inference.
- [X-NeMo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.23143): Uses disentangled latent attention for expressive neural motion reenactment in video generation.
- [TITAN-Guide: Taming Inference-Time Alignment for Guided Text-to-Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.00289): Guides text-to-video diffusion at inference with forward-gradient latent optimization from discriminative models, reducing memory versus backprop-based training-free control.
- [IN2OUT: Fine-Tuning Video Inpainting Model for Video Outpainting Using Hierarchical Discriminator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.00418): Fine-tunes a video inpainting model for outpainting with a hierarchical discriminator and local/global adversarial loss to keep extended borders coherent.
- [Semantic and Temporal Integration in Latent Diffusion Space for High-Fidelity Video Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.00471): Integrates semantic and temporal cues inside latent diffusion space for video super-resolution, targeting high-fidelity restoration with frame-to-frame consistency.
- [D3 for AI-generated video detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.00701): Uses second-order temporal features to detect AI-generated videos without detector training.
- [AudioGen-Omni: A Unified Multimodal Diffusion Transformer for Video-Synchronized Audio, Speech, and Song Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.00733): Uses a unified multimodal diffusion transformer to generate video-synchronized audio, speech, and song rather than treating audio as a post-process.
- [SpA2V: Harnessing Spatial Auditory Cues for Audio-driven Spatially-aware Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.00782): As prior methods largely ignore this factor, we present SpA2V, the first framework explicitly exploits these spatial auditory cues from audios to generate videos with high semantic and spatial correspondence.
- [MoCA: Identity-Preserving Text-to-Video Generation via Mixture of Cross Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.03034): Inserts mixture-of-cross-attention layers, hierarchical temporal pooling, and latent video perceptual loss into a DiT backbone to preserve identity across text-to-video frames.
- [Macro-from-Micro Planning for High-Quality and Parallelized Autoregressive Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.03334): Introduces Macro-from-Micro Planning for High-Quality and Parallelized Autoregressive Long Video Generation, to address these limitations, introduces a novel planning-then-populating framework centered on Macro-from-Micro Planning (MMPL) for long video generation.
- [VideoGuard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.03480): protects video content from unauthorized editing, adding a content-protection model line for video generation and editing.
- [Scaling Up Audio-Synchronized Visual Animation: An Efficient Training Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.03955): Scales audio-synchronized animation with noisy large-scale pretraining, small curated finetuning, multi-feature audio conditioning, window attention, and 1.9% added trainable parameters.
- [IDCNet: Guided Video Diffusion for Metric-Consistent RGBD Scene Generation with Precise Camera Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.04147): Jointly synthesizes RGB videos and metric depth maps with camera-image-depth training data and geometry-aware transformer blocks for precise camera-controlled scene generation.
- [LayerT2V](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.04228): unifies multi-layer representations for controllable video generation.
- [PoseGen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.05091): Uses in-context LoRA finetuning for pose-controllable long human video generation, adding a controllable human-motion video model.
- [RAP: Real-time Audio-driven Portrait Animation with Video Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.05115): Builds a real-time audio-driven portrait diffusion transformer with hybrid attention for fine audio control and a static-dynamic training and inference scheme to reduce drift.
- [X-MoGen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.05162): Unifies motion generation across humans and animals, broadening text or condition-driven motion synthesis beyond human-only templates.
- [DreamVE: Unified Instruction-based Image and Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.06080): To this end, we introduce DreamVE, a unified model for instruction-based image and video editing. Specifically, We propose a two-stage training strategy: first image editing, then video editing.
