- [SwiftVideo: A Unified Framework for Few-Step Video Generation through Trajectory-Distribution Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.06082): Proposes {SwiftVideo}, a unified and stable distillation framework that combines the advantages of trajectory-preserving and distribution-matching strategies.
- [ViPro-2: Unsupervised State Estimation via Integrated Dynamics for Guiding Video Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.06335): Adds unsupervised state estimation to video prediction by inferring symbolic dynamics from observations, avoiding reliance on a clean ground-truth initial state in ViPro.
- [FVGen: Accelerating Novel-View Synthesis with Adversarial Video Diffusion Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.06392): Distills a multi-step video-diffusion teacher into a few-step student with GAN training and softened reverse KL minimization, cutting sparse-view novel-view sampling time by more than 90%.
- [LaVieID](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.07603): uses local autoregressive diffusion transformers for identity-preserving video creation.
- [Generative Video Matting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.07905): Proposes to solve the problem from two perspectives. First, we emphasize the importance of large-scale pre-training by pursuing diverse synthetic and pseudo-labeled segmentation datasets.
- [S^2VG: 3D Stereoscopic and Spatial Video Generation via Denoising Frame Matrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.08048): Turns monocular generations into stereoscopic and spatial videos by depth-warping camera views and applying frame-matrix inpainting with dual updates for multi-view consistency.
- [Fine-grained Video Dubbing Duration Alignment with Segment Supervised Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.08550): We propose the Segment Supervised Preference Optimization (SSPO) method, which employs a segment-wise sampling strategy and fine-grained loss to mitigate duration mismatches between source and target lines.
- [RealisMotion: Decomposed Human Motion Control and Video Generation in the World Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.08588): Decomposes human video generation into subject, background, trajectory, and action controls in world space, improving controllable motion composition.
- [Yan: Foundational Interactive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.08601): We present Yan, a foundational framework for interactive video generation, covering the entire pipeline from simulation and generation to editing. Specifically, Yan comprises three core modules.
- [TaoCache: Structure-Maintained Video Generation Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.08978): Accelerates video DiTs with training-free fixed-point noise-output caching, calibrating cosine similarity and noise-delta norm ratios to skip late denoising while preserving structure.
- [Spatial-Temporal Multi-Scale Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.08991): quantizes motion at multiple spatial-temporal scales for flexible motion generation.
- [X-UniMotion: Animating Human Images with Expressive, Unified and Identity-Agnostic Motion Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.09383): We present X-UniMotion, a unified and expressive implicit latent representation for whole-body human motion, encompassing facial expressions, body poses, and hand gestures.
- [Identity-Preserving Video Generation via Mixture of Facial Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.09476): Uses a mixture of facial experts to preserve identity under large-angle face changes, adding a specialized controllability model for portrait video generation.
- [OneVAE: Joint Discrete and Continuous Optimization Helps Discrete Video VAE Train Better](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.09857): Improves discrete video VAE training by preserving continuous-VAE priors with FSQ and joint discrete-continuous optimization for stable compressed tokens.
- [SpeechForensics: Audio-Visual Speech Representation Learning for Face Forgery Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.09913): Learns audio-visual speech representations from real videos and transfers them to face-forgery detection, using speech-motion consistency as a generated-video forensic signal.
- [BLADE: Block-Sparse Attention Meets Step Distillation for Efficient Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.10774): Combines block-sparse attention with step distillation for efficient video generation.
- [TexVerse: A Universe of 3D Objects with High-Resolution Textures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.10868): Releases TexVerse, a large 3D dataset with high-resolution textures, PBR materials, rigged models, and animated assets for texture-rich generation.
- [Versatile Video Tokenization with Generative 2D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.11183): uses generative 2D Gaussian splatting as a video tokenization route for video synthesis and representation.
- [VimoRAG: Video-based Retrieval-augmented 3D Motion Generation for Motion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.12081): Adds video-based retrieval augmentation to motion LLMs for 3D motion generation, using retrieved human-motion signals and dual-alignment DPO training.
- [Adaptive Hybrid Caching for Efficient Text-to-Video Diffusion Model Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.12691): Introduces MixCache, a training-free video DiT accelerator that selects caching granularity with context-aware triggers instead of a fixed step, CFG, or block cache.
- [Lumen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.12945): Uses video generative models for consistent relighting and harmonious background replacement, extending video editing toward scene-level illumination control.
- [Compact Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.12969): Exploits structured spatio-temporal sparsity to make video generation faster while preserving motion-relevant attention structure.
- [EgoTwin: Dreaming Body and View in First Person](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.13013): Jointly generates egocentric video and human motion with a head-centric motion representation and cybernetics-inspired visual-motion interaction.
- [Precise Action-to-Video Generation Through Visual Action Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.13104): Generates precise action-to-video outputs through visual action prompts.
- [Motion2Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.13139): Transfers animations across different skeletal topologies with sparse correspondence, improving controllable character-motion generation.
- [Sketch3DVE: Sketch-based 3D-Aware Scene Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.13797): Enables sketch-driven local scene edits under large viewpoint changes by estimating point clouds and cameras, editing 3D geometry with depth maps, and propagating masks into video diffusion.
- [Vivid-VR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.14483): Distills concepts from text-to-video diffusion transformers for photorealistic video restoration, linking generative priors with restoration.
- [FakeHunter Explainable Video Forensics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.14581): uses memory-anchored multimodal reasoning for step-by-step explainable video forensics, extending deepfake and generated-video authenticity models.
- [AnchorSync: Global Consistency Optimization for Long Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.14609): Edits minute-scale videos by separating sparse anchor-frame editing from intermediate-frame interpolation, enforcing structural consistency with progressive denoising and multimodal guidance.
- [Tinker: Diffusion's Gift to 3D-Multi-View Consistent Editing From Sparse Inputs without Per-Scene Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.14811): Performs 3D multi-view consistent editing from sparse inputs without per-scene optimization.
- [VideoEraser](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.15314): Erases specified concepts from text-to-video diffusion models, adding a model-side safety and controllability method for video generation.
- [MoSA: Motion-Coherent Human Video Generation via Structure-Appearance Decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.17404): Decouples structure and appearance for human video generation, using a 3D structure transformer and dense tracking constraints to improve motion coherence.
- [Warm Chat: Diffuse Emotion-aware Interactive Talking Head Avatar with Tree-Structured Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.18337): Generates bidirectional emotion-aware talking-head avatars with a Transformer head-mask generator and an interactive talking-tree state structure for speaking, listening, and emotion transitions.
- [Wan-S2V: Audio-Driven Cinematic Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.18621): Builds an audio-driven cinematic video model on Wan for expressive character interactions, body movement, and camera work beyond speech-only animation.
- [VoxHammer: Training-Free Precise and Coherent 3D Editing in Native 3D Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.19247): Edits 3D objects directly in native latent space by reusing inversion latents and cached key-value tokens to preserve unedited regions coherently.
- [MIDAS: Multimodal Interactive Digital-humAn Synthesis via Real-time Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.19320): Introduces an autoregressive video generation framework that enables interactive multimodal control and low-latency extrapolation in a streaming manner.
- [InfinityHuman: Towards Long-Term Audio-Driven Human](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.20210): Proposes InfinityHuman, a coarse-to-fine framework that first generates audio-synchronized representations, then progressively refines them into high-resolution, long-duration videos using a pose-guided refiner.
- [3D Gaussian-Guided Object Editing for Driving Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.20471): Uses 3D Gaussian guidance for controllable object edits in driving videos, extending domain-grounded video editing beyond generic prompts.
- [EmoCAST: Emotional Talking Portrait via Emotive Text Description](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.20615): Drives talking portraits from emotive text by adding text-guided emotive attention, emotion-aware audio attention, an in-the-wild emotional dataset, and training strategies for precise expression control.
- [POSE: Phased One-Step Adversarial Equilibrium for Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.21019): To bridge this gap, we propose the Video Phased Adversarial Equilibrium (V-PAE), a distillation framework that enables high-quality, single-step video generation from large-scale video models.
- [Look Beyond: Two-Stage Scene View Generation via Panorama and Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.00843): Generates scene views through panorama and video diffusion stages.
- [Bidirectional Sparse Attention for Faster Video Diffusion Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.01085): Introduces Bidirectional, to overcome this limitation, introduces a Bidirectional Sparse Attention (BSA) framework for faster video DiT training, the first to dynamically sparsify both Queries and Key-Value pairs within 3D full attention.
- [Identity-Preserving Text-to-Video Generation via Training-Free Prompt, Image, and Guidance Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.01362): Finally, we propose ID-Aware Spatiotemporal Guidance Enhancement, utilizing unified gradients to optimize identity preservation and video quality jointly du.
- [O-DisCo-Edit: Object Distortion Control for Unified Realistic Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.01596): Introduces O-DisCo-Edit, to address this, introduces O-DisCo-Edit, a unified framework that incorporates a novel object distortion control (O-DisCo).
- [GenCompositor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.02460): performs generative video compositing with a diffusion Transformer.
- [LuxDiT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.03680): Estimates lighting with a video diffusion transformer. Core idea: use video diffusion representations to recover lighting cues that support controllable and physically grounded video generation.
- [Zero-shot 3D-Aware Trajectory-Guided image-to-video generation via Test-Time Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.06723): Uses depth-based 3D kinematic projection plus trajectory-guided test-time LoRA and latent co-adaptation to impose perspective-aware object motion without annotated trajectory training data.
- [AnyPortal: Zero-Shot Consistent Video Background Replacement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.07472): We introduce ANYPORTAL, a novel zero-shot framework for video background replacement that leverages pre-trained diffusion models. Our framework collaboratively integrates the temporal prior of video diffusion models with the relighting capabilities of image diffusion models in a zero-shot setting.
- [LINR Bridge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.07484): combines neural implicits with video diffusion priors for vector-graphic animation.
- [Multi-Feature Fusion for Video Diffusion Transformer Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.09547): Aligns self-supervised vision-encoder features to improve video diffusion transformer training, adding a representation-fusion training recipe.
- [T2Bs: Text-to-Character Blendshapes via Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.10678): Combines text-to-3D static assets with video diffusion through deformable 3D Gaussian splatting and a view-dependent deformation MLP to create registered, expressive character-head blendshapes.
- [PanoLora: Bridging Perspective and Panoramic Video Generation with LoRA Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.11092): Adapts perspective video generation to panoramic video through LoRA, supporting wider-field controllable video synthesis.
- [AvatarSync: Rethinking Talking-Head Animation through Autoregressive Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.12052): Reduces talking-head flicker with a phoneme-guided autoregressive pipeline that generates facial keyframes using causal attention and fills transitions with timestamp-aware interpolation.
- [Hunyuan3D Studio: End-to-End AI Pipeline for Game-Ready 3D Asset Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.12815): Presents an end-to-end AI pipeline for game-ready 3D asset generation, matching 3D and world-generation model coverage.
- [BWCache](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.13789): Accelerates video diffusion transformers with block-wise caching, adding a deployment-relevant inference route for video generation.
- [Lynx: Towards High-Fidelity Personalized Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.15496): We present Lynx, a high-fidelity model for personalized video synthesis from a single input image. Built on an open-source Diffusion Transformer (DiT) foundation model, Lynx introduces two lightweight adapters to ensure identity fidelity.
- [Follow-Your-Emoji-Faster: Towards Efficient, Fine-Controllable, and Expressive Freestyle Portrait Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.16630): Accelerates freestyle portrait animation with expression-aware landmark motion signals, a fine-grained facial loss, and efficiency-oriented diffusion design.
- [VidCLearn: A Continual Learning Approach for Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.16956): Applies continual-learning techniques to text-to-video generation so models can absorb new video concepts while mitigating forgetting of earlier motion and appearance knowledge.
- [VAInpaint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.17022): Builds a zero-shot video-audio inpainting framework with an LLM-driven module, extending multimodal video editing beyond visual-only restoration.
- [Stable Video-Driven Portraits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.17476): Reenacts portraits by using masked eye, nose, and mouth regions as strong motion controls, cross-identity supervision, spatiotemporal attention, and history frames for consistency.
- [From Prompt to Progression: Taming Video Diffusion Models for Seamless Attribute Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.19690): Adds frame-wise denoising guidance and a data-specific transition direction so video diffusion can generate gradual attribute changes without breaking motion dynamics.
- [CamPVG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.19979): uses epipolar-aware diffusion for camera-controlled panoramic video generation.
- [MultiSoundGen: Video-to-Audio Generation for Multi-Event Scenarios via SlowFast Contrastive Audio-Visual Pretraining and Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.19999): Improves video-to-audio generation for multi-event scenes with SlowFast contrastive audio-visual pretraining and direct preference optimization. Core idea: align semantic events, fast dynamics, and audio quality in cluttered videos rather than optimizing only generic synchronization.
- [SimDiff: Simulator-constrained Diffusion Model for Physically Plausible Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.20927): Treats simulator projection as diffusion guidance and conditions denoising on gravity, wind, and other environment parameters, avoiding repeated simulator calls while controlling physical coefficients.
- [MotionFlow: Learning Implicit Motion Flow for Complex Camera Trajectory Control in Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.21119): Introduces MotionFlow, to address this challenge, introduces a novel approach that integrates both camera and object motions by converting them into the motion of corresponding pixels.
- [Reasoning-Enhanced Domain-Adaptive Pretraining of Multimodal Large Language Models for Short Video Content Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.21486): Pretrains MLLMs for short-video moderation with captioning, VQA, and reasoning-oriented tasks to improve inappropriate-content detection across issue types.
- [ControlHair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.21541): Uses physically based video diffusion for controllable dynamic hair rendering.
- [UniVid: Unifying Vision Tasks with Pre-trained Video Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.21760): Introduces UniVid, to answer this, introduces UniVid, a framework that fine-tunes a video diffusion transformer to handle various vision tasks without task-specific modifications.
- [DiTraj](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.21839): Provides training-free trajectory control for video diffusion transformers, adding an inference-time motion-control method for generated videos.
- [Syncphony: Synchronized Audio-to-Video Generation with Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.21893): Synchronizes audio-to-video generation with motion-aware loss and audio sync guidance on a pretrained video backbone for 24 fps temporally aligned outputs.
- [Taming Flow-based I2V Models for Creative Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.21917): To this end, we propose IF-V2V, an Inversion-Free method that can adapt off-the-shelf flow-matching-based I2V models for video editing without significant computational overhead.
- [Jailbreaking on Text-to-Video Models via Scene Splitting Strategy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.22292): Demonstrates a black-box SceneSplit jailbreak that decomposes a harmful narrative into individually benign scenes, then iteratively constrains text-to-video outputs toward unsafe composites.
- [Sparse2Dense Human Video Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.23169): uses keypoints to generate dense human video and vertex predictions for compression-oriented synthesis.
- [Vid-Freeze: Protecting Images from Malicious Image-to-Video Generation via Temporal Freezing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.23279): Introduces Vid-Freeze -- a novel adversarial defense that adds imperceptible perturbations to enforce temporal freezing in generated videos. the method explicitly targets attention dynamics in I2V models to suppress motion synthesis.
- [VividFace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.23584): builds a one-step diffusion model for video face enhancement, improving efficiency in generative face restoration.
- [ReLumix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.23769): Extends image relighting to temporally coherent video relighting with video diffusion models, adding a controllable lighting-editing method for generated or edited videos.
- [Redundancy Reduction for Video Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.23980): Reduces redundancy in diffusion models for efficient video super-resolution, extending generative video restoration efficiency.
- [Autoregressive Video Generation beyond Next Frames Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.24081): Introduces VideoAR, to address this, introduces VideoAR, a unified framework that supports a spectrum of prediction units including full frames, key-detail frames, multiscale refinements, and spatiotemporal cubes.
- [NeRV-Diffusion: Diffuse Implicit Neural Representations for Video Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.24353): We present NeRV-Diffusion, an implicit latent video diffusion model that synthesizes videos via generating neural network weights. The generated weights can be rearranged as the parameters of a convolutional neural network, which forms an implicit neural representation (INR), and decodes into videos with frame indices as the input.
- [Attention Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.24899): Linearizes video diffusion transformers through attention surgery, tying efficiency to the video-generation architecture itself.
- [PanoWorld-X](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.24997): Generates explorable panoramic worlds with sphere-aware video diffusion and camera-controllable 360-degree synthesis.
- [MotionRAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.26391): adds retrieval-augmented motion references to image-to-video generation, improving motion control without relying only on the prompt.
- [Arbitrary Generative Video Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.00578): generalizes video interpolation through a generative modeling approach.
- [FreeViS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.01686): Performs training-free video stylization under inconsistent references, adding an inference-time control route for video generation.
- [TempoControl: Temporal Attention Guidance for Text-to-Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.02226): Adds temporal-attention guidance for text-to-video models, directly targeting controllable temporal dynamics in video generation.
- [VidGuard-R1: AI-Generated Video Detection and Explanation via Reasoning MLLMs and RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.02282): Trains an AI-video detector with GRPO, temporal-stability and diffusion-complexity rewards, and 140K real/fake pairs so reasoning MLLMs explain physics-grounded artifacts.
- [Learning to Generate Rigid Body Interactions with Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.02284): Introduces KineMask, a video diffusion method that uses object velocity and mask-supervised training to generate physically plausible rigid-body interactions.
- [Input-Aware Sparse Attention for Real-Time Co-Speech Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.02617): Accelerates co-speech video generation by distilling diffusion models and making attention input-aware. Core idea: reduce generation latency for audio-driven talking-video synthesis while preserving motion quality.
- [Mask2IV: Interaction-Centric Video Generation via Mask Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.03135): Introduces Mask2IV, to overcome this limitation, introduces Mask2IV, a novel framework specifically designed for interaction-centric video generation.
- [Generating Human Motion Videos using a Cascaded Text-to-Video Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.03909): Introduces CAMEO, a cascaded framework that connects text-to-motion models with conditional video diffusion to generate general human-motion videos from motion descriptions and visual conditions.
- [Character Mixing for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.05093): We introduce a framework that tackles these issues with Cross-Character Embedding (CCE), which learns identity and behavioral logic across multimodal sources, and Cross-Character Augmentation (CCA), which enriches training with synthetic co-existence and mixed-style data.
- [LightCache: Memory-Efficient, Training-Free Acceleration for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.05367): Provides a training-free memory-efficient acceleration method for video generation.
- [Controllable Audio-Visual Viewpoint Generation from 360° Spatial Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.06060): A model framework for controllable audio-visual viewpoint generation from 360-degree spatial information. Core idea: condition generation on panoramic spatial cues so off-camera events can shape viewpoint-specific video and audio.
- [ShapeGen4D: Towards High Quality 4D Shape Generation from Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.06208): Generates 4D shapes from videos with temporal attention, time-aware point sampling, 4D latent anchoring, and noise sharing for stable dynamic geometry.
- [A Bridge from Audio to Video: Phoneme-Viseme Alignment Allows Every Face to Speak Multiple Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.06612): Uses a phoneme-guided mixture-of-experts and phoneme-viseme alignment to generalize talking-face synthesis across languages with a multilingual talking-face benchmark.
- [No MoCap Needed: Post-Training Motion Diffusion Models with Reinforcement Learning using Only Textual Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.06988): Fine-tunes motion diffusion models with denoising diffusion policy optimization and a text-motion retrieval reward, adapting to unseen actions without motion-capture ground truth.
- [MV-Performer: Taming Video Diffusion Model for Faithful and Synchronized Multi-view Performer Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.07190): To maintain synchronization in the generated videos, we propose a multi-view human-centric video diffusion model that fuses information from the reference video, partial rendering, and different viewpoints.
- [TTOM: Test-Time Optimization and Memorization for Compositional Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.07940): Introduces Test-Time Optimization and Memorization (TTOM), a training-free framework that aligns VFM outputs with spatiotemporal layouts during inference for better text-image alignment.
- [Physics-Driven AI-Generated Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.08073): uses physics-driven spatiotemporal modeling to detect AI-generated videos.
- [UniMMVSR: A Unified Multi-Modal Framework for Cascaded Video Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.08143): Unifies cascaded video super-resolution across text, image, and video conditions, studying condition injection, training schemes, and data mixtures.
- [FlexTraj: Image-to-Video Generation with Flexible Point Trajectory Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.08527): We present FlexTraj, a framework for image-to-video generation with flexible point trajectory control. FlexTraj introduces a unified point-based motion representation that encodes each point with a segmentation ID, a temporally consistent trajectory ID, and an optional color channel for appearance cues, enabling both dense and sparse trajectory control.
- [X2Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.08530): Extends intrinsic-guided image diffusion to video with hybrid self-attention, masked cross-attention, recursive sampling, and the InteriorVideo dataset for material, geometry, lighting, reference-image, and text control.
- [VideoCanvas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.08555): unifies video completion from arbitrary spatiotemporal patches via in-context conditioning.
- [DEMO: Disentangled Motion Latent Flow Matching for Fine-Grained Controllable Talking Portrait Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.10650): Builds an audio-driven talking-portrait model with an orthogonalized motion autoencoder and optimal-transport flow matching for separate lip, head-pose, and eye-gaze control.
- [AdaViewPlanner: Adapting Video Diffusion Models for Viewpoint Planning in 4D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.10670): To this end, we propose a two-stage paradigm to adapt pre-trained T2V models for viewpoint prediction, in a compatible manner. First, we inject the 4D scene representation into the pre-trained T2V model via an adaptive learning branch, where the 4D scene is viewpoint-agnostic and the conditional generated video embeds the viewpoints visually.
- [Task-Specific Dual-Model Framework for Comprehensive Traffic Safety Video Description and Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.11907): Separately optimizes VideoLLaMA for temporal captioning and Qwen2.5-VL for VQA on WTS traffic videos, reducing task interference in safety analysis.
- [Time-Correlated Video Bridge Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.12453): adds time-correlated bridge matching for generative video modeling.
- [MVP4D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.12785): Generates animatable 4D avatars with multi-view portrait video diffusion. Core idea: use multi-view temporal generation to preserve identity and motion across dynamic avatar synthesis.
- [Edit-Your-Interest: Efficient Video Editing via Feature Most-Similar Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.13084): Performs zero-shot text-driven video editing with a spatiotemporal feature memory and most-similar token propagation to reduce compute while preserving temporal fidelity.
- [VIST3A: Text-to-3D by Stitching a Multi-view Reconstruction Network to a Video Generator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.13454): We introduce VIST3A, a general framework that does just that, addressing two main challenges. First, the two components must be joined in a way that preserves the rich knowledge encoded in their weights.
- [EditCast3D: Single-Frame-Guided 3D Editing with Video Propagation and View Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.13652): Proposes EditCast3D, a pipeline that employs video generation foundation models to propagate edits from a single first frame across the entire dataset prior to reconstruction.
- [CanvasMAR: Improving Masked Autoregressive Video Prediction With Canvas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.13669): Improves masked autoregressive video prediction by adding a blurred global canvas prior and motion-aware sampling order. Core idea: provide global frame structure early so few-step autoregressive video synthesis is more coherent.
- [STANCE Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.14588): uses sparse-to-dense anchored encoding for motion-coherent video generation.
- [NANO3D: A Training-Free Approach for Efficient 3D Editing Without Masks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.15019): Edits 3D objects without masks by combining FlowEdit with TRELLIS and region-aware Voxel or Slat merging to preserve unedited structure.
- [TGT: Text-Grounded Trajectories for Locally Controlled Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.15104): Conditions video generation on paired trajectories and localized text descriptions, using location-aware cross-attention and dual CFG to bind motions to specific entities.
- [EDVD-LLaMA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.16442): uses multimodal large-language-model reasoning for explainable deepfake video detection.
- [HGC-Avatar: Hierarchical Gaussian Compression for Streamable Dynamic 3D Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.16463): Compresses dynamic 3D avatars by separating structural Gaussians from SMPL-X motion layers, supporting progressive decoding and controllable rendering from video or text pose inputs.
- [From Mannequin to Human: A Pose-Aware and Identity-Preserving Video Generation Framework for Lifelike Clothing Display](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.16833): Introduces a new task called mannequin-to-human (M2H) video generation, which aims to synthesize identity-controllable, photorealistic human videos from footage of mannequins.
- [Video Prediction of Dynamic Physical Simulations With Pixel-Space Spatiotemporal Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.20807): Uses pixel-space spatiotemporal transformers for autoregressive physical-simulation video prediction, extending horizons without latent feature machinery.
- [Enhancing Video Inpainting with Aligned Frame Interval Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.21461): Proposes VidPivot, which separates multi-frame image inpainting from masked-area motion propagation and uses frame-interval priors, FrameProp propagation, and a context controller for coherent video inpainting.
- [BachVid: Training-Free Video Generation with Consistent Background and Character](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.21696): Improves training-free text-to-video generation with consistent characters and backgrounds across clips. Core idea: cache and reuse diffusion-transformer attention and feature signals so multi-video generation preserves identity and scene context without reference images.
- [LSF-Animation: Label-Free Speech-Driven Facial Animation via Implicit Feature Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.21864): Removes explicit identity and emotion labels by extracting emotion from speech and identity from a neutral mesh, then fuses cues with a hierarchical interaction block.
- [STG-Avatar: Animatable Human Avatars via Spacetime Gaussian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.22140): Couples linear blend skinning with Spacetime Gaussians, using optical-flow-guided densification for high-dynamic regions while retaining real-time animatable avatar rendering.
- [FAME](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.22960): A fairness-aware video editing method that injects fairness embeddings and modulates attention to reduce stereotype drift while preserving temporal consistency.
- [Lookahead Anchoring: Preserving Character Identity in Audio-Driven Human Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.23581): Preserves identity in autoregressive audio-driven animation by guiding each window toward future keyframe anchors or a self-keyframed reference image.
- [Model-Guided Dual-Role Alignment for High-Fidelity Open-Domain Video-to-Audio Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.24103): Uses a flow-based Transformer, dual-role audio-visual alignment, and a model-guided objective for open-domain video-to-audio generation, improving cross-modal coherence on VGGSound and UnAV-100.
- [SAGE: Structure-Aware Generative Video Transitions between Diverse Clips](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.24667): Generates structure-aware transitions between diverse clips by preserving silhouettes and salient features, extending video generation toward coherent in-betweening across large semantic gaps.
- [VividCam: Learning Unconventional Camera Motions from Virtual Synthetic Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.24904): Teaches video diffusion unconventional camera motions from simple synthetic 3D videos, using disentanglement strategies to isolate camera motion from low-poly appearance artifacts.
- [SEE4D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.26796): Generates pose-free 4D content through autoregressive video inpainting, extending 4D generation with a video-diffusion style synthesis route.
- [OmniX: From Unified Panoramic Generation and Perception to Graphics-Ready 3D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.26800): Repurposes 2D generative priors for panoramic perception, generation, and completion, producing PBR-ready 3D scenes from panorama workflows.
- [DANCER: Dance ANimation via Condition Enhancement and Rendering with diffusion model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.27169): Extends Stable Video Diffusion for single-person dance with an appearance-enhancement module, pose-rendering motion guidance, and the TikTok-3K training set.
- [Generative human motion mimicking through feature extraction in denoising diffusion settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.00011): Combines motion inpainting and style-transfer diffusion to mimic and creatively vary incoming motion-capture sequences using high-level movement features.
- [LeMiCa: Lexicographic Minimax Path Caching for Efficient Diffusion-Based Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.00090): Frames cache scheduling as an error-weighted graph and uses lexicographic minimax path optimization to bound worst-case accumulated error in accelerated video diffusion.
- [Diff4Splat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.00503): Generates controllable 4D scenes with latent dynamic reconstruction models, linking 3D scene structure with temporal video-world generation.
- [MotionStream: Real-Time Video Generation with Interactive Motion Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.01266): Introduces MotionStream, present MotionStream, enabling sub-second latency with up to 29 FPS streaming generation on a single GPU. the approach begins by augmenting a text-to-video model with motion control.
- [Towards One-step Causal Video Generation via Adversarial Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.01419): Introduces a distillation-based framework for efficient causal video generation that enables high-quality synthesis with extremely limited denoising steps. the approach builds upon the Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) framework.
- [UniLumos: Fast and Unified Image and Video Relighting with Physics-Plausible Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.01678): To enable fine-grained relighting control and supervision, we design a structured six-dimensional annotation protocol capturing core.
- [Unified Long Video Inpainting and Outpainting via Overlapping High-Order Co-Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.03272): Unifies long-video inpainting and outpainting with overlapping high-order co-denoising for temporally extended editing.
- [PhysCorr: Dual-Reward DPO for Physics-Constrained Text-to-Video Generation with Automated Preference Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.03997): Combines PhysicsRM, a dual-dimensional reward model for object stability and interactions, with PhyDPO preference optimization to steer text-to-video outputs toward physical consistency.
- [RISE-T2V: Rephrasing and Injecting Semantics with LLM for Expansive Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.04317): Conditions video diffusion on LLM next-token hidden states through a Rephrasing Adapter, implicitly expanding short prompts into richer intent-aligned semantics.
- [THEval. Evaluation Framework for Talking Head Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.04520): Evaluates talking-head generators with eight efficient metrics across quality, naturalness, and synchronization, using 85K videos from 17 models and a new real dataset.
- [Neodragon: Mobile Video Generation using Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.06055): We introduce Neodragon, a text-to-video system capable of generating 2s (49 frames @24 fps) videos at the 640x1024 resolution directly on a Qualcomm Hexagon NPU in a record 6.7s (7 FPS).
- [RelightMaster](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.06271): Uses multi-plane light images for precise video relighting, adding an editing-oriented control method for generated or restored video.
- [4DSTR: Advancing Generative 4D Gaussians with Spatial-Temporal Rectification for High-Quality and Consistent 4D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.07241): Improves generative 4D Gaussians with spatial-temporal rectification, targeting higher-quality and more temporally consistent 4D generation.
- [ISExplore:Informative Segment Selection for Efficient Personalized 3D Talking Face Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.07940): Selects a few-second reference segment by audio diversity, lip movement, and viewpoint diversity, cutting NeRF or 3DGS talking-face preparation time while preserving quality.
- [3D4D: An Interactive, Editable, 4D World Model via 3D Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.08536): Turns text and static images into interactive 4D scenes through four generation modules, WebGL Supersplat rendering, and foveated real-time exploration.
- [PipeDiT: Accelerating Diffusion Transformers in Video Generation with Task Pipelining and Model Decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.12056): Accelerates DiT video generation with PipeSP sequence-parallel pipelining and DeDiVAE, which decouples diffusion and VAE execution across GPU groups to reduce latency and memory pressure.
- [ProAV-DiT: A Projected Latent Diffusion Transformer for Efficient Synchronized Audio-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.12072): Aligns audio and video in a shared latent space with a multi-scale dual-stream spatiotemporal autoencoder, group cross-modal attention, and a 3D latent DiT.
- [Generative Photographic Control for Scene-Consistent Video Cinematic Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.12921): Introduces CineCtrl, which disentangles camera motion from photographic controls such as bokeh and shutter speed through decoupled cross-attention for scene-consistent video editing.
- [InstantViR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.14208): Uses distilled diffusion priors for real-time video inverse problems, adding a fast generative restoration route for video inputs.
- [StreamingTalker: Audio-driven 3D Facial Animation with Autoregressive Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.14223): Generates speech-driven 3D facial motion with an autoregressive diffusion model that streams audio chunks and conditions each window on a limited history of past motion frames.
- [FreeSwim: Revisiting Sliding-Window Attention Mechanisms for Training-Free Ultra-High-Resolution Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.14712): Motivated by this limitation, we introduce a training-free approach that leverages video Diffusion Transformers pretrained at their native scale to synthesize higher resolution videos without any additional training or adaptation.
- [First Frame Is the Place to Go for Video Content Customization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.15700): Treats the first frame as a conceptual memory buffer, enabling reference-based video customization from 20 to 50 examples without architecture changes or large-scale finetuning.
- [Degradation-Aware Hierarchical Termination for Compressed Video Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.16137): improves blind quality enhancement of compressed video through degradation-aware hierarchical termination.
- [TriDiff-4D: Fast 4D Generation through Diffusion-based Triplane Re-posing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.16662): Generates long 4D avatar sequences by creating a canonical 3D avatar and motion sequence from text, then re-posing triplanes with a second diffusion model.
- [NoPo-Avatar: Generalizable and Animatable Avatars from Sparse Inputs without Human Poses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.16673): Reconstructs animatable 3D human avatars from one or sparse images without test-time camera or human-pose inputs, improving robustness to noisy pose estimates.
- [Self-Supervised Audio-Visual Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.17181): evaluates self-supervised audio, video, and multimodal representations for interpretable deepfake detection.
- [PostCam: Camera-Controllable Novel-View Video Generation with Query-Shared Cross-Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.17185): Aligns 6-DoF poses and rendered features in a shared latent space with Query-Shared Cross-Attention, improving camera-trajectory precision while preserving dynamic-scene details.
- [Plan-X: Instruct Video Generation via Semantic Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.17986): Proposes Plan-X, a framework that explicitly enforces high-level semantic planning to instruct video generation process. At its core lies a Semantic Planner, a learnable multimodal language model that reasons over the user's intent from both text prompts and visual context, and autoregressively generates a sequence of text-grounded spatio-temporal semanti.
- [EgoControl: Controllable Egocentric Video Generation via 3D Full-Body Poses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.18173): Conditions egocentric video diffusion on explicit 3D full-body pose sequences with a pose representation that captures camera dynamics and body articulation.
- [Sequence-Adaptive Video Prediction in Continuous Streams using Diffusion Noise Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.18255): Adapts a frozen diffusion video predictor to continuous streams by optimizing inference noise per sequence, evaluated under simultaneous adaptation and prediction on long Ego4D-style videos.
- [Point-to-Point: Sparse Motion Guidance for Controllable Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.18277): Represents motion with anchor tokens, a compact set of informative point trajectories extracted from video diffusion priors and relocated for controllable subject edits.
- [FlowPortal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.18346): Uses residual-corrected flow for training-free video relighting and background replacement, extending lightweight video-editing control.
- [MagicWorld: Interactive Geometry-driven Video World Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.18886): Proposes MagicWorld, an interactive video world model built upon an autoregressive framework.
- [Eevee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.18957): targets close-up high-resolution video-based virtual try-on, extending human-centric video generation and editing.
- [Beyond Reward Margin: Rethinking and Resolving Likelihood Displacement in Diffusion Models via Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.19049): Informed by these insights, we introduce a novel solution named Policy-Guided DPO (PG-DPO), combining Adaptive Rejection Scaling (ARS) and Implicit Preference Regularization.
- [Learning Plug-and-play Memory for Guiding Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.19229): Adds plug-and-play memory to guide video diffusion models, targeting temporal consistency and controllability in generated videos.
- [Rectified SpaAttn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.19835): Revisits attention sparsity for efficient video generation, adding another sparse-attention route for video diffusion or autoregressive synthesis.
- [UltraViCo: Breaking Extrapolation Limits in Video Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.20123): Improves video diffusion transformers for length extrapolation beyond training windows.
- [Exo2EgoSyn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.20186): Adapts foundation video generators for exocentric-to-egocentric synthesis with view alignment and multi-view conditioning.
- [STARFlow-V: End-to-End Video Generative Modeling with Normalizing Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.20462): Additionally, we propose flow-score matching, which equips the model with a light-weight causal denoiser to improve the video generation consistency in an autoregressive fashion. To improve the sampling efficiency, STARFlow-V employs a vide.
- [PhysChoreo: Physics-Controllable Video Generation with Part-Aware Semantic Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.20562): Adds part-aware semantic grounding for physics-controllable video generation.
- [Inferix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.20714): Uses block diffusion as an inference engine for world simulation, connecting video generation systems with interactive world-model simulation.
- [Split-then-Merge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.20809): Learns layer-aware video composition by splitting unlabeled videos into foreground and background and recombining them.
- [CtrlVDiff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.21129): Unifies multimodal controls for video diffusion so geometry, intrinsics, semantics, and appearance constraints can guide controllable video generation.
- [Efficient Training for Human Video Generation with Entropy-Guided Prioritized Progressive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.21136): Uses conditional entropy inflation to prioritize critical components and an adaptive progressive schedule to reduce human-video diffusion training time and memory.
- [3MDiT: Unified Tri-Modal Diffusion Transformer for Text-Driven Synchronized Audio-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.21780): Models video, audio, and text as jointly evolving streams in a tri-modal diffusion transformer with isomorphic audio branches and omni-block fusion.
- [WorldWander: Bridging Egocentric and Exocentric Worlds in Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.22098): Bridges egocentric and exocentric views for video generation, strengthening viewpoint transfer in world-video synthesis.
- [BlockVid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.22973): Uses block diffusion for high-quality minute-long video generation, adding a long-duration synthesis model line to video generation.
- [DualCamCtrl: Dual-Branch Diffusion Model for Geometry-Aware Camera-Controlled Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.23127): Introduces dual-branch geometry-aware camera-controlled video generation.
- [PanFlow: Decoupled Motion Control for Panoramic Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.00832): Decouples panorama camera rotation from optical-flow motion control, adds spherical noise warping for loop consistency, and trains on pose and flow annotated panoramic video.
- [Goal-Driven Reward by Video Diffusion Models for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.00961): Experiments on Meta-World and Distracting Control Suite demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach.
- [AlignVid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.01334): A training-free attention-scaling method for text-guided image-to-video generation with lightweight query or key scaling and guidance scheduling.
- [Open-world Hand-Object Interaction Video Generation Based on Structure and Contact-aware Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.01677): Generates open-world hand-object interaction videos with structure and contact-aware representations.
- [Generative Video Motion Editing with 3D Point Tracks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.02015): We present a track-conditioned V2V framework that enables joint editing of camera and object motion. We achieve this by conditioning a video generation model on a source video and paired 3D point tracks representing source and target motions.
- [Progressive Image Restoration via Text-Conditioned Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.02273): Treats still-image restoration as a text-conditioned video generation process, progressively synthesizing intermediate restoration states rather than applying a single-step enhancer.
- [Video Diffusion Models Excel at Tracking Similar-Looking Objects Without Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.02339): Studies the unsupervised tracking ability that emerges in video diffusion models, especially for similar-looking objects, and links motion encoding to model-side video representations.
- [YingVideo-MV: Music-Driven Multi-Stage Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.02492): Generates long music-performance videos through audio semantic analysis, shot planning, and camera-conditioned diffusion. Core idea: make song structure and camera motion explicit controls for temporally coherent music-video generation.
- [Hear What Matters! Text-conditioned Selective Video-to-Audio Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.02650): Introduces text-conditioned selective video-to-audio generation for isolating the intended sound source in multi-object videos. Core idea: use text as a selector over visual events so audio generation supports controllable editing and mixing.
- [LoVoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.02933): Enables text-guided, mask-free video object removal and addition through learnable object-aware localization.
- [In-Context Sync-LoRA for Portrait Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.03013): Introduces Sync-LoRA, present Sync-LoRA, a method for editing portrait videos that achieves high-quality visual modifications while maintaining frame-accurate synchronization and identity consistency. the approach uses an image-to-video diffusion model.
- [GeoVideo: Introducing Geometric Regularization into Video Generation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.03453): Introduces geometric regularization losses into video generation by augmenting latent diffusion models with per-frame depth prediction. We adopted depth as the geometric representation because of the great progress in depth prediction and its compatibility with image-based latent encoders.
- [LAMP: Language-Assisted Motion Planning for Controllable Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.03619): Uses LLMs as motion planners that translate natural-language scene descriptions into a motion DSL and 3D object and camera trajectories for controllable video generation.
- [Zero-Shot Video Translation and Editing with Frame Spatial-Temporal Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.03905): Performs zero-shot video translation and editing with explicit frame spatial-temporal correspondence. Core idea: constrain both intra-frame and inter-frame features so edited videos remain temporally coherent without additional training.
- [UniMo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.03918): Unifies 2D video and 3D human motion in an autoregressive framework, linking human-motion modeling with video generation.
- [Beyond Flicker: Detecting Kinematic Inconsistencies for Generalizable Deepfake Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.04175): Trains deepfake video detectors on synthesized facial kinematic inconsistencies, shifting generated-video forensics from frame flicker toward violations of natural motion dependencies.
- [Inference-time Stochastic Refinement of GRU-Normalizing Flow for Real-time Video Motion Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.04282): Adds MCMC-style stochastic refinement to GRU-normalizing-flow inference, improving diverse yet coherent future keypoint trajectories for real-time motion transfer.
- [FMA-Net++: Motion- and Exposure-Aware Real-World Joint Video Super-Resolution and Deblurring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.04390): Models coupled motion and changing exposure with hierarchical bidirectional propagation, exposure-time modulation, and flow-guided dynamic filtering for joint VSR and deblurring.
- [EgoLCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.04515): Generates egocentric videos with long-context diffusion, extending video generation toward first-person long-context synthesis.
- [VideoSSM: Autoregressive Long Video Generation with Hybrid State-Space Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.04519): We propose VideoSSM, a Long Video Model that unifies AR diffusion with a hybrid state-space memory.
- [Deep Forcing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.05081): Extends long video generation through training-free deep-sink guidance and participative compression.
- [TV2TV: A Unified Framework for Interleaved Language and Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.05103): Interleaves language modeling and video flow matching in a Mixture-of-Transformers so the model can reason in text before generating video frames.
- [IE2Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.05240): Adapts pretrained diffusion models for event-based video reconstruction, adding a generative reconstruction route for event-camera video signals.
- [Delving into Latent Spectral Biasing of Video VAEs for Superior Diffusability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.05394): We present a statistical analysis of video VAE latent spaces and identify two spectral properties essential for diffusion training: a spatio-temporal frequency spectrum biased toward low frequencies, and a channel-wise eigenspectrum dominated by a few modes.
- [USV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.05754): Uses unified sparsification to accelerate video diffusion models.
- [Bring Your Dreams to Life: Continual Text-to-Video Customization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.05802): To resolve the above challenges, we develop a novel Continual Customized Video Diffusion (CCVD) model, which can continuously learn new concepts to generate videos across various text-to-video generation tasks by tackling forgetting and concept neglect.
- [Coherent Audio-Visual Editing via Conditional Audio Generation Following Video Edits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.07209): Edits audio conditionally after video edits so the sound track stays aligned with changed visual events. Core idea: treat video editing and audio generation as a coupled consistency problem rather than independent post-processing steps.
- [Unified Camera Positional Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.07237): Adds camera-position encoding for controlled video generation, improving viewpoint and motion control in generated videos.
- [Communication-Efficient Serving for Video Diffusion Models with Latent Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.07350): To tackle this issue, we exploit the local spatio-temporal dependencies inherent in the diffusion denoising process and propose Latent Parallelism (LP), the first parallelism strategy tailored for VDM serving.
- [MultiMotion: Multi Subject Video Motion Transfer via Video Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.07500): Introduces MultiMotion, present MultiMotion, a novel unified framework that overcomes these limitations. Furthermore, introduces RectPC, a high-order predictor-corrector solver for efficient and accurate sampling, particularly beneficial for multi-entity generation.
- [OneStory: Coherent Multi-Shot Video Generation with Adaptive Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.07802): Proposes OneStory, enabling global yet compact cross-shot context modeling for consistent and scalable narrative generation.
- [WorldReel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.07821): Generates 4D video with consistent geometry and motion modeling, strengthening video generation coverage for temporally persistent scene structure.
- [EgoX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.08269): Generates egocentric video from an exocentric video, adding a viewpoint-transfer route for first-person video generation.
- [PAVAS: Physics-Aware Video-to-Audio Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.08282): Introduces physics-aware video-to-audio synthesis using object mass, motion, and interaction cues. Core idea: make generated sounds reflect physical causes in the video instead of only matching appearance-level audio-visual correlations.
- [GeoDiffMM: Geometry-Guided Conditional Diffusion for Motion Magnification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.08325): Conditions diffusion motion magnification on optical-flow geometry and a learnable magnification factor, with noise-free flow augmentation and flow-based video synthesis.
- [GimbalDiffusion: Gravity-Aware Camera Control for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.09112): Introduces gravity-aware camera control for video generation, extending camera-motion conditioning beyond ordinary prompt or trajectory control.
- [StereoWorld: Geometry-Aware Monocular-to-Stereo Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.09363): Repurposes a pretrained video generator for monocular-to-stereo video with geometry-aware regularization, spatiotemporal tiling, and an 11M-frame stereo dataset.
- [DirectSwap: Mask-Free Cross-Identity Training and Benchmarking for Expression-Consistent Video Head Swapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.09417): Creates HeadSwapBench with synthetic paired cross-identity swaps and trains a mask-free video diffusion head-swapper using motion and expression aware reconstruction loss.
- [VHOI: Controllable Video Generation of Human-Object Interactions from Sparse Trajectories via Motion Densification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.09646): Introduces VHOI, propose VHOI, a two-stage framework that first densifies sparse trajectories into HOI mask sequences, and then fine-tunes a video diffusion model conditioned on these dense masks. introduces a novel HOI-aware motion representation that uses color encodings to dis.
- [ShotDirector: Directorially Controllable Multi-Shot Video Generation with Cinematographic Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.10286): Proposes ShotDirector, an efficient framework that integrates parameter-level camera control and hierarchical editing-pattern-aware prompting. Specifically, we adopt a camera control module that incorporates 6-DoF poses and intrinsic settings to enable precise camera information injection.
- [OmniZoo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.10352): Generates topology-agnostic animal motion from text prompts, expanding motion generation across skeletal structures.
- [Audio-sync Video Instance Editing with Granularity-Aware Mask Refiner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.10571): Introduces AVI-Edit with an iterative granularity-aware mask refiner and self-feedback audio agent, giving instance-level video edits fine spatial masks and audio-synchronized temporal control.
- [GaussianHeadTalk: Wobble-Free 3D Talking Heads with Audio Driven Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.10939): Maps 3D Gaussian splats with 3D morphable models and predicts audio-driven parameters with a transformer to stabilize real-time talking-head generation.
- [AutoRefiner: Improving Autoregressive Video Diffusion Models via Reflective Refinement Over the Stochastic Sampling Path](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.11203): Adds a pathwise noise refiner and reflective KV-cache to improve autoregressive video diffusion samples at inference time without updating model weights.
- [REST: Diffusion-based Real-time End-to-end Streaming Talking Head Generation via ID-Context Caching and Asynchronous Streaming Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.11229): Learns a compact spatiotemporal VAE latent, maintains identity and temporal coherence with ID-Context Cache, and distills a streaming student asynchronously.
- [JoyStreamer-Flash: Real-time and Infinite Audio-Driven Avatar Generation with Autoregressive Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.11423): Enables infinite audio-driven avatar diffusion with progressive step bootstrapping, motion-condition injection from noisy previous frames, and cache-resetting unbounded RoPE.
- [Flowception: Temporally Expansive Flow Matching for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.11438): We present Flowception, a novel non-autoregressive and variable-length video generation framework. Flowception learns a probability path that interleaves discrete frame insertions with continuous frame denoising.
- [V-RGBX: Video Editing with Accurate Controls over Intrinsic Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.11799): Controls intrinsic properties accurately for video editing.
- [SPDMark: Selective Parameter Displacement for Robust Video Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.12090): Embeds in-generation watermarks by applying LoRA-based selective parameter displacements to video diffusion models, with frame-specific messages and matching-based recovery for temporal tampering.
- [SMRABooth: Subject and Motion Representation Alignment for Customized Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.12193): Aligns subject and motion representations for customized video generation.
- [V-Warper: Appearance-Consistent Video Diffusion Personalization via Value Warping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.12375): Improves appearance-consistent video diffusion personalization through value warping.
- [Endless World: Real-Time 3D-Aware Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.12430): Introduces Endless, motivated by this, introduces Endless World, a real-time framework for infinite, 3D-consistent video this http URL support infinite video generation, introduces a conditional autoregressive training strategy that aligns newly generated content with existing video frames.
- [InteracTalker: Prompt-Based Human-Object Interaction with Co-Speech Gesture Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.12664): Unifies co-speech gestures and prompt-based object interaction with a shared motion-speech-prompt embedding space, generalized motion adaptation, and adaptive fusion during diffusion sampling.
- [STARCaster](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.13247): Uses spatio-temporal autoregressive video diffusion for identity- and view-aware talking portraits, extending portrait video generation with stronger identity and viewpoint control.
- [Transform Trained Transformer: Accelerating Naive 4K Video Generation Over 10⨉](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.13492): Introduces a transformer acceleration method for high-resolution video generation.
- [Soul: Breathe Life into Digital Human for High-fidelity Long-term Multimodal Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.13495): Animates digital humans from portrait, text, and audio with Soul-1M data, audio-injection layers, threshold-aware codebook replacement, and distillation for long-term consistency.
- [LongVie 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.13604): Builds a controllable ultra-long video world model, extending video generation toward longer, multimodal, stateful scene synthesis.
- [Grab-3D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.13665): detects AI-generated videos through 3D geometric temporal consistency.
- [Elastic3D: Controllable Stereo Video Conversion with Guided Latent Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.14236): Converts monocular videos into controllable stereo videos with guided latent decoding, adding a 3D-aware video-generation route for immersive content.
- [HGS: Hybrid Gaussian Splatting with Static-Dynamic Decomposition for Compact Dynamic View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.14352): Separates static and dynamic Gaussian primitives with RBF modeling and two-stage training, yielding compact real-time dynamic view synthesis for free-viewpoint video.
- [Native and Compact Structured Latents for 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.14692): Uses native compact structured latents for 3D generation, replacing heavier intermediate representations with generation-friendly structured tokens.
- [IC-Effect: Precise and Efficient Video Effects Editing via In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.15635): We propose IC-Effect, an instruction-guided, DiT-based framework for few-shot video VFX editing that synthesizes complex effects ( flames, particles and cartoon characters) while strictly preserving spatial and temporal consistency.
- [End-to-End Training for Autoregressive Video Diffusion via Self-Resampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.15702): Trains autoregressive video diffusion end to end with self-resampling, reducing train-test mismatch in sequential denoising.
- [EverybodyDance: Bipartite Graph-Based Identity Correspondence for Multi-Character Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.16360): Enforces identity correspondence in multi-character animation with a bipartite identity matching graph, mask-query attention, identity-embedded guidance, and a dedicated IC benchmark.
- [Anchored Video Generation: Decoupling Scene Construction and Temporal Synthesis in Text-to-Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.16371): We introduce Anchored Video Generation (AVG), a modular pipeline that decouples these tasks by decomposing the Text-to-Video generation into three specialized stages: (1) Reasoning, where a Large Language Model (LLM) rewrites the video prompt to describe only the initial scene, resolving temporal ambiguities;.
- [Pixel Seal: Adversarial-only training for invisible image and video watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.16874): Trains invisible image and video watermarks with an adversarial-only objective, adding provenance protection for generated and edited visual media.
- [Infinite-Homography as Robust Conditioning for Camera-Controlled Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.17040): Conditions camera-controlled video generation with homography-based geometric guidance. Core idea: preserve camera-pose faithfulness and view consistency without depending entirely on fragile depth reprojection.
- [Asynchronous Pipeline Parallelism for Real-Time Multilingual Lip Synchronization in Video Communication Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.18318): Pipelines translation, speech processing, and lip synchronization asynchronously with graph compilation, quantization, kernel fusion, and silence-aware segmentation for low-latency multilingual video calls.
- [EchoMotion: Unified Human Video and Motion Generation via Dual-Modality Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.18814): To address this, we introduce EchoMotion, a framework designed to model the joint distribution of appearance and human motion, thereby improving the quality of complex human action video generation.
- [CETCAM: Camera-Controllable Video Generation via Consistent and Extensible Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.19020): Uses consistent and extensible geometry-aware tokenization to control camera motion in video diffusion without requiring scalable camera-pose annotations.
- [WorldWarp: Propagating 3D Geometry with Asynchronous Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.19678): Introduces WorldWarp, to bridge this gap, introduces WorldWarp, a framework that couples a 3D structural anchor with a 2D generative refiner.
- [Generating the Past, Present and Future from a Motion-Blurred Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.19817): Repurposes a pretrained video diffusion model to infer videos before, during, and after a blurred exposure, supporting camera trajectory, object-motion, and dynamic-3D recovery.
- [Learning to Refocus with Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.19823): Uses video diffusion priors for refocusing, turning blur and depth cues into temporally coherent focus edits rather than isolated per-frame restoration.
- [Few-Shot-Based Modular Image-to-Video Adapter for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.20000): Introduces a few-shot modular adapter for image-to-video diffusion models.
- [AirGS: Real-Time 4D Gaussian Streaming for Free-Viewpoint Video Experiences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.20943): Streams 4D Gaussian video by converting updates into multi-channel 2D formats, selecting keyframes, pruning transmitted Gaussian updates, and preserving temporal coherence.
- [FluencyVE: Marrying Temporal-Aware Mamba with Bypass Attention for Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.21015): Replaces temporal attention in one-shot video editing with temporal-aware Mamba plus low-rank bypass attention to reduce compute while preserving frame-level consistency.
- [ACD: Direct Conditional Control for Video Diffusion Models via Attention Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.21268): Adds direct conditional control to video diffusion models through attention supervision over sparse 3D-aware object layouts. Core idea: make layout and condition faithfulness part of the denoising objective rather than relying only on indirect classifier-free guidance.
- [Knot Forcing: Taming Autoregressive Video Diffusion Models for Real-time Infinite Interactive Portrait Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.21734): Stabilizes infinite autoregressive portrait animation by constraining diffusion trajectories with knot-style temporal anchors for real-time interactive generation.
- [Inference-based GAN Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.21776): Uses leverages a Markov chain framework with a recall mechanism, where each state represents a short-length VAE-GAN video generator.
- [EasyOmnimatte](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.21865): adapts pretrained inpainting diffusion models for video layered decomposition, linking omnimatte-style editing with diffusion priors.
- [Characterizing Motion Encoding in Video Diffusion Timesteps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.22175): Analyzes where motion information appears across diffusion timesteps, clarifying how video diffusion models encode dynamics during denoising.
- [SoulX-FlashTalk: Real-Time Infinite Streaming of Audio-Driven Avatars via Self-Correcting Bidirectional Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.23379): Uses self-correcting bidirectional distillation, retrospective self-correction, hybrid sequence parallelism, parallel VAE, and kernel optimizations for 14B real-time infinite avatar streaming.
- [HY-Motion 1.0: Scaling Flow Matching Models for Text-To-Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.23464): Scales flow-matching models for text-to-motion generation, providing a motion-generation foundation model rather than a narrow animation post-process.
- [LiveTalk: Real-Time Multimodal Interactive Video Diffusion via Improved On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.23576): Improves on-policy distillation for text, image, and audio conditioned avatar diffusion, then combines audio language models with Anchor-Heavy Identity Sinks for real-time multi-turn interaction.
- [Stream-DiffVSR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.23709): Uses autoregressive diffusion for low-latency streamable video super-resolution, extending efficient video restoration models.
- [Pretraining Frame Preservation for Lightweight Autoregressive Video History Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.23851): We present a lightweight history encoder that maps long video histories into short-length embeddings, pretrained with a frame query objective that learns to attend to content features at arbitrary temporal positions.
- [RainFusion2.0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.24086): Adds temporal-spatial awareness and hardware-efficient block-wise sparse attention for video restoration and generation workflows.
- [I2VGuard](https://doi.org/10.1109/CVPR52734.2025.01175): Protects input images from misuse by diffusion-based image-to-video models.
- [HomoGen](https://doi.org/10.1109/CVPR52734.2025.02137): Combines homography propagation and diffusion for enhanced video inpainting.
- [LLM hidden-state conditioning for video diffusion](https://doi.org/10.1109/ECAI65401.2025.11095434): Conditions video diffusion on hidden states from instruction-following LLMs.
- [MotionShot](https://doi.org/10.1109/ICCV51701.2025.01103): Transfers motion adaptively across arbitrary objects in text-to-video generation.
- [MotionMatcher](https://doi.org/10.1109/ICCVW69036.2025.00728): Customizes cinematic motion in text-to-video diffusion via motion-feature matching.
- [DAADiff](https://doi.org/10.1109/BigData66926.2025.11401904): Provides a dual-axis adaptive framework for real-time video inpainting.
- [VASA-Rig](https://doi.org/10.1109/TVCG.2025.3549168): Generates audio-driven 3D facial animation with live mood dynamics. Core idea: condition facial motion on speech and affect so avatar video generation can preserve expressive temporal behavior.
- [L-C4](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.neucom.2025.132199): Uses language-based control for creative and consistent video colorization. Core idea: guide video color generation with text while preserving temporal color consistency.
- [Keyframe-Guided Creative Video Inpainting](https://doi.org/10.1109/cvpr52734.2025.01214): Uses keyframes to guide creative video inpainting, adding controllable temporal editing coverage.
- [Dlfr-Gen](https://doi.org/10.1109/ICCV51701.2025.01523): Generates video with dynamic latent frame rates, adding frame-rate control to diffusion-based video synthesis.
- [Adaptor](https://doi.org/10.1109/CVPRW67362.2025.00632): Reduces tokens adaptively in video diffusion transformers, adding a video-specific efficiency route for long or high-resolution generation.
- [PARO for Video Generation](https://doi.org/10.1109/DAC63849.2025.11133383): Uses pattern-aware reorder-based attention quantization for video generation models, adding a hardware-software co-design route for efficient synthesis.
- [MoAnimate](https://doi.org/10.1109/TCSVT.2025.3639082): Bridges motion-oriented latent representation gaps for human video animation so generated human videos can follow more controllable motion.
- [Aligning Global Semantics and Local Textures in Generative Video Enhancement](https://doi.org/10.1109/ICCV51701.2025.01587): Improves generative video enhancement by balancing semantic coherence and local texture fidelity.
- [Enhancing Video Stylization with Integral Noise](https://doi.org/10.1109/ICNC64010.2025.10993671): Uses integral-noise design to improve video stylization, adding an editing-oriented model line for temporal style control.
- [Flow Enhanced Dual Transformer](https://doi.org/10.1109/MSN69125.2025.00062): Uses optical-flow-enhanced dual Transformer modeling for video inpainting, extending temporal-consistency methods for generative video editing.
- [Drafting and Revision for Video Inpainting](https://doi.org/10.24963/ijcai.2025/230): advances high-fidelity video inpainting through a drafting-and-revision design.
- [22DEditor](https://doi.org/10.1109/TCSVT.2025.3642772): edits volumetric videos from text with spatiotemporal consistency using a single 2D diffusion prior.
- [IDCNet Deepfake Detection](https://doi.org/10.1109/TIFS.2025.3593353): uses image decomposition and cross-view distillation for generalizable deepfake detection.
- [RivuletMLP](https://doi.org/10.1109/CVPR52734.2025.00688): uses an MLP architecture for efficient compressed-video quality enhancement, extending video restoration models.
- [Frequency-Domain Video Demoireing](https://doi.org/10.1109/TCSVT.2025.3636874): removes video moire artifacts through spatial-temporal filtering in the frequency domain, extending video restoration and enhancement coverage.
- [GAN Spatio-Temporal Video Watermarking](https://doi.org/10.1109/ICICCS67942.2025.11382501): combines spatio-temporal features and attention in a GAN-based robust video-watermarking method for content protection.
- [Mdff-Net Deepfake Video Detection](https://doi.org/10.1109/ACROSET66531.2025.11281048): uses multi-domain feature fusion to improve robust deepfake video detection.
- [Hybrid Deepfake Video Detection](https://doi.org/10.1109/ICONAT66879.2025.11362687): uses hybrid deep learning to improve generalization and fairness in deepfake video detection.
- [Multistream Face-Swapping Attack Detection](https://doi.org/10.1109/COMSCI67172.2025.11225271): detects face-swapping attacks with multistream feature embeddings, extending generated-video forensic models.
- [Face Forgery Video Detection via Temporal Forgery Cue Unraveling](https://doi.org/10.1109/cvpr52734.2025.00693): Models temporal forgery cues for face-forgery video detection, strengthening generated-video forensics beyond frame-level artifacts.
- [DTR: Dynamic Tree-Ring Watermarking Framework for Diffusion-Based Video Generation](https://doi.org/10.1109/icassp49660.2025.10888152): Introduces dynamic Tree-Ring watermarking for diffusion-based video generation, strengthening provenance and ownership signals for generated videos.
- [RBMark: Robust and blind video watermark in DT CWT domain](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.jvcir.2025.104438): Adds a robust blind video-watermarking method in the DT-CWT domain, complementing generated-video provenance and content-protection methods.
- [Implanting Robust Watermarks in Latent Diffusion Models for Video Generation](https://doi.org/10.1109/icassp49660.2025.10888991): Implants robust watermarks into latent diffusion models for video generation, adding a provenance method tied directly to the generator.
- [Video Diffusion Posterior Sampling for Seeing Beyond Dynamic Scattering Layers](https://doi.org/10.1109/tpami.2025.3598457): Uses video diffusion posterior sampling to recover scenes behind dynamic scattering layers.
- [RINGet: A Robust Watermarking Framework for Diffusion-Based Video Generation](https://doi.org/10.1109/tcsvt.2025.3643532): Introduces a robust watermarking framework for diffusion-based video generation.
- [UniAdapter: All-in-One Control for Flexible Video Generation](https://doi.org/10.1109/tcsvt.2025.3532495): Provides an all-in-one adapter for flexible video control, unifying multiple conditioning signals instead of training separate control modules for each generation mode.
- [TokensGen: Harnessing Condensed Tokens for Long Video Generation](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccv51701.2025.01691): Uses condensed tokens for long video generation, compressing temporal context so autoregressive synthesis can extend duration with lower token redundancy.
- [Harnessing Conventional Video Processing Insights for Emerging 3D Video Generation Models: A Comprehensive Attention-aware Way](https://doi.org/10.1109/dac63849.2025.11132992): Transfers conventional video-processing priors into attention-aware 3D video generation, using motion and temporal-structure insights to guide emerging generative backbones.
- [A Scalable Framework for Satellite Video and Annotation Generation based on 3D Foundation Model](https://doi.org/10.1109/igarss55030.2025.11243378): Uses a 3D foundation model to synthesize satellite videos together with annotations, targeting scalable remote-sensing video data generation rather than generic T2V clips.
- [Hierarchical Emotion-Guided Masked Transformer for Long-Sequence Co-Speech Gestures with Partial Supervision](https://doi.org/10.1109/ijcb65343.2025.11411217): Generates long co-speech gestures with a hierarchical emotion-guided masked Transformer, using partial supervision to model emotion-conditioned motion over long sequences.
