- [VideoVAE+: Large Motion Video Autoencoding with Cross-Modal Video VAE](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccv51701.2025.01668): Designs a cross-modal video VAE for large-motion autoencoding, strengthening latent video tokenization for downstream generation backbones.
- [RADiT: Redundancy-Aware Diffusion Transformer Acceleration Leveraging Timestep Similarity](https://doi.org/10.1109/dac63849.2025.11133190): Exploits timestep similarity in diffusion transformers to skip redundant computation, providing a redundancy-aware acceleration path for video generation inference.
- [Generating Long-Take Videos via Effective Keyframes and Guidance](https://doi.org/10.1109/wacv61041.2025.00365): Generates long-take videos by selecting effective keyframes and using guidance between them, improving temporal continuity beyond short clip synthesis.
- [QK-Edit: Revisiting Attention-based Injection in MM-DiT for Image and Video Editing](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccv51701.2025.01770): Revisits attention injection in multimodal DiTs, editing images and videos through query-key manipulation rather than full model retraining.
- [STDD: Spatio-Temporal Dual Diffusion for Video Generation](https://doi.org/10.1109/cvpr52734.2025.01173): Splits video generation into coupled spatial and temporal diffusion processes, modeling frame quality and motion dynamics with a dual spatiotemporal denoising design.
- [AST-Adapter: Parameter-Efficient Video-to-Video Transfer Learning With Adaptive Spatiotemporal Information Bias](https://doi.org/10.1109/tcsvt.2025.3638017): Uses an adaptive spatiotemporal-information-bias adapter for parameter-efficient video-to-video transfer learning.
- [Spatio-Temporal Energy-Guided Diffusion Model for Zero-Shot Video Synthesis and Editing](https://doi.org/10.1109/tcsvt.2025.3531390): Guides diffusion with spatiotemporal energy terms for zero-shot video synthesis and editing, steering temporal coherence and edit fidelity without task-specific finetuning.
- [GE-adapter: A general and efficient adapter for enhanced video editing with pretrained text-to-image diffusion models](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.eswa.2025.129649): Adapts pretrained text-to-image diffusion models for video editing with a general efficient adapter, reusing image priors while adding temporal edit consistency.
- [Denoising Reuse: Exploiting Inter-Frame Motion Consistency for Efficient Video Generation](https://doi.org/10.1109/tcsvt.2025.3548728): Reuses denoising computation across frames by exploiting inter-frame motion consistency, reducing redundant work in efficient video generation.
- [Mirage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.24227): Applies one-step video diffusion to coherent asset editing in driving scenes, adding a fast domain-grounded video-editing route.
- [Corgi: Cached Memory Guided Video Generation](https://doi.org/10.1109/wacv61041.2025.00450): Guides video generation with cached memory, preserving previously generated scene and motion context to improve continuity across frames or clips.
- [Layer-Animate for Transparent Video Generation](https://doi.org/10.1109/icassp49660.2025.10887999): Introduces a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, verifier, reward model, or data-generation route for video generation.
- [Tuning-Free High-Resolution Video Diffusion With Spatial-Temporal Latent Grouping](https://doi.org/10.1109/tmm.2025.3618540): Uses ST-LAG to denoise video latents in spatial and temporal groups, reusing a low-resolution diffusion prior while recombining regions into globally coherent high-resolution videos.
- [LCGD: Enhancing Text-to-Video Generation via Contextual LLM Guidance and U-Net Denoising](https://doi.org/10.1109/access.2025.3550945): Uses an LLM to expand contextual guidance for text prompts and couples it with U-Net denoising so text-to-video diffusion receives richer semantic control during generation.
- [Explicit Depth-Aware Blurry Video Frame Interpolation Guided by Differential Curves](https://doi.org/10.1109/cvpr52734.2025.00192): Adds explicit depth and differential-curve guidance to blurry video frame interpolation, targeting occlusion and motion-boundary consistency when synthesizing missing frames.
- [Explicit Guidance for Robust Video Frame Interpolation Against Discontinuous Motions](https://doi.org/10.1109/wacv61041.2025.00820): Guides video frame interpolation with explicit discontinuous-motion cues so generated intermediate frames handle abrupt object motion and occlusion more robustly.
- [CCE: A Content Creation Engine With Outlier and Mixed-Representation Computing, Semantic-Defined Instruction Generation for Video Diffusion](https://doi.org/10.1109/jssc.2025.3622515): Implements a hardware-oriented video-diffusion content engine that mixes representation formats, handles outlier computation, and generates semantic instructions for efficient synthesis.
- [A Two Stage Generative Model for Controllable Human Head Motion Video Generation](https://doi.org/10.1109/nicoint67466.2025.00021): Splits controllable head-motion video generation into a motion-planning stage and a rendering stage, separating temporal head trajectory control from final visual synthesis.
- [Enhancing Human-Computer Interaction Through Decoupling Motion and Camera Control in Human-Centric Video Generation](https://doi.org/10.1080/10447318.2025.2487877): Decouples human body motion from camera motion in human-centric video generation, giving interactive systems separate controls over subject dynamics and viewpoint.
- [Enhancing Text-to-Video Generation by Using a Training-Free Approach](https://doi.org/10.1109/nnice64954.2025.11064441): Improves text-to-video generation through inference-time guidance rather than finetuning, making prompt or latent control portable across pretrained video generators.
- [Joint Global-Local Frames Modeling to enhance semantic alignment for zero-shot long video editing](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.neucom.2025.130836): Combines global sequence context with local frame modeling for zero-shot long-video editing, aiming to keep semantic edits aligned while preserving temporal coherence.
- [SUE-Adapter: Enhancing Complex-Semantic-Long-Text to Video Generation Models with Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/icbaie66852.2025.11326569): Uses an LLM-assisted adapter to restructure complex long-text prompts before video generation, improving semantic grounding for multi-clause text-to-video requests.
- [3D Denoising Diffusion Video Frame Interpolation (5DVFI)](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccr67607.2025.11372085): Applies a 3D denoising diffusion formulation to video frame interpolation, modeling space and time jointly while synthesizing intermediate frames.
- [DM-360VFI: 360° Spherical Video Interpolation Using Diffusion Model](https://doi.org/10.1109/mlmc65154.2025.11189310): Adapts diffusion-based frame interpolation to spherical 360-degree video, where generated frames must remain coherent across panorama boundaries.
- [EasyControl: Adding Control to Video Diffusion for Controllable Video Generation and Interpolation](https://doi.org/10.1109/icassp49660.2025.10889997): Transfers ControlNet-style condition features into video diffusion with lightweight adapters, enabling controllable generation and interpolation from single condition maps.
- [AdaptEdit: An Adaptive Correspondence Guidance Framework for Reference-Based Video Editing](https://doi.org/10.24963/ijcai.2025/1131): Uses adaptive correspondence guidance to transfer reference edits through a video, preserving spatial alignment and temporal consistency without dense manual masks.
- [TH-Mamba: Spatial-Temporal Correlation Learning for Mamba-Based Talking Head Generation](https://doi.org/10.1109/tcsvt.2025.3596747): Builds talking-head generation around Mamba-based spatiotemporal correlation learning, using sequence-state modeling to coordinate facial dynamics across frames.
- [Co-Speech Gesture Video Generation with Implicit Motion-Audio Entanglement](https://doi.org/10.1109/cvpr52734.2025.01063): Learns implicit audio-motion entanglement for co-speech gesture video generation, aligning synthesized body gestures with speech rhythm and content.
- [Unity in Diversity: Video Editing via Gradient-Latent Purification](https://doi.org/10.1109/cvpr52734.2025.02179): Purifies gradient and latent signals during diffusion video editing so edits can vary locally while retaining unified temporal structure across the clip.
- [NADM: Noise-Aware Diffusion Model for Landscape Painting Video Generation](https://doi.org/10.1109/tcyb.2025.3576752): Introduces a noise-aware diffusion model for landscape-painting video generation, using noise modeling to stabilize stylized temporal evolution.
- [Dynamic Attention-Guided Video Generation from Text with Multi-Scale Synthesis and LoRA Optimization](https://doi.org/10.1109/iscait64916.2025.11010506): Combines dynamic attention, multi-scale synthesis, and LoRA optimization to improve text-to-video detail control without retraining a full generator.
- [Align-A-Video: Deterministic Reward Tuning of Image Diffusion Models for Consistent Video Editing](https://doi.org/10.1109/cvpr52734.2025.00199): Tunes image diffusion models with deterministic reward signals so per-frame edits become temporally consistent when lifted to video editing.
- [CoFaCo: Controllable Generative Talking Face Video Coding](https://doi.org/10.1109/tip.2025.3648562): Treats talking-face video coding as controllable generation, using compact facial controls to reconstruct or transmit expressive talking-head videos.
- [SynGauss: Real-Time 3D Gaussian Splatting for Audio-Driven Talking Head Synthesis](https://doi.org/10.1109/access.2025.3548015): Uses 3D Gaussian splatting for real-time audio-driven talking-head synthesis, mapping speech cues to deformable Gaussian head representations.
- [GaussianSpeech: Audio-Driven Personalized 3D Gaussian Avatars](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccv51701.2025.01214): Builds personalized 3D Gaussian avatars driven by audio, linking speech features to animatable Gaussian geometry and appearance.
- [Diffused Poses and Distilled Expressions for Controllable Audio-driven Talking Face Generation](https://doi.org/10.1109/icassp49660.2025.10887982): Separates diffused head-pose generation from distilled expression control, improving controllability in audio-driven talking-face videos.
- [FlowStyler: Artistic Video Stylization Via Transformation Fields Transports](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccv51701.2025.00953): Transports transformation fields across frames for artistic video stylization, maintaining temporal coherence while applying style changes.
- [DreamAnimate: Temporal Consistency and Detail Preservation for Character Animation](https://doi.org/10.1109/icme59968.2025.11210030): Focuses character animation on both temporal consistency and detail preservation, balancing motion transfer with source-character appearance fidelity.
- [Event-based Video Super-Resolution via State Space Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/cvpr52734.2025.01172): Uses state-space models to fuse event-camera signals for video super-resolution, exploiting sparse high-temporal-resolution events to reconstruct sharper frames.
- [Truncate Diffusion: Efficient Video Editing With Low-Rank Truncate](https://doi.org/10.1109/tmm.2025.3590901): Applies low-rank truncation to diffusion-based video editing to reduce compute while preserving the editing trajectory.
- [Controllable Human Video Generation From Sparse Sketches](https://doi.org/10.1109/tvcg.2025.3543687): Conditions human video generation on sparse sketches, giving users lightweight spatial controls while the model fills in appearance and temporally coherent motion.
- [DreamHA: Towards High-Quality Human Animation with Image-to-Video Diffusion Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/icassp49660.2025.10890226): Adapts image-to-video diffusion to human animation, emphasizing identity preservation and smooth motion from still human references.
- [High-Fidelity Semantic Video Communication with Controllable Image-To-Video Diffusion Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/ism66958.2025.00033): Uses controllable image-to-video diffusion as a semantic video communication decoder, reconstructing high-fidelity clips from compact visual or semantic controls.
- [VidEvo: Evolving Video Editing through Exhaustive Temporal Modeling](https://doi.org/10.24963/ijcai.2025/99): Strengthens video editing with exhaustive temporal modeling, explicitly tracking long-range frame dependencies so edits stay coherent over time.
- [A Framework With Multi-Scale Hybrid Mamba Voxel Flow for Video Prediction](https://doi.org/10.1109/tcsvt.2025.3612980): Combines multi-scale voxel flow with hybrid Mamba sequence modeling for video prediction, using state-space dynamics to forecast future frames efficiently.
- [HTMDF-DD: Hybrid triple modality based spatial-temporal features early fusion for deepfake detection](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.compeleceng.2025.110860): Fuses three modalities early in a spatiotemporal detector for deepfake videos, targeting model-side authenticity classification rather than generation.
- [Video Steganography With Optimized Robust Modulation Paths for Lossy Channels](https://doi.org/10.1109/lsp.2025.3632809): Optimizes modulation paths for video steganography so hidden payloads survive lossy channels while preserving visual quality.
- [Resampling video super-resolution based on multi-scale guided optical flow](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.compeleceng.2025.110176): Uses multi-scale guided optical flow to resample frames for video super-resolution, aligning motion before reconstructing high-resolution details.
- [Aurora: Adaptive Audio-Video Multi-Scale Attention Fusion for Deepfake Detection](https://doi.org/10.1109/icpads67057.2025.11322961): Detects deepfakes by adaptively fusing audio and video cues with multi-scale attention, capturing cross-modal inconsistencies in generated media.
- [Boosting Deepfake Video Detection Using Non-Local Attention and Sharpness-Aware Minimization](https://doi.org/10.1109/avss65446.2025.11149952): Combines non-local attention with sharpness-aware minimization to improve deepfake-video detector generalization across manipulation sources.
- [TEV-STT: Text-Guided Expressive Video Generation from Facial Images via Spatio-Temporal Transformer](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccds64403.2025.11209591): Uses a spatiotemporal transformer to animate facial images under text guidance, turning expression descriptions into temporally coherent portrait videos.
- [LMTalker: Sparse Landmark-guided Gaussian Splatting for High-fidelity Talking Head Synthesis](https://doi.org/10.1109/icassp49660.2025.10889082): Drives Gaussian-splatting talking heads with sparse landmarks, improving high-fidelity facial motion while keeping the control signal lightweight.
- [HERA: Hybrid Explicit Representation for Ultra-Realistic Head Avatars](https://doi.org/10.1109/cvpr52734.2025.00033): Uses a hybrid explicit representation for head avatars, combining controllable geometry with appearance detail for ultra-realistic reenactment.
- [StruGauAvatar: Learning Structured 3D Gaussians for Animatable Avatars From Monocular Videos](https://doi.org/10.1109/tvcg.2025.3557457): Learns structured 3D Gaussian avatars from monocular videos, making the recovered representation animatable from limited single-view footage.
- [BM-Edit: Background retention and motion consistency for zero-shot video editing](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.knosys.2025.113784): Edits videos zero-shot while explicitly preserving background content and motion consistency, reducing unintended drift outside the target edit.
- [Zero-Shot Customized Video Editing with Diffusion Feature Transfer](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccvw69036.2025.00643): Transfers diffusion features from reference customization into target videos, enabling zero-shot subject or style edits without per-video training.
- [Towards Long-Sequence Image-to-Video Generation: A Multi-Stage Diffusion Framework for Temporal and Motion Consistency](https://doi.org/10.1109/ispacs68724.2025.11383105): Uses a multi-stage diffusion pipeline for long image-to-video generation, separating temporal extension and motion-consistency refinement.
- [Reconstructing High Quality Raw Video Using Temporal Affinity and Diffusion Prior](https://doi.org/10.1109/tpami.2025.3596623): Reconstructs raw video with temporal-affinity modeling and diffusion priors, using neighboring-frame relationships to restore high-quality detail.
- [Few-shot-based video generation via multimodal fusion and Fourier Spliter](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.imavis.2025.105794): Combines multimodal fusion with a Fourier splitter for few-shot video generation, separating frequency components to stabilize appearance and motion from limited examples.
- [Research on Methods to Improve Video Generation Quality Based on Google DeepMind’s Veo Model](https://doi.org/10.1109/cvaa66438.2025.11193318): Studies quality-improvement strategies around Veo-style text-to-video generation, focusing on prompt, control, or workflow choices rather than a newly released backbone.
- [LLM-Guided Dual-Branch Diffusion Model for Fine-Grained Motion Synthesis](https://doi.org/10.1109/icassp49660.2025.10889902): Uses LLM guidance with a dual-branch diffusion model to synthesize fine-grained motion, separating semantic planning from motion-detail generation.
- [4EV: Adaptive Video Editing With Spatial Temporal Dynamics and Motion Pathways](https://doi.org/10.1109/access.2025.3624055): Extends Stable Diffusion for prompt-driven video edits such as object movement, path navigation, background transitions, and zoom effects using spatiotemporal attention and attention-map injection.
- [Text-to-Sign Language Video Generation Using GANs, BERT, and Sora](https://doi.org/10.1109/isec64801.2025.11147381): Explores text-to-sign-language video generation with multimodal generative components.
- [Enhancing Video Generation Based on Text-to-Image Diffusion Models Using a Multimodal Approach](https://doi.org/10.1109/mercon67903.2025.11217072): Contributes a model, training, inference, or data-generation approach for video generation and editing.
- [DiffPortraitVideo: Diffusion-Based Expression-Consistent Zero-Shot Portrait Video Translation](https://doi.org/10.1109/tvcg.2025.3642300): Contributes a model, training, inference, or data-generation approach for video generation and editing.
- [TrackDiffusion: Tracklet-Conditioned Video Generation via Diffusion Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/wacv61041.2025.00349): Conditions diffusion on object tracklets and adds an instance enhancer, giving multi-object trajectory control while preserving object consistency across frames.
- [UniTMGE: Uniform Text-Motion Generation and Editing Model via Diffusion](https://doi.org/10.1109/wacv61041.2025.00595): Introduces TMMGE, a diffusion framework that unifies text-conditioned motion generation and motion editing under multimodal text-motion controls.
- [Optimizing Semantic and Texture Consistency in Video Generation](https://doi.org/10.32604/cmc.2025.065529): Proposes Dual Consistency Training with a multi-scale spatial adapter, CLIP and VGG semantic-texture losses, and dynamic CLWS weighting for one-shot text-to-video consistency.
- [Pixel-to-4D: Camera-Controlled Image-to-Video Generation with Dynamic 3D Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.00678): Uses dynamic 3D Gaussians for camera-controlled image-to-video generation, linking controllable video synthesis with 4D spatial structure.
- [DreamID-V](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.01425): uses a diffusion transformer for high-fidelity identity-preserving image-to-video face swapping.
- [MM-Sonate: Multimodal Controllable Audio-Video Generation with Zero-Shot Voice Cloning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.01568): Provides multimodal controllable audio-video generation with zero-shot voice cloning.
- [MotionAdapter: Video Motion Transfer via Content-Aware Attention Customization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.01955): Introduces MotionAdapter, a content-aware motion transfer framework that enables robust and semantically aligned motion transfer within DiT-based video diffusion models.
- [MagicFight: Personalized Martial Arts Combat Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.02107): Personalizes martial-arts combat video generation by conditioning action choreography on target identities, combining subject preservation with fast interaction motion.
- [DreamStyle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.02785): provides a unified video stylization framework, extending controllable video editing and generation.
- [Mind the Generative Details: Direct Localized Detail Preference Optimization for Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.04068): Introduces LocalDPO, to address these limitations, introduces LocalDPO, a novel post-training framework that constructs localized preference pairs from real videos and optimizes alignment at the spatio-temporal region level. During training.
- [ReHyAt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.04342): Adds recurrent hybrid attention to video diffusion transformers, contributing an architecture line for temporal visual generation.
- [PackCache: A Training-Free Acceleration Method for Unified Autoregressive Video Generation via Compact KV-Cache](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.04359): Introduces PackCache, leveraging these observations, introduces PackCache, a training-free KV-cache management method that dynamically compacts the KV cache through three coordinated mechanisms: condition anchoring that preserves semantic references.
- [Plenoptic Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.05239): To address it, we introduce PlenopticDreamer, a framework that synchronizes generative hallucinations to maintain spatio-temporal memory. The core idea is to train a multi-in-single-out video-conditioned model in an autoregressive manner, aided by a camera-guided video retrieval strategy that adaptively selects salient videos from previous generations as.
- [GaussianSwap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.05511): uses 3D Gaussian splatting for animatable video face swapping.
- [Rotate Your Character: Revisiting Video Diffusion Models for High-Quality 3D Character Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.05722): Presents RCM (Rotate your Character Model), an advanced image-to-video diffusion framework tailored for high-quality novel view synthesis (NVS) and 3D character generation.
- [TAGRPO: Boosting GRPO on Image-to-Video Generation with Direct Trajectory Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.05729): Presents TAGRPO, a robust post-training framework for I2V models inspired by contrastive learning.
- [VideoAR: Autoregressive Video Generation via Next-Frame & Scale Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.05966): Generates videos autoregressively by predicting both the next frame and scale, using tokenized temporal progression to extend clips with controlled resolution growth.
- [Focal Guidance: Unlocking Controllability from Semantic-Weak Layers in Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07287): To address this, we propose Focal Guidance (FG), which enhances the controllability from Semantic-Weak Layers.
- [Rewriting Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.08565): Reauthors existing video footage from text instructions, adding a text-driven video editing method to the generation-model section.
- [MoCha:End-to-End Video Character Replacement without Structural Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.08587): Proposes MoCha, a pioneering framework that bypasses these limitations by requiring only a single arbitrary frame mask.
- [Motion Attribution for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.08828): Analyzes how video generators attribute motion, adding a diagnostic model-side lens for temporal control and motion consistency.
- [V-DPM: 4D Video Reconstruction with Dynamic Point Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.09499): Introduces V-DPM, the approach achieves state of the art performance in 3D and 4D reconstruction for dynamic scenes.
- [Efficient Camera-Controlled Video Generation of Static Scenes via Sparse Diffusion and 3D Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.09697): Combines sparse diffusion and 3D rendering for camera-controlled video generation, strengthening viewpoint control in generated static scenes.
- [FlowAct-R1: Towards Interactive Humanoid Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.10103): Proposes FlowAct-R1, a framework specifically designed for real-time interactive humanoid video generation. Built upon a MMDiT architecture, FlowAct-R1 enables the streaming synthesis of video with arbitrary durations while maintaining low-latency responsiveness.
- [ELITE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.10200): Builds Gaussian head avatars from monocular video with learned initialization and test-time generative adaptation, adding an efficient personalized-avatar generation method.
- [Beyond Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.10214): Uses 3D understanding for precise camera-controlled video generation.
- [FrankenMotion: Part-level Human Motion Generation and Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.10909): Composes human motion at the body-part level, enabling generated motions to recombine localized actions while maintaining whole-body coordination.
- [Dynamic Sparse Attention for Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.11641): Uses mixture-aware dynamic sparse attention to make video diffusion transformers more efficient.
- [Learning Stochastic Bridges for Video Object Removal via Video-to-Video Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.12066): To address the trade-off where strong bridge priors hinder the removal of large objects, we propose a novel adaptive mask modulation strategy.
- [Moaw: Unleashing Motion Awareness for Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.12761): Trains video diffusion models for motion perception and dense tracking, then reuses the learned motion awareness for controllable motion transfer.
- [LAViG-FLOW: Latent Autoregressive Video Generation for Fluid Flow Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.13190): Applies latent autoregressive video generation to fluid-flow simulations, treating physical dynamics as a temporally rolled-out video modeling problem.
- [VideoMaMa](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.14255): Uses generative priors and mask guidance for video matting, extending video editing coverage to temporally coherent foreground extraction.
- [LaVR: Scene Latent Conditioned Generative Video Trajectory Re-Rendering using Large 4D Reconstruction Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.14674): Uses scene-latent conditioned generative trajectory re-rendering for video.
- [Towards Holistic Modeling for Video Frame Interpolation with Auto-regressive Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.14959): Introduces LDF-VFI, an autoregressive diffusion-transformer frame-interpolation paradigm with skip-concatenate sampling for long-range temporal stability.
- [StableWorld](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.15281): Targets stable and consistent long interactive video generation.
- [HVD: Human Vision-Driven Video Representation Learning for Text-Video Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.16155): Uses a human-vision-inspired coarse-to-fine retrieval model with frame selection and patch compression modules, removing redundant frames and aggregating salient patch entities for entity-level text-video matching.
- [PyraTok: Language-Aligned Pyramidal Tokenizer for Video Understanding and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.16210): Learns language-aligned pyramidal video tokens with multi-scale quantization, improving video reconstruction, text-to-video generation, and zero-shot video understanding.
- [Memory-V2V: Memory-Augmented Video-to-Video Diffusion for Consistent Multi-Turn Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.16296): We introduce Memory-V2V, a memory-augmented framework that treats prior edits as structured constraints for subsequent generations. Memory-V2V maintains an external memory of previous outputs, retrieves task-relevant edits, and integrates them through relevance-aware tokenization and adaptive compression.
- [SALAD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.16515): Tunes efficient linear attention to achieve high sparsity in video diffusion transformers.
- [LoL: Longer than Longer, Scaling Video Generation to Hour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.16914): Introduces LoL, to address it, introduces a lightweight, training-free approach that effectively suppresses this behavior by introducing multi-head RoPE jitter that breaks inter-head attention homogenization.
- [Reward-Forcing: Autoregressive Video Generation with Reward Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.16933): Introduces Reward-Forcing, by using reward signals to guide the model, the method simplifies training while preserving high visual fidelity and temporal consistency. Through extensive experiments on standard benchmarks.
- [SkyReels-V3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.17323): Reports a unified conditional video generation model for reference-image synthesis, video extension, and audio-guided generation.
- [Entropy-Guided k-Guard Sampling for Long-Horizon Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.19488): Adapts autoregressive video-token sampling by token entropy, reducing redundant randomness in low-uncertainty regions and error compounding in high-uncertainty regions for long-horizon video generation.
- [FAIRT2V: Training-Free Debiasing for Text-to-Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.20791): Mitigates demographic bias in text-to-video diffusion without finetuning by neutralizing prompt embeddings with anchor-based spherical geodesic transformations.
- [Zero-Shot Video Restoration and Enhancement with Assistance of Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21922): Proposes the first framework that utilizes the rapidly-developed video diffusion model to assist the image-based method in maintaining more temporal consistency for zero-shot video restoration and enhancement.
- [JUST-DUB-IT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22143): Adapts an audio-video diffusion foundation model with LoRA and synthetic multilingual video pairs for translated audio and synchronized facial motion.
- [VMonarch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22275): Builds efficient video diffusion transformers with structured attention.
- [Hi-Light](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23167): Targets high-fidelity, high-resolution video relighting. Core idea: combine a relighting model with a dedicated evaluation paradigm so lighting edits are judged on temporal and physical consistency.
- [TokenTrim: Inference-Time Token Pruning for Autoregressive Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.00268): Prunes unstable latent conditioning tokens during autoregressive inference so corrupted tokens are not reused, reducing long-horizon temporal drift without retraining the video generator.
- [Scalable Generative Game Engine: Breaking the Resolution Wall via Hardware-Algorithm Co-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.00608): Decouples compute-bound world-model generation from memory-bound decoding through hardware-algorithm co-design for higher-resolution neural game simulation.
- [PISCES: Annotation-free Text-to-Video Post-Training via Optimal Transport-Aligned Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.01624): Post-trains text-to-video generators without annotations using Dual Optimal-Transport aligned rewards that match reward signals to human-judgment dimensions.
- [GPD: Guided Progressive Distillation for Fast and High-Quality Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.01814): Accelerates video diffusion through Guided Progressive Distillation, where an online teacher progressively trains a student to take larger denoising steps.
- [FSVideo: Fast Speed Video Diffusion Model in a Highly-Compressed Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02092): We introduce FSVideo, a fast speed transformer-based image-to-video (I2V) diffusion framework. We build our framework on the following key components: 1.) a new video autoencoder with highly-compressed latent space ($64644$ spatial-temporal downsampling ratio), achieving competitive reconstruction quality; 2.
- [Causal Forcing: Autoregressive Diffusion Distillation Done Right for High-Quality Real-Time Interactive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02214): Introduces Causal, to address this issue, introduces Causal Forcing, which uses an autoregressive teacher for ODE initialization to bridge the architectural gap.
- [Quant VideoGen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02958): Enables autoregressive long-video generation with two-bit KV-cache quantization for more efficient extended synthesis.
- [Morphe: High-Fidelity Generative Video Streaming with Vision Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.03529): Uses a vision foundation model for high-fidelity generative video streaming, targeting continuous generation rather than offline clip synthesis.
- [3D-Aware Implicit Motion Control for View-Adaptive Human Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.03796): We introduce 3DiMo, which jointly trains a motion encoder with a pretrained video generator to distill driving frames into compact, view-agnostic motion tokens, injected semantically via cross-attention.
- [Adaptive 1D Video Diffusion Autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.04220): Proposes One-Dimensional Diffusion Video Autoencoder (One-DVA), a transformer-based framework for adaptive 1D encoding and diffusion-based decoding. The encoder employs query-based vision transformers to extract spatiotemporal features and produce latent representations, while a variable-length dropout mechanism dynamically adjusts the latent length.
- [Light Forcing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.04789): Accelerates autoregressive video diffusion with sparse attention, improving long-video generation efficiency.
- [Euphonium: Steering Video Flow Matching via Process Reward Gradient Guided Stochastic Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.04928): Proposes Euphonium, a novel framework that steers generation via process reward gradient guided dynamics.
- [GT-SVJ: Generative-Transformer-Based Self-Supervised Video Judge For Efficient Video Reward Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.05202): Recasts video generative transformers as energy-based reward models so temporal structure from generators can judge video quality without VLM-only rewards.
- [FastVMT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.05551): Removes motion and gradient redundancy in DiT-based video generation with local attention masking and diffusion-step gradient reuse.
- [Pathwise Test-Time Correction for Autoregressive Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.05871): Introduces Test-Time, to overcome these limitations, introduces Test-Time Correction (TTC), a training-free alternative. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the method seamlessly integrates with various distilled models.
- [Context Forcing: Consistent Autoregressive Video Generation with Long Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06028): Introduces Context Forcing, to resolve this, introduces Context Forcing, a novel framework that trains a long-context student via a long-context teacher. To make this computationally feasible for extreme durations (e.g., 2 minutes).
- [CineScene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06959): Injects 3D-aware scene features into pretrained text-to-video models to control camera motion while preserving scene consistency.
- [Ex-Omni: Enabling 3D Facial Animation Generation for Omni-modal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.07106): Adds speech-accompanied 3D facial animation to omni-modal LLMs by decoupling semantic reasoning from temporal motion generation with speech-unit scaffolding and token-as-query gated fusion.
- [VFace Video Face Swapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.07835): provides a training-free diffusion approach for video face swapping.
- [Geometry-Aware Rotary Position Embedding for Consistent Video World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.07854): Adds geometry-aware rotary position embeddings for consistent video world models.
- [PISCO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.08277): Trains a video diffusion model for precise instance insertion from sparse keyframe controls, using variable-information guidance, temporal masking, and geometry-aware conditioning to preserve scene dynamics.
- [Language-Guided Motion Tokenizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.08337): Uses a language-guided transformer tokenizer for human motion generation, linking motion tokens with text control.
- [D2-VR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.08395): Combines degradation robustness and distillation for video restoration, adding an optimization route for generative video enhancement.
- [ALIVE: Animate Your World with Lifelike Audio-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.08682): Adapts pretrained text-to-video models to text-to-video-and-audio and reference animation with TA-CrossAttn, UniTemp-RoPE, and joint audio-video branches.
- [Causality in Video Diffusers is Separable from Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10095): Shows that the causal reasoning in these models is separable from the multi-step denoising process. Through systematic probing of autoregressive video diffusers, we uncover two key regularities: (1) early layers produce highly similar features across denoising steps, indicating redundant computation along the diffusion trajectory;.
- [Flow Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.10825): Uses flow caching to accelerate autoregressive video generation.
- [HairWeaver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11117): Synthesizes photorealistic hair motion with few-shot sim-to-real guided video diffusion.
- [LUVE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11564): Uses latent-cascaded generation and dual-frequency experts for ultra-high-resolution video synthesis, adding a frequency-specialized architecture line.
- [Variation-aware Flexible 3D Gaussian Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11638): Edits 3D Gaussians flexibly with variation-aware modeling, preserving intended structure while allowing controlled shape or appearance changes.
- [Light4D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11769): Provides training-free 4D video relighting for extreme viewpoints, extending controllable video editing to viewpoint-changing lighting effects.
- [MonarchRT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12271): Uses efficient attention to support real-time video generation.
- [DCDM: Divide-and-Conquer Diffusion Models for Consistency-Preserving Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.13637): Uses divide-and-conquer diffusion for consistency-preserving video generation.
- [EchoTorrent: Towards Swift, Sustained, and Streaming Multi-Modal Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.13669): Targets swift and sustained streaming multimodal video generation.
- [Train Short, Inference Long: Training-free Horizon Extension for Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14027): Extends autoregressive video generation horizons without additional training, targeting longer coherent generated videos.
- [Adapting VACE for Real-Time Autoregressive Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14381): Moves VACE reference-frame control into a parallel conditioning pathway so pretrained VACE weights can support causal chunks, KV caching, structural control, and inpainting in real-time autoregressive video diffusion.
- [AnchorWeave](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14941): Uses retrieved local spatial memories and multi-anchor weaving to improve long-term scene consistency in camera-controllable video generation.
- [EditCtrl](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15031): Separates local and global controls for real-time generative video editing, adding a controllability method for video diffusion workflows.
- [Consistency-Preserving Diverse Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15287): Jointly samples flow-matching videos with latent-space diversity updates while projecting out components that would harm temporal consistency, improving batch diversity without decoder backpropagation.
- [CHAI: CacHe Attention Inference for text2video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.16132): Introduces Cache Attention to reuse latents across semantically related prompts, cutting text-to-video diffusion latency while preserving quality with as few as eight denoising steps.
- [Generated Reality: Human-centric World Simulation using Interactive Video Generation with Hand and Camera Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18422): We introduce a human-centric video world model that is conditioned on both tracked head pose and joint-level hand poses.
- [Flash-VAED](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19161): Adds plug-and-play VAE decoders for efficient video generation, targeting faster decoding without changing the full generator stack.
- [PropFly: Learning to Propagate via On-the-Fly Supervision from Pre-trained Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.20583): Learns propagation from pretrained video diffusion through on-the-fly supervision, transferring generative priors into temporal propagation tasks.
- [Human Video Generation from a Single Image with 3D Pose and View Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.21188): Uses HVG, a latent video diffusion model with articulated pose modulation, to generate multi-view spatiotemporally coherent human videos from one image.
- [Towards Controllable Video Synthesis of Routine and Rare OR Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.21365): Builds an operating-room video diffusion framework that abstracts scene geometry, conditions synthesis on rare events, and generates synthetic near-miss sterile-field violation videos for detector training.
- [MultiAnimate: Pose-Guided Image Animation Made Extensible](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.21581): Extends pose-guided image animation to multi-character scenes with an Identifier Assigner and Identifier Adapter that preserve per-person identity and occlusion structure.
- [ProjFlow: Projection Sampling with Flow Matching for Zero-Shot Exact Spatial Motion Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22742): Building on the observation that many animation tasks can be formulated as a linear inverse problem, we introduce ProjFlow, a training-free sampler that achieves zero-shot, exact satisfaction of linear spatial constraints while preserving motion realism.
- [UCM: Unifying Camera Control and Memory with Time-aware Positional Encoding Warping for World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22960): Presents UCM, a novel framework that unifies long-term memory and precise camera control via a time-aware positional encoding warping mechanism.
- [SwitchCraft: Training-Free Multi-Event Video Generation with Attention Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.23956): Controls multi-event video generation without training through attention manipulation, scheduling separate events inside one generated clip.
- [SKeDA: A Generative Watermarking Framework for Text-to-video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00194): Adds generative watermarking to text-to-video diffusion models.
- [PreciseCache](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00976): designs precise feature caching for efficient, high-fidelity video generation.
- [FREE-Edit: Using Editing-aware Injection in Rectified Flow Models for Zero-shot Image-Driven Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01164): Recognizing this, we propose an Editing-awaRE (REE) injection method to modulate the injection intensity of each token. Specifically, we first compute the pixel difference between the source and edited first frame to form a corresponding editing mask.
- [UniTalking: A Unified Audio-Video Framework for Talking Portrait Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01418): Uses multi-modal transformer blocks with shared self-attention over audio and video latents for end-to-end lip-synchronized talking-portrait generation.
- [CamDirector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02256): edits long-term coherent camera trajectories in video, adding a control route for temporally stable video editing.
- [NOVA: Sparse Control, Dense Synthesis for Pair-Free Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02802): Proposes NOVA: Sparse Control \& Dense Synthesis, a new framework for unpaired video editing. Specifically, the sparse branch provides semantic guidance through user-edited keyframes distributed across the video, and the dense branch continuously incorporates motion and texture information from the original video to maintain high fidelity and coherence.
- [SIGMark: Scalable In-Generation Watermark with Blind Extraction for Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02882): Adds scalable in-generation watermarking with blind extraction for video diffusion models, targeting provenance and robustness under temporal disturbances.
- [CubeComposer: Spatio-Temporal Autoregressive 4K 360{\deg} Video Generation from Perspective Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04291): We introduce CubeComposer, a novel spatio-temporal autoregressive diffusion model that natively generates 4K-resolution 360° videos. By decomposing videos into cubemap representations with six faces, CubeComposer autoregressively synthesizes content in a well-planned spatio-temporal order, reducing memory demands while enabling high-resolution output.
- [Helios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04379): Targets real real-time long video generation, emphasizing streaming latency and sustained temporal coherence as model design constraints.
- [How Effective Are Publicly Accessible Deepfake Detection Tools? A Comparative Evaluation of Open-Source and Free-to-Use Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04456): Evaluates six practitioner-facing deepfake or tampering detection tools under blinded investigator protocols, contrasting forensic tools with AI classifiers on authentic, tampered, and AI-generated media.
- [FC-VFI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04899): targets faithful and consistent video-frame interpolation for high-FPS slow-motion generation.
- [FaceCam: Portrait Video Camera Control via Scale-Aware Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05506): Controls portrait-video camera behavior with scale-aware conditioning.
- [VDCook:DIY video data cook your MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05539): Provides a self-evolving video-data construction platform that combines query optimization, real-video retrieval, controlled synthesis, provenance, and notebooks for MLLM data.
- [Video Compression Meets Video Generation: Latent Inter-Frame Pruning with Attention Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05811): Proposes LIPAR, which skips recomputation of redundant latent patches and recovers pruned-token attention values, accelerating video generation and editing without visible quality loss.
- [Text-Driven Emotionally Continuous Talking Face Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06071): Defines emotionally continuous talking-face generation and introduces TIE-TFG, which maps text plus changing emotion descriptions into speech videos with dynamic facial expressions.
- [Positive-Incentive Noise for Neural Video Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06095): improves neural video compression of static scenes through positive-incentive noise.
- [FlowMotion: Training-Free Flow Guidance for Video Motion Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06289): Presents FlowMotion, a novel training-free framework that enables efficient and flexible motion transfer by directly leveraging the predicted outputs of flow-based T2V models.
- [Physical Simulator In-the-Loop Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06408): Introduces Test-Time, to further improve texture consistency during object movement, introduces a Test-Time Texture Consistency Optimization (TTCO) technique that adapts text and feature embeddings based on pixel correspondences from the simulator.
- [NEGATE: Constrained Semantic Guidance for Linguistic Negation in Text-to-Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06533): Adds constrained semantic guidance for linguistic negation in text-to-video diffusion, targeting a known text-conditioning failure mode in video generation.
- [Accelerating Video Generation Inference with Sequential-Parallel 3D Positional Encoding Using a Global Time Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06664): Adapts causal autoregressive video generation to sequence-parallel inference with Causal-RoPE SP, operator fusion, and RoPE precomputation to lower memory and first-frame latency.
- [Text-to-Skeleton Cascades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08028): Generates controllable complex human-motion video through text-to-skeleton cascades.
- [CinemaWorld: Generative Augmented Reality with LLMs and 3D Scene Generation for Movie Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08060): Extracts movie-scene features with MLLMs and generates synchronized 3D augmented-reality effects, objects, textures, and lighting for film viewing.
- [Video2LoRA: Unified Semantic-Controlled Video Generation via Per-Reference-Video LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08210): Controls video generation through per-reference-video LoRA.
- [HiAR: Efficient Autoregressive Long Video Generation via Hierarchical Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08703): Introduces HiAR, drawing inspiration from bidirectional diffusion models, which denoise frames at a shared noise level while maintaining coherence.
- [HECTOR: Hybrid Editable Compositional Object References for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08850): Uses hybrid image or video object references plus per-object trajectories so generated videos preserve referenced elements while controlling their location, scale, and speed.
- [SVG-EAR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08982): Compensates sparse video-generation attention through error-aware routing, adding an inference-efficiency method for diffusion-transformer video synthesis.
- [Training-free Motion Factorization for Compositional Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09104): Provides training-free motion factorization for compositional video generation.
- [From Ideal to Real](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09283): Improves stable video object removal under imperfect conditions, extending video editing models toward robustness when masks, inputs, or scene assumptions are noisy.
- [Streaming Autoregressive Video Generation via Diagonal Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09488): Introduces Diagonal, to address these issues, introduces Diagonal Distillation, which operates orthogonally to existing approaches and better exploits temporal information across both video chunks.
- [FrameDiT: Diffusion Transformer with Matrix Attention for Efficient Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09721): Introduces matrix attention for diffusion-transformer video generation, a model-architecture contribution to efficient synthesis.
- [Motion Forcing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10408): decouples motion dynamics for robust video generation.
- [SignSparK: Efficient Multilingual Sign Language Production via Sparse Keyframe Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10446): Uses sparse keyframe learning and conditional flow matching for multilingual sign-language production, scaling text-to-3D signing generation across several sign languages.
- [MANSION: Multi-floor lANguage-to-3D Scene generatIOn for loNg-horizon tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11554): Generates multi-floor 3D scenes from language for long-horizon tasks, extending scene generation to large indoor layouts rather than single rooms.
- [Controllable Egocentric Video Generation via Occlusion-Aware Sparse 3D Hand Joints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11755): Introduces Controllable Egocentric Video Generation via Occlusion-Aware Sparse 3D Hand Joints, to address these limitations, introduces a novel framework that generates egocentric videos from a single reference frame.
- [FlashMotion: Few-Step Controllable Video Generation with Trajectory Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12146): Introduces FlashMotion, to bridge this gap, introduces FlashMotion, a novel training framework designed for few-step trajectory-controllable video generation. For evaluation, introduces FlashBench, a benchmark for long-sequence.
- [DreamVideo-Omni: Omni-Motion Controlled Multi-Subject Video Customization with Latent Identity Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12257): Introduces DreamVideo-Omni, a unified framework enabling harmonious multi-subject customization with omni-motion control via a progressive two-stage training paradigm. To ensure robust and precise controllability.
- [EVATok](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12267): Uses adaptive-length video tokenization for efficient visual autoregressive generation, adding a token-budget control route for video synthesis.
- [MemRoPE: Training-Free Infinite Video Generation via Evolving Memory Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12513): Introduces MemRoPE, introduce MemRoPE, a training-free framework with two co-designed components.
- [LibraGen: Playing a Balance Game in Subject-Driven Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13506): To address this, we propose LibraGen, a novel framework that views extending foundation models for S2V generation as a balance game between intrinsic VGFM strengths and S2V capability.
- [CamLit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14241): Adds explicit camera and lighting control to video diffusion. Core idea: expose camera motion and illumination as controllable variables for more structured video generation.
- [Early Failure Detection and Intervention in Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14320): Studies early failure detection and intervention for video diffusion models.
- [LatSearch: Latent Reward-Guided Search for Faster Inference-Time Scaling in Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14526): Introduces LatSearch, introduce a latent reward model that scores partially denoised latents at arbitrary timesteps with respect.
- [MVHOI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14686): uses a 3D foundation model to bridge multi-view conditions for complex human-object interaction video reenactment.
- [Edit2Interp: Adapting Image Foundation Models from Spatial Editing to Video Frame Interpolation with Few-Shot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15003): Adapts an image editing foundation model to video frame interpolation with only 64 to 256 LoRA samples, exposing temporal synthesis from spatial priors.
- [Spatial-Temporal Likelihoods for Generated-Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15026): detects generated videos without training by scoring spatial-temporal likelihoods.
- [Generative Video Compression with 1D Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15302): uses one-dimensional latent representations for generative video compression.
- [Tri-Prompting: Video Diffusion with Unified Control over Scene, Subject, and Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15614): Controls scene, subject, and motion jointly in video diffusion, adding a fine-grained controllability method for video generation.
- [Physics-Informed Video Diffusion for Shallow Water Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15627): Injects shallow-water-equation physics into video diffusion so generated rollouts respect PDE dynamics rather than only visual plausibility.
- [SparkVSR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16864): Uses sparse keyframe propagation for interactive video super-resolution, extending generative video restoration and enhancement coverage.
- [Script-to-Slide Grounding: Grounding Script Sentences to Slide Objects for Automatic Instructional Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16931): Grounds script sentences to slide objects for automatic instructional video generation, linking narration text to visual elements before synthesis.
- [Stereo World Model: Camera-Guided Stereo Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17375): We present StereoWorld, a camera-conditioned stereo world model that jointly learns appearance and binocular geometry for end-to-end stereo video this http URL monocular RGB or RGBD approaches, StereoWorld operates exclusively within the RGB modality, while simultaneously grounding geometry directly from disparity.
- [Motion-Adaptive Temporal Attention for Lightweight Video Generation with Stable Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17398): Adds lightweight temporal attention to frozen Stable Diffusion, using local attention for high-motion clips and global attention for low-motion clips with only 25.8M trainable parameters.
- [SHIFT: Motion Alignment in Video Diffusion Models with Adversarial Hybrid Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17426): Aligns motion in video diffusion models through adversarial hybrid fine-tuning.
- [ChopGrad: Pixel-Wise Losses for Latent Video Diffusion via Truncated Backpropagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17812): Introduces pixel-wise losses for latent video diffusion through truncated backpropagation.
- [3DreamBooth: High-Fidelity 3D Subject-Driven Video Generation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18524): To resolve these issues, we introduce a novel framework for 3D-aware video customization, comprising 3DreamBooth and 3Dapter. 3DreamBooth decouples spatial geometry from temporal motion through a 1-frame optimization paradigm.
- [Improving Joint Audio-Video Generation with Cross-Modal Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18600): To alleviate these issues, we propose Cross-Modal Context Learning (CCL), equipped with several carefully designed modules.
- [Input-Stable Attention Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18636): Uses offline sparsity profiling and online QK co-clustering for training-free sparse attention in video generation.
- [EffectErase: Joint Video Object Removal and Insertion for High-Quality Effect Erasing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19224): To address this, we introduce VOR (Video Object Removal), a large-scale dataset that provides diverse paired videos, each consisting of one video where the target object is present with its effects and a counterpart where the object and effects are absent, with corresponding object masks.
- [OrbitNVS: Harnessing Video Diffusion Priors for Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19613): Proposes OrbitNVS, which reformulates NVS as an orbit video generation task. Through tailored model design and training strategies, we adapt a pre-trained video generation model to the NVS task, leveraging its rich visual priors to achieve high-quality view synthesis.
- [Minor Adjustments for User Intent in Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19672): Improves video-model adherence to user intent with lightweight adjustments rather than full retraining.
- [PerformRecast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19731): Disentangles expression and head pose for portrait video editing.
- [LumosX: Relate Any Identities with Their Attributes for Personalized Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20192): Recent advances in diffusion models have significantly improved text-to-video generation, enabling personalized content creation with fine-grained control over both foreground and background elements. However, precise face-attribute alignment across subjects remains challenging, as existing methods lack explicit mechanisms to ensure intra-group consistency.
- [PROBE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21547): Diagnoses residual concept capacity after erasure in text-to-video diffusion models, making concept-removal failures visible for video-generation safety.
- [Manifold-Aware Exploration for Reinforcement Learning in Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21872): Proposes SAGE-GRPO, constraining RL exploration near the pretrained video data manifold with manifold-aware SDE corrections and macro-level rollout filtering for more stable video post-training.
- [CLEAR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21901): Performs context-aware mask-free video subtitle removal, adding a specialized video editing and restoration model line.
- [PAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22193): builds a pose-appearance-motion engine for human-object-interaction video generation, improving sim-to-real motion and interaction synthesis.
- [ST-GDance++](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22316): Scales spatial-temporal diffusion for long-duration group choreography, adding a multi-person motion-generation line to video synthesis.
- [A Video Steganography for H.265/HEVC Based on Multiple CU Size and Block Structure Distortion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22850): Embeds secret bits by selecting H.265 coding-unit sizes under a block-structure stability metric and distortion model, improving anti-steganalysis robustness and capacity.
- [InterDyad](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23132): A dyadic speech-to-video generation framework that aligns conversational audio with motion priors and role-aware Gaussian guidance.
- [ViBe: Ultra-High-Resolution Video Synthesis Born from Pure Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23326): Proposes a pure image adaptation framework that upgrades a video Diffusion Transformer pre-trained at its native scale to synthesize higher-resolution videos.
- [I3DM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23413): Uses implicit 3D-aware memory retrieval and injection to improve consistent video scene generation.
- [Foveated Diffusion: Efficient Spatially Adaptive Image and Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23491): Uses starts with a mask modeling the foveated resolution to allocate tokens non-uniformly, assigning higher token density to foveal regions and lower density to peripheral regions.
- [Accelerating Diffusion-based Video Editing via Heterogeneous Caching: Beyond Full Computing at Sampled Denoising Timestep](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24260): Accelerates diffusion-based video editing with heterogeneous caching, avoiding full computation at every sampled denoising timestep.
- [Unleashing Vision-Language Semantics for Deepfake Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24454): Uses vision-language semantics to detect deepfake videos, adding a generated-media authenticity model that reasons beyond low-level visual artifacts.
- [Anti-I2V: Safeguarding your photos from malicious image-to-video generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24570): Protects photos against malicious image-to-video generation, adding a misuse-defense model for video generation.
- [AnyID: Ultra-Fidelity Universal Identity-Preserving Video Generation from Any Visual References](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25188): Presents AnyID, an ultra-fidelity identity-preservation video generation framework that features two core contributions. First, we introduce a scalable omni-referenced architecture that effectively unifies heterogeneous identity inputs (e.g., faces, portraits, and videos) into a cohesive representation.
- [InstanceAnimator: Multi-Instance Sketch Video Colorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25357): Introduces InstanceAnimator, propose InstanceAnimator, a novel Diffusion Transformer framework for multi-instance sketch video colorization. To address these challenges, introduces three corresponding innovations.
- [MemCam: Memory-Augmented Camera Control for Consistent Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26193): Proposes MemCam, a memory-augmented interactive video generation approach that treats previously generated frames as external memory and leverages them as contextual conditioning to achieve controllable camera viewpoints with high scene consistency.
- [VGGRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26599): Uses a 4D latent reward for world-consistent video generation, aligning video synthesis with persistent scene geometry and dynamics.
- [LightCtrl](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27083): Provides training-free controllable video relighting, adding an inference-time editing method for video diffusion workflows.
- [EFlow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27086): Trains few-step video generators from scratch with efficient solution flow, adding a fast-training route for video generation models.
- [FlashSign](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27915): Provides pose-free guidance for efficient sign-language video generation. Core idea: reduce dependence on explicit pose inputs while preserving controllable sign-motion generation.
- [AutoCut: End-to-end advertisement video editing based on multimodal discretization and controllable generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28366): Parent-routing-needed: this advertisement-video system combines multimodal discretization and controllable generation, but its workflow scope looks closer to application editing than a reusable model row.
- [Not All Frames Are Equal: Complexity-Aware Masked Motion Generation via Motion Spectral Descriptors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29655): Uses motion spectral descriptors to measure frame-wise dynamic complexity and guide masking, attention, and decoding in masked motion generation.
- [OmniRoam: World Wandering via Long-Horizon Panoramic Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.30045): Introduces OmniRoam, propose OmniRoam, a controllable panoramic video generation framework that exploits the rich per-frame scene coverage and inherent long-term spatial and temporal consistency of panoramic representation.
- [MotionGrounder: Grounded Multi-Object Motion Transfer via Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00853): Adds flow-based motion signals and object-caption alignment loss to diffusion transformers for grounded multi-object motion transfer.
- [TRACE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01207): A 3D scene editing method using geometry-aligned video masking, multi-view anchors, and tangible geometry anchoring for temporally stable edits.
- [DynaVid: Learning to Generate Highly Dynamic Videos using Synthetic Motion Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01666): Trains highly dynamic video generators with synthetic motion data, using curated motion signals to increase movement intensity and temporal diversity.
- [Bidirectional Cycle Consistency for Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01700): tests and improves whether video diffusion models can predict past frames through reversible interpolation.
- [Control-DINO: Feature Space Conditioning for Controllable Image-to-Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01761): Conditions pretrained image-to-video diffusion on decoupled DINO-style feature spaces, improving controllability for stylization, relighting, and video-from-3D generation.
- [ActionParty: Multi-Subject Action Binding in Generative Video Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02330): For this purpose, we propose ActionParty, an action controllable multi-subject world model for generative video games. It introduces subject state tokens, i.e. latent variables that persistently capture the state of each subject in the scene.
- [HVG-3D: Bridging Real and Simulation Domains for 3D-Conditional Hand-Object Interaction Video Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03305): Proposes HVG-3D, a unified framework for 3D-aware hand-object interaction (HOI) video synthesis conditioned on explicit 3D representations. Specifically, we develop a diffusion-based architecture augmented with a 3D ControlNet, which encodes geometric and motion cues from 3D inputs to enable explicit 3D reasoning during video synthesis.
- [BiTDiff: Fine-Grained 3D Conducting Motion Generation via BiMamba-Transformer Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04395): Introduces CM-Data for 3D conducting and a BiMamba-Transformer diffusion model for long, fine-grained music-conditioned conducting-motion generation.
- [Inter-Request Caching Reuse for Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04451): Accelerates video diffusion transformer serving by reusing cache across requests, adding a deployment-oriented efficiency method for video generation.
- [HumANDiff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05961): uses articulated noise diffusion to improve motion consistency in human video generation.
- [OmniCamera: A Unified Framework for Multi-task Video Generation with Arbitrary Camera Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06010): Introduces OmniCamera, a unified framework designed to explicitly disentangle and command these two dimensions. This compositional approach enables flexible video generation by allowing arbitrary pairings of camera and content conditions, unlocking unprecedented creative control.
- [DiffHDR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06161): Re-exposes LDR videos with video diffusion models, adding a video-editing and enhancement method to the generation-model section.
- [Accelerating Training of Autoregressive Video Generation Models via Local Optimization with Representation Continuity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07402): Speeds autoregressive video-generator training with local optimization and representation-continuity constraints that stabilize adjacent-frame prediction.
- [Uni-ViGU: Towards Unified Video Generation and Understanding via A Diffusion-Based Video Generator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08121): This imbalance motivates us to invert the conventional paradigm: rather than extending understanding-centric MLLMs to support generation, we propose Uni-ViGU, a framework that unifies video generation and understanding by extending a video generator as the foundation.
- [When Numbers Speak: Aligning Textual Numerals and Visual Instances in Text-to-Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08546): Aligns textual numerals with visual instances in text-to-video diffusion models, targeting count faithfulness over time.
- [Matrix-Game 3.0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08995): Builds a real-time streaming interactive world model with long-horizon memory, relevant to video generation systems that must maintain state across user actions.
- [Generalizing Video DeepFake Detection by Self-generated Audio-Visual Pseudo-Fakes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09110): Trains video deepfake detectors with self-generated audio-visual pseudo-fakes from authentic samples, improving cross-dataset generalization without requiring real deepfake training data.
- [CT-1: Vision-Language-Camera Models Transfer Spatial Reasoning Knowledge to Camera-Controllable Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09201): Proposes a novel Vision-Language-Camera model, termed CT-1 (Camera Transformer 1), a specialized model designed to transfer spatial reasoning knowledge to video generation by accurately estimating camera trajectories.
- [Prompt Relay: Inference-Time Temporal Control for Multi-Event Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10030): Relays prompts across inference timesteps to control when multiple events appear in a generated video, adding temporal scheduling without retraining.
- [Long-Horizon Streaming Video Generation via Hybrid Attention with Decoupled Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10103): Improves long-horizon streaming video generation with hybrid attention and decoupled distillation.
- [Rein3D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10578): A reinforced 3D indoor scene generation method using panoramic video diffusion to refine trajectories as pseudo-ground truth for 3D Gaussian updates.
- [ReplicateAnyScene: Zero-Shot Video-to-3D Composition via Textual-Visual-Spatial Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10789): Proposes ReplicateAnyScene, a framework capable of fully automated and zero-shot transformation of casually captured videos into compositional 3D scenes.
- [Immune2V: Image Immunization Against Dual-Stream Image-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10837): Building on these findings, we propose the Immune2V framework which enforces temporally balanced latent divergence at the encoder level to prevent signal dilution, and aligns intermediate generative representations with a precomputed collapse-inducing trajectory to counteract the text-guidance override.
- [HDR Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11788): Uses latent alignment with logarithmic encoding for HDR video generation, adding dynamic-range control to video synthesis.
- [LottieGPT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11792): Tokenizes vector animations for autoregressive generation, extending video and animation generation beyond raster frames.
- [Ride the Wave](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12219): Allocates sparse-attention precision for smoother video generation, adding a video-specific efficiency and quality-control method.
- [VideoFlexTok](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12887): Introduces flexible-length coarse-to-fine video tokenization, improving token efficiency for video generation models.
- [VibeFlow: Versatile Video Chroma-Lux Editing through Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13425): This paper proposes VibeFlow, a novel self-supervised framework that unleashes the intrinsic physical understanding of pre-trained video generation models.
- [DiT as Real-Time Rerenderer: Streaming Video Stylization with Autoregressive Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13509): Uses an autoregressive diffusion transformer for streaming video stylization, extending video-generation models toward real-time rerendering workflows.
- [Metadata-Guided Diffusion for Bitstream-Corrupted Video Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13906): Recovers corrupted video through metadata-guided diffusion, adding another generative restoration route for degraded video inputs.
- [Controllable Video Object Insertion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14556): uses multiview priors for controllable object insertion in videos, extending video editing with geometry-aware consistency.
- [Seen-to-Scene: Keep the Seen, Generate the Unseen for Video Outpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14648): Performs video outpainting by preserving visible regions while generating unseen scene content with temporal and spatial consistency.
- [Flow of Truth: Proactive Temporal Forensics for Image-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15003): Adds proactive temporal forensics for image-to-video generation, using motion-flow evidence to identify or authenticate generated clips.
- [Latent-Compressed VAE for Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16479): compresses video latents for video diffusion models through a variational autoencoder design.
- [Motif-Video 2B: Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16503): Training strong video generation models usually requires massive datasets, large parameter counts, and substantial compute. In this work, we ask whether strong text-to-video quality is possible at a much smaller budget: fewer than 10M clips and less than 100,000 H200 GPU hours.
- [Modeling Biomechanical Constraint Violations for Language-Agnostic Lip-Sync Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16808): Detects lip-sync deepfakes from biomechanical constraint violations by modeling perioral landmark displacement, velocity, acceleration, and jerk without pixels or audio.
- [DVAR: Adversarial Multi-Agent Debate for Video Authenticity Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16987): Parent-routing-needed: the adversarial multi-agent debate setup is an authenticity-detection harness around generated video, not a core generator architecture.
- [Generalizable Face Forgery Detection via Separable Prompt Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17307): Uses separable prompt learning for generalizable face-forgery detection, separating manipulation cues from domain-specific visual bias.
- [Speculative Decoding for Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17397): Applies speculative decoding to autoregressive video generators, adding an inference-scaling route for longer or faster video synthesis.
- [GS-STVSR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18047): uses 2D Gaussian splatting for ultra-efficient continuous spatio-temporal video super-resolution.
- [AdaCluster: Adaptive Query-Key Clustering for Sparse Attention in Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18348): Proposes adaptive query-key clustering for sparse attention in video generation.
- [TS-Attn: Temporal-wise Separable Attention for Multi-Event Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19473): Uses temporal-wise separable attention to disentangle event timing in multi-event video generation, improving control over ordered actions.
- [CityRAG: Stepping Into a City via Spatially-Grounded Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19741): To this end, we present CityRAG, a video generative model that leverages large corpora of geo-registered data as context to ground generation to the physical scene, while maintaining learned priors for complex motion and appearance changes.
- [DynamicRad](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20470): Uses content-adaptive sparse attention for long-video diffusion, reducing video-synthesis compute while preserving scene-dependent temporal evidence.
- [Sparse Forcing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21221): Trains native sparse attention for real-time autoregressive diffusion video generation, adding an architecture-level efficiency method.
- [KD-CVG: A Knowledge-Driven Approach for Creative Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21362): Injects external knowledge into creative video generation, using structured semantic guidance to steer concepts and scene composition.
- [Rethinking Cross-Domain Evaluation for Face Forgery Detection with Semantic Fine-grained Alignment and Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21478): Combines semantic fine-grained alignment with a mixture-of-experts detector to improve cross-domain face-forgery detection.
- [Reshoot-Anything](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21776): learns self-supervised video reshooting from monocular videos, adding camera-control and viewpoint-transfer capability to video editing.
- [Vista4D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21915): Uses 4D point clouds for video reshooting, adding a geometry-aware video editing and generation method for changing viewpoints or trajectories.
- [FlowAnchor: Stabilizing the Editing Signal for Inversion-Free Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22586): Stabilizes optical-flow-like editing signals for inversion-free video editing, reducing temporal drift without per-video DDIM inversion.
- [FreqFormer: Hierarchical Frequency-Domain Attention with Adaptive Spectral Routing for Long-Sequence Video Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22808): Adds hierarchical frequency-domain attention with adaptive spectral routing to long-sequence video diffusion transformers.
- [Hallo-Live](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23632): A real-time audio-video avatar generation method using asynchronous dual-stream diffusion, future-expanding attention, and preference-guided distillation.
- [YOSE: You Only Select Essential Tokens for Efficient DiT-based Video Object Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27322): Presents YOSE, You Only Select Essential Tokens, an efficient fine-tuning framework. YOSE introduces two key components: Batch Variable-length Indexing (BVI) and Diffusion Process Simulator (DiffSim) Module.
- [UniVidX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00658): Unifies multimodal video generation through diffusion priors. Core idea: combine conditioning signals in one framework so video generation can handle several creation and editing modes.
- [Latent Space Probing for Adult Content Detection in Video Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00874): Attaches lightweight classifiers to CogVideoX denoised latent states for real-time adult-content detection before video decoding.
- [AnimationDiff: A Visual Comparison Tool for Generated 3D Character Animations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01001): Parent-routing-needed: AnimationDiff is a visual comparison tool for generated 3D character animations, so it belongs closer to evaluation tooling than Model.
- [VAnim: Rendering-Aware Sparse State Modeling for Structure-Preserving Vector Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01517): Frames text-to-SVG animation as sparse state updates over persistent vector geometry, preserving structure while generating editable animations.
- [Predictive Latents for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02134): uses predictive latent representations for video generation, strengthening temporally grounded generative modeling.
- [ActDiff-VC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02849): uses conditional controlled diffusion and active sampling for ultra-low-bitrate video compression, fitting generative video reconstruction coverage.
