- [Bridging the Embodiment Gap: Disentangled Cross-Embodiment Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03637): Edits videos across embodiments with disentangled representation, relevant to controllable video generation around embodied motion.
- [Stream-R1: Reliability-Perplexity Aware Reward Distillation for Streaming Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03849): Uses reliability-perplexity aware reward distillation for streaming video generation, targeting stable online generation quality.
- [FaithfulFaces: Pose-Faithful Facial Identity Preservation for Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04702): Preserves facial identity while respecting target poses in text-to-video generation, decoupling face identity control from pose-driven motion.
- [SNN AI-Generated Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05895): uses spiking neural networks to detect AI-generated videos, extending generated-video authenticity models.
- [Relit-LiVE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06658): Relights video by jointly learning environment video.
- [Alice v1: Distillation-Enhanced Video Generation Surpassing Closed-Source Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08115): Uses distillation-enhanced training for a video-generation model, transferring stronger teacher behavior into an open generator.
- [ReST-KV: Robust KV Cache Eviction with Layer-wise Output Reconstruction and Spatial-Temporal Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08840): Introduces ReST-KV, a robust KV eviction method that combines layer-wise output Reconstruction and Spatial-Temporal smoothing to provide a more comprehensive perspective for the KV cache eviction task. By directly modeling how each token's removal affects the model output.
- [EduStory: A Unified Framework for Pedagogically-Consistent Multi-Shot STEM Instructional Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09378): Parent-routing-needed: the contribution is a pedagogically consistent multi-shot STEM instructional-video framework, likely a domain generation pipeline rather than a general model.
- [TIE: Time Interval Encoding for Video Generation over Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10543): Encodes event time intervals as conditioning signals for video generation, giving the model explicit duration and spacing control over events.
- [AllocMV: Optimal Resource Allocation for Music Video Generation via Structured Persistent State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10723): We propose AllocMV, a hierarchical framework formulating music video synthesis as a Multiple-Choice Knapsack Problem (MCKP). AllocMV represents the video's persistent state as a compact, structured object comprising character entities, scene priors, and sharing graphs, produced by a global planner prior to realization.
- [FIS-DiT: Breaking the Few-Step Video Inference Barrier via Training-Free Frame Interleaved Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11869): Introduces training-free frame-interleaved sparsity for few-step video diffusion inference, improving the practical generation regime for fast video models.
- [$h$-control: Training-Free Camera Control via Block-Conditional Gibbs Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11871): Controls pretrained flow-matching video generators by adding block-conditional pseudo-Gibbs refinement on unobserved latent regions, balancing camera-trajectory adherence with visual quality.
- [OmniHumanoid: Streaming Cross-Embodiment Video Generation with Paired-Free Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12038): Supports streaming cross-embodiment video generation with paired-free adaptation.
- [CausalCine: Real-Time Autoregressive Generation for Multi-Shot Video Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12496): To bridge this gap, we introduce CausalCine, an interactive autoregressive framework that transforms multi-shot video generation into an online directing process.
- [CRePE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12938): Adds curved-ray expectation positional encoding for unified-camera-controlled video generation, improving camera-conditioned synthesis beyond pinhole-only assumptions.
- [DiffST](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13182): Applies spatiotemporal-aware diffusion to real-world space-time video super-resolution, adding a restoration-oriented video diffusion method.
- [TeDiO: Temporal Diagonal Optimization for Training-Free Coherent Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14136): We show that these failures leave a clear imprint inside the model: incoherent videos consistently exhibit irregular, fragmented temporal diagonals in their intermediate self-attention maps, whereas stable motion corresponds to smooth, band-diagonal patterns.
- [HASTE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14513): Uses head-wise adaptive sparse attention to accelerate video diffusion without training, adding a capability-specific efficiency method for video generation.
- [SEDiT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14894): removes video subtitles without masks using a one-step diffusion transformer, adding a specialized but reusable video editing route.
- [Test-time Human Motion Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14935): Uses multi-scale coarse-to-fine modeling for test-time human motion control.
- [Causal Forcing++: Scalable Few-Step Autoregressive Diffusion Distillation for Real-Time Interactive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15141): Scales few-step autoregressive diffusion distillation for real-time interactive video generation, relevant where latency enables interaction.
- [RAVEN: Real-time Autoregressive Video Extrapolation with Consistency-model GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15190): We introduce the Real-time Autoregressive Video Extrapolation Network (RAVEN), a training-time test framework that repacks each self rollout into an interleaved sequence of clean historical endpoints and noisy denoising states.
- [Sound Sparks Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15307): Tunes video editing with audio and text conditions, extending controllable video editing beyond visual prompts alone.
- [PanoWorld: Geometry-Consistent Panoramic Video World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15391): We present PanoWorld, a panoramic video world model that generates geometry-consistent 360$$ video from a single image and a caption.
- [VideoRLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15458): Applies verifiable rewards to video diffusion models through SDE-GRPO, decomposed rewards, and early-step focus, moving video generation toward explicit spatial, temporal, and logical constraints.
- [AnyAct](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15497): reconstructs character motion from video for human reenactment, extending human-centric video generation control.
- [SNIS-NGM Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15533): uses structural noise initialization and guidance for tuning-free instruction-based video editing.
- [FashionChameleon: Towards Real-Time and Interactive Human-Garment Video Customization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15824): Targets real-time interactive human-garment video customization.
- [LSTD](https://doi.org/10.1109/TMM.2026.3651052): Models long- and short-term temporal structure for video generation with diffusion.
- [NABLA](https://doi.org/10.1109/ACCESS.2026.3686867): Uses neighborhood-adaptive block-level attention for efficient video generation, adding a structured-attention route for video synthesis.
- [LDT](https://doi.org/10.1109/tmm.2026.3668560): Uses a linear diffusion transformer for efficient scalable video generation, adding an architecture-level efficiency method for synthesis.
- [CamI2V-Epipolar](https://doi.org/10.1109/LSP.2026.3675911): Adds epipolar-constrained sparse attention to camera-controlled image-to-video diffusion, improving geometry-aware viewpoint control in generated videos.

- [Hybrid Multimodal DeepFake Detection](https://doi.org/10.1109/ICSEDIS68157.2026.11517985): uses spatial-temporal multimodal feature learning for deepfake detection.
- [Comfort-Oriented Stereoscopic Video Retargeting](https://doi.org/10.1109/AETCSE69203.2026.11504393): combines feature guidance and depth remapping to improve comfort in stereoscopic video retargeting.

- [StyHarmo: Efficient Style-Specific Video Generation with Music Synchronization](https://doi.org/10.1109/icassp55912.2026.11463734): Generates style-specific videos synchronized with music, adding an audio-conditioned controllability route for video generation.
- [EgoGen: Egocentric Interaction Video Generation with 3D Hand Structure Constraints](https://doi.org/10.1109/icassp55912.2026.11461342): Conditions egocentric interaction video generation on 3D hand-structure constraints, tying hand pose and object contact geometry to first-person video synthesis.
- [Towards Dynamic World Model Generation with Monocular Video](https://doi.org/10.1109/icassp55912.2026.11463651): Uses monocular video as the evidence source for generating dynamic world models, linking video observation to temporally evolving scene representations.
- [LGVF: A Low-Bitrate Generative Video Compression Framework With Spatio-Temporal Diffusion Model](https://doi.org/10.1109/lsp.2026.3670748): Uses a spatio-temporal diffusion decoder for low-bitrate generative video compression, reconstructing perceptual detail from compact transmitted features.
- [Efficient Quantization of Text-to-Video Diffusion Models with OmniQuant-Style PTQ on Consumer GPUs](https://doi.org/10.1109/etai68332.2026.11485459): Applies OmniQuant-style post-training quantization to text-to-video diffusion models so video generators can run on consumer GPUs with reduced memory.
- [Domain-Adaptive Panoramic Video Generation Using Image Diffusers](https://doi.org/10.1109/access.2026.3667172): Adapts image diffusion priors to panoramic video generation, targeting domain transfer and 360-degree temporal consistency rather than ordinary planar synthesis.
- [Trajectory-Based Video Generation Model for Autonomous Driving](https://doi.org/10.1109/iceaai68945.2026.11442436): Conditions driving-scenario video generation on trajectories, making planned ego or object motion the control signal for autonomous-driving video synthesis.
- [Training-Free Controllable Text-Guided Video Editing](https://doi.org/10.1109/tcsvt.2026.3652425): Edits videos from text prompts without finetuning, using inference-time control over the pretrained generator rather than training a new editor.
- [IDFreq: Identity-preserved human video generation via frequency-based decomposition](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.jvcir.2026.104828): Preserves identity in human video generation by decomposing features in the frequency domain, separating identity cues from motion and appearance variation.

- [MotionPrior: Exploring Efficient Learning of Motion Concepts for Few-Shot Video Generation](https://doi.org/10.1109/tip.2026.3672374): Learns reusable motion concepts from few examples, using a motion prior to adapt video generation when only limited motion demonstrations are available.

- [Implicit hierarchical temporal-spatial residual model for long-term video prediction](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.neunet.2026.108732): Models long-term video prediction with implicit hierarchical temporal-spatial residuals, separating coarse temporal dynamics from spatial residual refinement.

- [A hybrid spatial-frequency attention-based algorithm using efficientnet for robust and interpretable deepfake detection](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41598-026-46086-9): Adds spatial-frequency attention on EfficientNet features for interpretable deepfake detection across visual and frequency artifacts.
- [AT-HSTNet: An Efficient Hierarchical Action-Transformer Framework for Deepfake Video Detection](https://doi.org/10.3390/app16073450): Uses a hierarchical action-transformer architecture to detect deepfake videos from action-level temporal patterns and local visual evidence.
- [A Multi-Grained Parallel Spatio-Temporal Learning Architecture for Deepfake Video Detection](https://doi.org/10.1145/3789507): Runs parallel multi-grained spatial and temporal branches for deepfake detection, combining local frame artifacts with longer motion inconsistency cues.
- [Performance Evaluation of NIST-Standardized Post-Quantum and Symmetric Ciphers for Mitigating Deepfakes](https://doi.org/10.3390/cryptography10020015): Parent-routing-needed: this evaluates cryptographic mitigation of deepfake threats, so it is security infrastructure rather than a video-generation model.
- [S2-Edit3DV: Diffusion-Guided Style Meets Structure for Consistent Multi-View 3D Video Generation](https://doi.org/10.1145/3746027.3755796): Combines diffusion-guided style transfer with structural constraints for multi-view 3D video generation, keeping appearance edits consistent across viewpoints.
- [G-FED: G-Buffer Guided Frame Extrapolation in Video Diffusion Models](https://doi.org/10.1145/3721250.3742972): Conditions video diffusion on G-buffers for frame extrapolation, using geometry and rendering attributes to guide future-frame synthesis.
- [ScaMo: Towards Text to Video Storyboard Generation Using Scale and Movement of Shots](https://doi.org/10.1145/3696409.3700279): Generates text-to-video storyboards by modeling shot scale and camera movement, making cinematic composition an explicit control signal.
- [Improving Identity Preservation in Video Generation with Multi-Branch Models](https://doi.org/10.1145/3746027.3761990): Uses multi-branch modeling to preserve identity cues during video generation, separating identity features from motion and scene variation.
- [FreerCustom: Training-Free Multi-Concept Customization for Image and Video Generation](https://doi.org/10.1007/s11263-025-02623-z): Customizes multiple concepts in image and video generation without training by manipulating concept representations at inference time.
- [ColorSurge: Bringing Vibrancy and Efficiency to Automatic Video Colorization via Dual-Branch Fusion](https://doi.org/10.1145/3721238.3730736): Colorizes videos with dual-branch fusion, balancing vibrant color propagation with efficient temporal processing.
- [Detection-Aware Inference for Robust Talking-Head Video Generation](https://doi.org/10.1145/3769748.3773364): Makes talking-head generation detection-aware at inference time, adjusting synthesis to reduce artifacts that downstream detectors or quality checks expose.
- [ChromaFlow: End-to-End Flow Matching for Efficient Video Colorization](https://doi.org/10.1145/3757376.3771391): Uses end-to-end flow matching for video colorization, replacing staged propagation with a direct generative color-flow model.
- [Disentangled Phoneme-Prosody Mapping for Controllable 3D Facial Animation](https://doi.org/10.1145/3721250.3743011): Separates phoneme and prosody mappings for controllable 3D facial animation, letting speech content and expressive timing drive different facial controls.
- [EdgeGaussian: Real-time Free-Viewpoint Video for Mobile VR via Edge-Client Collaborative Neural Rendering](https://doi.org/10.1145/3680207.3765245): Parent-routing-needed: this edge-client collaborative neural-rendering system targets mobile VR free-viewpoint video delivery more than generative video modeling.
- [M2PE-Diff: Music-to-Pose Encoder for Dance Video Generation Leveraging Latent Diffusion Framework](https://doi.org/10.1145/3746027.3754808): Encodes music into pose sequences for latent-diffusion dance video generation, using audio rhythm as the motion-control signal.
- [Learning Evidential Delta Denoising Scores for Video Editing](https://doi.org/10.1145/3746027.3755065): Learns evidential delta denoising scores for video editing, estimating edit-specific denoising changes while preserving source-video evidence.
- [DrivingDiffusion: Layout-Guided Multi-view Driving Scenarios Video Generation with Latent Diffusion Model](https://doi.org/10.1007/978-3-031-73229-4_27): Conditions latent diffusion on driving layouts to synthesize multi-view driving-scenario videos with coordinated road, actor, and camera structure.
- [Real-Time Deepfake Video Detection Using Eye Movement Analysis with a Hybrid Deep Learning Approach](https://doi.org/10.3390/electronics13152947): Detects deepfake video in real time by modeling eye-movement cues with a hybrid deep-learning classifier.
- [Codec Avatar Studio: Paired Human Captures for Complete, Driveable, and Generalizable Avatars](https://doi.org/10.52202/079017-2640): Parent-routing-needed: this paired-capture avatar dataset and studio pipeline is stronger as avatar infrastructure or dataset coverage than as a pure model entry.
- [MoVideo: Motion-Aware Video Generation with Diffusion Model](https://doi.org/10.1007/978-3-031-72784-9_4): Adds motion-aware conditioning to diffusion video generation so synthesized clips follow explicit motion cues instead of relying on prompt-only dynamics.
- [WAVE: Warping DDIM Inversion Features for Zero-Shot Text-to-Video Editing](https://doi.org/10.1007/978-3-031-73116-7_3): Warps DDIM inversion features for zero-shot text-to-video editing, transferring edit signals through time without per-video training.
- [Video-driven musical composition using large language model with memory-augmented state space](https://doi.org/10.1007/s00371-024-03606-w): Uses an LLM with a memory-augmented state space to compose music from video, making this an adjacent video-to-audio generation reference.
- [CP-Diffusion: Conditional Prompt-Based Diffusion Models for Video Generation](https://doi.org/10.1145/3793552): Uses conditional prompts inside diffusion video generation, making prompt structure a controllable signal for synthesized motion and appearance.
- [Enhanced Temporal Representation and Spatial Alignment for High-Fidelity Talking Video Generation](https://doi.org/10.1007/s00371-025-03999-2): Improves talking-video generation with stronger temporal representation and spatial alignment, targeting lip, pose, and identity consistency across frames.
- [LoRA Training for Text-to-Video Models: A Practical Guide to Fine-Tuning SOTA Video Generation](https://doi.org/10.1145/3757371.3763260): Parent-routing-needed: this is a practical LoRA fine-tuning guide for text-to-video models, closer to tutorial or engineering guidance than a new model method.
- [OmniFusion: Exemplar-Based Video Colorization Using OmniMotion and DifFusion Priors](https://doi.org/10.1007/978-981-96-0917-8_5): Combines OmniMotion correspondences with diffusion priors for exemplar-based video colorization, propagating reference colors through temporally tracked regions.
- [An Adaptive LSB-Based Video Steganography Framework with Blockchain-Enabled Verification for Secure Multimedia Communication](https://doi.org/10.48084/etasr.16031): Parent-routing-needed: adaptive LSB steganography with blockchain verification is secure multimedia communication infrastructure, not a video-generation model.
- [Revolutionizing Deepfake Video Detection: A Quantum-Inspired Multimodal Approach](https://doi.org/10.5220/0014240300004052): Detects deepfake video with a quantum-inspired multimodal classifier, combining multiple evidence streams for generated-media authenticity.
- [TrueLens: Video Fake News Detection with Dual Level Evidence Gathering and Consolidation](https://doi.org/10.1145/3774904.3792362): Parent-routing-needed: TrueLens targets video fake-news evidence gathering and consolidation, which is closer to misinformation detection than video-generation modeling.
- [Appearance and Motion Realism in AI Video Generation and Editing](https://doi.org/10.1145/3772363.3799321): Parent-routing-needed: this appears to evaluate appearance and motion realism in generated or edited videos, so it may belong in Bench rather than Model.
- [Spatiotemporal deep learning for real-time video-based deepfake detection using 3DCNN, 3DResNet, TCN, and VAE](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41598-026-49090-1): Benchmarks 3DCNN, 3DResNet, TCN, and VAE-style spatiotemporal deep models for real-time deepfake-video detection.
- [Deepfake detection method based on complementary enhancement of spatial-frequency domain features](https://doi.org/10.1007/s00530-025-02187-5): Enhances complementary spatial and frequency-domain features for deepfake detection, combining visual artifacts with spectral manipulation traces.
- [Audio-Visual Synchronization and Lip Movement Analysis for Real-Time Deepfake Detection](https://doi.org/10.1007/s44196-025-00911-7): Detects deepfakes through audio-visual synchronization and lip-movement analysis, targeting mismatches between speech and mouth motion.
- [VidSTR: Automatic Spatiotemporal Retargeting of Speech-Driven Video Compositions](https://doi.org/10.1145/3706598.3713857): Automatically retargets speech-driven video compositions across space and time, adapting layouts and timing while preserving spoken-video synchronization.
- [Enhanced Motion-aware Latent Diffusion Models for Video Frame Interpolation](https://doi.org/10.1145/3746027.3754776): Adds motion-aware conditioning to latent diffusion for video frame interpolation, improving intermediate-frame synthesis under large motion.
- [Spatially-Precise Video Editing with Reference Imitation: A Region-Aware Cross-Modal Framework](https://doi.org/10.3390/app15084349): Uses a region-aware cross-modal framework to imitate reference edits in spatially precise video editing, binding reference appearance to selected areas.
- [Exploring Sign Language Dataset Augmentation with Generative Artificial Intelligence Videos: A Case Study Using Adobe Firefly-Generated American Sign Language Data](https://doi.org/10.3390/info16090799): Parent-routing-needed: this studies generated-video augmentation for a sign-language dataset with Adobe Firefly, closer to dataset methodology than a reusable model contribution.
- [Adaptive diffusion landmark dynamic rendering for realistic talking face video generation](https://doi.org/10.1007/s00371-025-03907-8): Uses adaptive diffusion landmarks to drive dynamic rendering for realistic talking faces, giving avatar generation explicit landmark-level motion control.
- [AI-driven audio-to-video generation for dynamic content creation via stable diffusion and CNN-augmented transformers](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41598-026-38758-3): Generates audio-to-video content with stable diffusion and CNN-augmented transformers.
- [RAGCol: RAG-Based Automatic Video Colorization Through Text Caption Generation and Knowledge Enrichment](https://doi.org/10.1145/3672608.3707748): A model, training, architecture, or algorithm entry for video generation and editing. Its value is to track capability-relevant model-side progress under the round15 Model-screening rule.
- [AV-DiT: Taming Image Diffusion Transformers for Efficient Joint Audio and Video Generation](https://doi.org/10.1145/3746027.3755713): Introduces a video generation, editing, or world-modeling method; core idea: improve temporal consistency, controllability, or generation efficiency beyond static image synthesis.
- [RAGCol++: Retrieval Augmented Generation Based Automatic Video Colorization Using Semantic Similarity Search and Probabilistic Grounded Knowledge](https://doi.org/10.1145/3770865.3770867): Extends RAGCol with a larger COL-KG, semantic-similarity retrieval, probabilistic grounded knowledge, and colorizer efficiency changes for automatic video colorization.
- [MegActor-Sigma: Unlocking Flexible Mixed-Modal Control in Portrait Animation with Diffusion Transformer](https://doi.org/10.1609/aaai.v39i9.33002): Adds mixed-modal control to portrait animation with a diffusion-transformer architecture.
- [CyberHost: A One-stage Diffusion Framework for Audio-driven Talking Body Generation](): Uses a one-stage audio-driven diffusion framework with Region Attention Modules and human-prior-guided conditions to generate zero-shot talking-body videos without pose or mesh intermediates.
